(iru eṉṉum tuṇai viṉaiccol)
poruḷ		S	Gk	L	Ger	E
irukkiṟēṉ		asmi	esmi	sum	bin	am
irukkiṟōm		’smas	esmes	’sumus	sind	are
irukkiṟāy		asi	eis	es	bist	art
irukkiṟīr(kaḷ)		stha	este	estis	seid	are
iruk-kiṟāṉ-ḷ,	
kiṉṟatu		asti	esti	est	ist	is
iruk-kiṟār(kaḷ)-
kiṉṟaṉa		santi	eisi	sunt	sind	are
itukāṟum kāṭṭap peṟṟavai eṭuttuk kāṭṭuc coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟu, vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr moḻi evvāṟu mēlaiyāriya moḻikaṭku iṉamāyirunta teṉpatait teḷi vāyk kaṇṭu koḷḷalām.  (va.va).
potuvāka, mēlaiyāriya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaṭkuḷ tiyūttāṉi yam muntiya nilaiyaiyum, kirēkkam, cilāvōṉiyam mutaliyavai pintiya nilaiyaiyum, kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, airōppāviṉ vaṭamēṟkōṭiyiliruntu teṉkīḻkkōṭi nōkki āriyamoḻi kaḷ paṭippaṭiyāyt tirintu vantiruppatai aṟiyalām.
cila coṟkaḷ ip potuviyalpiṟku māṟākat tiyūttāṉiyattil mikat tirintuḷḷaṉa. itaṟku avai intiyāviṉiṉṟu mutalil airōppā ceṉṟuḷḷamaiyē karaṇiyam.
e-ṭu: eṇṇup peyarkaḷum cilavuṟuppup peyarkaḷum.
āriya moḻikaḷuḷ mikat tirintatu kīḻaiyāriyam eṉpatu muṉpu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
orucila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ mēlaiyāriya vaḻiyākavum nēraṭi yākavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukuntuḷḷaṉa.  (va.va.)
e-ṭu: rut (rudh) - mēlaivaḻi
rakta (aratta) - nērvaḻi
arattam eṉṉum kurutip peyarē paṇpiyāku peyarāka ataṉ niṟattaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
2. pirākirutac coṟkaḷ
mēlaiyāriyattilum tamiḻilum teṉtiraviṭattilum illāta vētamoḻic coṟkaḷellām, pirākirutac coṟkaḷē.
v-L.: āti, kirāmam.  (va.va.)
mēḻam
mēḻam - mēṣa (i.vē.)
mēḻakam - mēṣaka.  (va.va:245)
moy
uṟaviṉar moy vaikkumpōtu, maṇavīṭṭār tamakku muṉpu ceyta aḷavē ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu karuta vēṇṭuvatillai. tam celva nilaikkēṟpak kūṭṭiyō kuṟaittō ceyyalām: allākkāl atu vaṭṭiyillāk kaṭaṉpōliruntu taṉ ciṟappaiyiḻakkum. maṇa vīṭṭārum uṟaviṉar nilaiyaṟintu perun taṉmaiyāy iruntu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.  (ta.ti.61).
moḻi eṉṟāl eṉṉa?
oru vakuppār allatu nāṭṭār tam karuttaip piṟarkkup pulappaṭuttaṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷum olittokutiyē moḻiyām. av voli colluñ coṟṟoṭarumāyirukkum. collum oreḻuttuc col īreḻuttuc col mutaliyavākap palavakaittu, 
ōr oli vēṟu; ataṉāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷvēṟu. olikkum poruḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu varivaṭiveḻuttiṟkum olivaṭi veḻuttiṟkum uḷḷatē. kāṟṟu iyaṅkuvatiṉālum iru allatu pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu urāyvataṉālum ōcai piṟakkum. av vōcaikku iyalpāyp poruḷillai. av vōcaiyaip poruḷuṇarttum aṭaiyāḷamākak koṇṭatē moḻiyām. kāṟṟiyakkam veḷḷiṭaiyiyaṅku vatum uyiri (pirāṇi)kaḷiṉ vāyvaḻi iyaṅkuvatum eṉa iru vakaittu. ivaṟṟuḷ, piṉṉataṉ payaṉāya olikaḷē perum pālum moḻik karuviyām. olikku iyalpāyp poruḷuṇart tum taṉmai yiruppiṉ, ulakameṅkum eṉṟum oru moḻiyā yum atuvum kallāmalē aṟiyappaṭuvatāyu mirukkum. aṅṅaṉam iṉmaiyaṟika.
moḻinūliṉ tiṟamaṟiyāta cilar, ōcai nilaippāṉatu ākavē ōcaivaṭivāṉa moḻikaḷum nilaippāṉavai eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itu, irumpu nilaippāṉatu; ākavē, irumpu vaṭivāṉa iyantiraṅkaḷum nilaippāṉavai; putitāyuṇṭāyavaiyalla eṉṟu kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatē.
ovvoru nāṭṭārum perumpālum vevvēṟu moḻiyaip pēci varukiṉṟaṉar. oru nāṭṭār maṟṟoru nāṭṭāriṉ moḻi yaik kaṟṟāloḻiyap pēca muṭiyātu. oru nāṭṭāruḷḷum kuḻantaikaḷ periyōr vāyilāyk kēṭṭā loḻiyat tam tāy moḻiyaip pēcamuṭiyātu. kuḻavip paruvattilēyē tam nāṭṭāriṉiṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭu moḻi vaḻaṅkāta allatu tāymoḻi vaḻaṅkāta iṭattil nilaiyāy vāḻntavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyaip pēca muṭiyātu. muḻuc ceviṭar piṟar pēcu vataik kēṭka iyalāmaiyālēyē perumpālum ūmaiyarā kiṉṟaṉar. vilaṅkukaḷaiyum paṟavaikaḷaiyum pōṉṟu, mikac cila olikaḷāl mikac cila karuttukaḷait terivittuk koṇṭa tamakkeṉa moḻiyillāta anākarika makkaḷum cila kāṭukaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru nāṭṭilum makkaḷiṉ nākarika vaḷarccikkēṟpa moḻiyum vaḷarntuḷḷatu.
oruvaṉukkup piṟappilēyē tāymoḻi yaṟivillai; ataip pēcuntiṟaṉ allatu kaṟkuntiṟaṉ maṭṭumuṇṭu. ōrāṇṭuk kuḻantai ammā, appā, pācci, (pāl), cōcci (cōṟu) mutaliya peyarkaḷaiyum, oṉṟaraiyāṇṭuk kuḻantai avaṟṟoṭu vā, pō mutaliya viṉaikaḷaiyum, īrāṇṭuk kuḻantai taṉ karuttiṟkuriya coṟkaḷellāvaṟṟaiyum collak kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu. allatu kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟatu. mūvāṇṭuk kuḻantai naṉṟāyp pēcukiṟatu; piṉpu vīṭṭaiviṭṭu veḷiyēṟip poruḷkaḷaiyum ceytikaḷaiyum aṟiya aṟiyac coṟ ṟokutiyum peruki varukiṟatu; ataṉpiṉ kalviyiṉāl pala coṟkaḷ putitāy aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam oruvaṉ vaḷara vaḷara, aṟivu perukap peruka, taṉ coṟṟokutiyai mikuttuk koṇṭē pōkiṉṟāṉ. oru kuḻantai taṉ peṟṟōriṭat tiṉiṉṟu pēcak kaṟṟukkoṇṭāṟpōla, oru nāṭṭār tam muṉṉōriṭattiṉiṉṟu pēcak kaṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
mēṟkūṟiya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, māntaṉ oru kālattil moḻi yillātiruntāṉ eṉpatum, moḻiyamainta piṉ vaḻivaḻip piṉṉō rellām muṉṉōriṭattiṉiṉṟu moḻiyaik kaṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatum uyttuṇarappaṭum.
ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷellām ōriṭattiṉiṉṟē paravip pōyiṉar eṉpatum, oru tāyvayiṟṟiṉarē eṉpatum ārāycciyāṟ kaṇṭa muṭipukaḷām. māntaṉ mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya iṭattil makkaḷ moḻiyillāmalē cilakālam vāḻntu vantaṉar. moḻiyillāta nilaiyil cila muṟaiyum moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ cila muṟaiyumāka makkaḷ palaticaikkum pala muṟai paravip pōyirukkiṉṟaṉar. moḻiyillātu pirintupōṉavar tām pōṉa iṭaṅkaḷil putitāyt tattamakku ēṟṟavāṟum iyaṉṟavāṟum iyaṉmoḻi kaḷai ākkikkoṇṭaṉar. moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ pirintu pōṉavar tattam cuṟṟuc cārpiṟkēṟpap putuc coṟkaḷai amaittum taṭpaveppa nilaikkēṟpap paḻañ coṟkaḷait tirittuṅ koṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, toṭarpuṭaiya makkaḷellām toṭarpuṭaiya moḻikaḷaip pēci varuvatum, makkaḷ toṭar piṉ perumai ciṟumaikkēṟpa avarkaḷ moḻikaḷiṉ toṭar pum mikkum kuṟaintu miruppatum iyalpām. itaṟku, amerikka nīkirōvaip pōla anākarika moḻiyarāyum kōvāp pōrttukkīciyaraip pōlac ciṟupāṉmaiyarāyu miruntu, ayaṉmoḻikaḷaik kaṭaippiṭippatu vilakkām.
moḻittōṟṟak koḷkaikaḷ
aṟiviyaṟpaṭṭa moḻiyārāycci tōṉṟumuṉ, moḻiyiṉ allatu moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟattaippaṟṟi aṟiñariṭaip palvēṟu tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ iruntuvantaṉa. avaiyāvaṉa:-
1. teyvak koḷkai (Divine Theory)
itu iṟaivaṉē moḻikaḷaippaṭaittāṉ eṉpatu. ituvē mutaṉmutal ellā nāṭukaḷilum koḷḷap peṟṟup piṉpu taḷḷappaṭṭatu.
itai maṟukkum ētukkaḷ:
1. 	moḻiyillāmalum cila vilaṅkāṇṭi makkaḷ uḷḷaṉar.
2. 	cilamoḻikaḷ ciṟiyaṉavākavum anākarikamākavum uḷḷaṉa.
3. 	evarum kaṟkāmal iyalpākat tam tāy moḻiyaip pēca iyalātu.
4. 	makkaḷ muyaṟciyāl ciṟumoḻikaḷum perumoḻikaḷāka vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa.
5.	tiripiṉālum kalappiṉālum pala putumoḻikaḷ tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa.
6.	makkaḷellārum eṉṟum orēmoḻiyaip pēcumāṟu iṟaivaṉ ceytirukkalām.
7.	iṟaivaṉ paṭaittiruntāl moḻikaḷ iṉṟiruppatiṉum mikacciṟappākavum kuṟṟaṅkuṟaiyaṟṟum iruntirukkum.
2. oppantak koḷkai (Contract Theory)
	itu makkaḷ kūṭi iṉṉa oli iṉṉa poruḷuṇarttuka eṉṟu oppantañ ceytu koṇṭatu moḻi eṉpatu. itu urūcō koḷkai.
itu veṇṇey taṭavik kokkup piṭikkuṅ kataipōl taṉ maṟuppuk koḷkaiyātalāl āyviṟkuriyataṉṟām.
3. tūṇṭuṇarccik koḷkai (Instinct Theory)
itu, iyaṟkaiyāka māntaṉiṭaiyeḻunta tūṇṭuṇarcciyoli kaḷāl moḻitōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu. itu kāṇṭilakku koḷkai.
ituvum iṟaivaṉ paṭaippuk koḷkaipōl ēraṇamuṟaikku ovvātatē.
4. kuṟippolik koḷkai (Pooh-Pooh Theory)
itu māntaṉiṉ iṉpa tuṉpak kuṟippolikaḷāl moḻi tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
kuṟippolikaḷ, orucila karuttukkaḷaiyēyaṉṟi ellāk karuttuk kaḷaiyum uṇarttum coṟṟokutiyait tōṟṟu vikkum āṟṟalaṟṟavai yākum.
5. oppolik koḷkai (Bow-wow Theory)
itu vilaṅku paṟavaikaḷālum palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷālum eḻum olikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷaik koṇṭu moḻi tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
oppolikaḷum ellā karuttukkaḷaiyum uṇarttum coṟ kaḷaip piṟappikkum āṟṟalaṟṟavaiyē.
6. maṇiyolik koḷkai (Ding-dong Theory)
itu, maṇiyaṭittavuṭaṉ oliyeḻuvatu pōl karuttut tōṉṟiya vuṭaṉ col veḷippaṭum eṉpatu. itu mākkacu mullar koṇṭu piṉpu viṭṭuviṭṭatu. colliṟkum poruḷiṟkum iyalpāṉa toṭarpuṇṭu eṉṉum vaḻūuk koḷkaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatu itu.
7. ayāvuyirppuk koḷkai (Yo-he-ho Theory)
itu, makkaḷ kūṭi uṭalaivarutti oru vēlaiyaic ceyyum pōtu mūccaṭakki veḷiyiṭum olikaḷāl moḻi tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu. itu nāyrē koḷkai.
ituvum uṇarcciyolik koḷkai yottatē.
8. caikaik koḷkai (Gesture Theory)
itu, karuttaṟivikkum iru vāyilkaḷuḷ caikai muntiyatu eṉṟum, olivaṭivāṉa moḻi ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭateṉṟum, kūṟuvatu.
caikaiyillāmal oliyum oliyillāmaṟ caikaiyum irutiṇai yuyirkaḷiṭattum tōṉṟuvatāl, ivviraṇṭiṟkum oṉṟupaṭṭa uṟavu illaiyeṉpatu teḷivu.
oliyoṭu vaṭiya vāycceykaiyāl moḻi tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatu (Mouth gesture theory) caikaik koḷkaiyiṉ maṟṟoru vakai. itaik koṇṭavar riccārṭu pēkeṟṟu.
9. toṭarpuk koḷkai (Contact Theory)
itu, mutalil piṟaroṭu toṭarpu koḷḷak kuraliṭuvatum, piṉpu potuvākak karaivatum ataṉpiṉ oruvaraic ciṟappāka viḷippatum, ataṉpiṉ oṉṟaic ceyya ēvuvatumāka moḻi tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatu. itu revecu (Revesz) koḷkai.
itu, piṟaroṭu toṭarpu koḷvatil nērum uḷanikaḻccikaḷai varicaip paṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟatēyaṉṟi, moḻi tōṉṟiya vakaiyai viḷakkukiṉṟilatu.
10. pāṭṭolik koḷkai (Musical Theory)
itu, muntiyal māntar viṉai muṭivil tam karuttaip pāṭ ṭoliyāka allatu iṉṉicaiyolit toṭarāka veḷip paṭuttiṉar eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu alakuṭaikkum neṭuñ coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum, avai uyarnta icaiyilum alakilum olittaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟē piṟkālac coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum, kūṟuvatu.
itu cecupperceṉ koḷkai.
vāykku vantapaṭi uḷaṟum icaiyolikaḷiṉiṉṟu, oḻuṅkāṉa peyar viṉai yiṭaic coṟkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ palvēṟu vaṭivu kaḷum tōṉṟiṉaveṉpatu, muṭceṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu mukkaṉikaḷum tōṉṟiṉa eṉpataiyē okkum.
iṉi, uṇarvolikkoḷkai (Emotion Theory) eṉpatum oṉṟuṇṭu.
iṅṅaṉam 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 20-ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai mēlai moḻinūlār moḻit tōṟṟattai āyntuvantatum, iṉṉum uṇmai kāṇa iyalātavarāyiruk kiṉṟaṉar. itaṟku muntiyal iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻai aṭippaṭaiyākak koḷḷātu pintiyal tirimoḻiyākiya camaṟkirutattai allatu āriyattai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatē karaṇiyam.
muḻaittalmoḻi, (Inarticulate Speech), iḻaittalmoḻi (Articulate Speech) eṉa moḻi iruvakaippaṭum ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu uṇarcciyolikaḷ, viḷiyolikaḷ mutaliya eḻuvakai yolikaḷālum, piṉṉatu cuṭṭoli vaḷarcciyālum, āyiṉa.
moḻi tōṉṟiya vakai
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām iyaṉmoḻikaḷ (Primitive Languages) tirimoḻikaḷ (Derivative Languages) eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. oṉṟaiyum cārātu tāṉē iyalpākat tōṉṟiya moḻi iyaṉmoḻi; oru moḻiyiṉiṉṟu tirinta moḻi tirimoḻi.
moḻikaḷellām orēyaḷavāṉa colvaḷamuṭaiyaṉavalla. makkaḷiṉ karuttu allatu aṟivu veḷippāṭṭoliyē col. uṇpatum uṭuppatum tuyilvatumē toḻilākak koṇṭa anākarika malaivāṇariṉ allatu kāṭṭu vāṇariṉ karuttukkaḷ mikamikac cila. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ moḻikaḷiluḷḷa coṟkaḷum mikamikac cila. pala toḻiluḷḷa nākarikam vāynta marutavāṇariṉ allatu nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ mikappala. ataṉāl, avarkaḷiṉ moḻiccoṟkaḷum mikappala. ettoḻilaiyum anākarikaṉ ceyyumuṟaikkum nākarikaṉ ceyyumuṟaikkum vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. ivviyalpuppaṭi, anākarikaṉ tiruttamiṉṟiyum nākarikaṉ tiruttamāyum pēcukiṟāṉ. ākaiyāl, anākarika nākarika makkaḷiṉ moḻic coṟkaḷ ciṉmai paṉmaiyāl maṭṭumaṉṟit tiruntāmaiyum tiruttamumpaṟṟiyum vēṟupaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. tiruttamum karuttuttiruttam coṟṟiruttam eṉa iru pāṟpaṭṭatu. ākavē, oru nāṭṭāriṉ nākarikattai yaṟivataṟku avaratu moḻipōṟ ciṟanta karuvi allatu vāyil piṟitoṉ ṟillai.
oru moḻiyār anākarikarāyiṉ, avar piṟa nākarika makkaḷoṭu kūṭumpōtu, tam anākarika nilaikku allatu collaḷavukkut takkavāṟu, nākarikariṉ coṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tirukkum. iḵtu iyaṉmoḻi tirimoḻi ākiya iruvakai moḻikkum ēṟkum. ēṉeṉiṉ, coṟkuṟaivum coṉṉiṟaivum avviruvakaikkumuṇṭu.
pala moḻikaḷ oṉṟāyk kalappiṉ kalavai moḻiyām. atu irumoḻik kalavai paṉmoḻik kalavai eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. oru moḻiyil piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷirunta māttiraiyāṉē, ataik kalavai yeṉṉamuṭiyātu. uṇmaiyil iṉṟiyamaiyāta kāṟpaṅkuc coṟkaḷaiyāvatu kaṭaṉkoṇṭa moḻiyaiyē kalavai moḻiyeṉṉalām.
iyaṉmoḻi vakaiyil, tiruntāmoḻikkut teṉkaṇṭa (ātirēliya) teṉ āppirikka moḻikaḷaiyum tirunti moḻikkut tamiḻaiyum; tirimoḻi vakaiyil, tiruntā moḻikkut tuṭākōṭṭā mutaliya moḻikaḷaiyum tiruntiya moḻikkut teluṅku karunaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷaiyum; kalavai moḻi vakaiyil, tiruntāmoḻikkuk kōṇṭi patri mutaliya moḻi kaḷaiyum tiruntiya moḻikkut teluṅku āṅkilam ātaliya moḻikaḷaiyum; iru moḻikkalavaikku marāṭṭi teluṅku mutaliya moḻikaḷaiyum paṉmoḻik kalavaikku āṅkilam inti mutaliya moḻikaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟalām.
moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟattai ōr iyaṉmoḻiyāl aṟiyamuṭiyumēyaṉṟit tirimoḻiyāl aṟiyamuṭiyātu. moḻi nūṟperumpulavar mākcumullar cīṉamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum teṉ kaṇṭat tīvukaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟumē moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟattai yuṇarntār.
ulakap perumoḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻi tiripu mutirnta tātaliṉ, ataṉaik koṇṭu moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟattaiyaṟiya virumpuvār, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟavaṉ eṉṟu koḷḷu pavarēyāvar. tamiḻō iyaṉ moḻiyāyum mikat tiruntiyatāyum moḻi vaḷarcciyiṉ pala nilaikaḷaik kāṭṭak kūṭiyatāyu miruttaliṉ, moḻiyiṉ iyalpāṉa tōṟṟattai yaṟitaṟkut talaiciṟanta vāyilākum.
moḻi mutalāvatu taṉittaṉi olikaḷāka allatu acaikaḷākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu. oru karuttu ippōtu pala coṟkaḷuḷḷa vākkiya māka amaintālum, atu mutalāvatu vākkiyamākat tōṉṟa villai. ēṉeṉiṉ, vākkiya amaippukku vēṇṭum coṟkaḷel lām appōtu illai. maṉitaṉukkuk karuttup perukaperuka olikaḷum allatu coṟkaḷum perukik koṇṭē vantaṉa. meḷḷa meḷḷac ciṟitu ciṟitāyp perukik koṇṭu vanta avvolik kūṭṭamē moḻiyākum.
oruvaṉiṉ uḷḷattileḻum karuttu vēṟu, ataṉaip piṟarkkup pulappaṭuttum oli vēṟu. oru karuttaip pulappaṭutta oru vākkiya voliyē vēṇṭum eṉṉum yāppuṟavillai. naṉṟāyp pēccuk kaṟṟa piḷḷai kūṭa ammā! eṉakkuc cōṟu vēṇṭum eṉṉum nāṟcoṟṟoṭarai ammā! cōṟu eṉṟu iru collil aṭakkiviṭukiṟatu. periyōr pēccilum, ‘nī aṅkē pōṉāyā?’ eṉṉum viṉāviṟkup ‘pōṉēṉ’ allatu ‘ām’ eṉṉum viṭaiyum ‘avar eṉṟaikku varuvār?  eṉṉum viṉāviṟku nāḷaikku eṉṉum viṭaiyum ovvoru collāyē niṉṟu ovvoru karuttait terivikkiṉṟaṉa. oru cila coṟkaḷaiyē kaṟṟa kuḻantai, eṉakkup pāl vēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ karuttai, pā pāci pālu pāl eṉṉuñ col vaṭivaṅ kaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉālēyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu. ataṟkumēṟ colla ataṉiṭam coṟkaḷillai. innilaiyai ayaṉmoḻiyai araikuṟaiyāyk kaṟṟavariṭamum kāṇalām. āṅkilam naṉṟā yaṟiyāta oruvaṉ ōr āṅkilak kaṭaikkāraṉiṭam ceṉṟāl, ‘eṉakku oru katti vēṇṭum’ eṉpatai “mankaina” eṉṟum, ‘itaṉ vilai eṉṉa?  eṉpatai “Price?” eṉṟum orē collāl kuṟikka lāmaṉṟō? ivaṉ taṉ moḻikkup periyavaṉāyiṉum ayaṉ moḻikkuk kuḻantaiyāyirukkiṉṟāṉ. kuḻantaiyum nōyāḷi yum perumpālum ovvoru collālēyē tam karuttai yaṟivikkiṉṟaṉar. periyōr tāymoḻiyiṟ pēcum pēccil orucolviṭai tokai vākkiyaṅkaḷākak karutap paṭalām. āṉāl, ayaṉmoḻi naṉṟāyaṟiyātāṉ ammoḻiyiṟ pēci ṉāl, tāymoḻiyil tōṉṟum coṟkaḷ avvayaṉ moḻi yil tōṉṟāmaiyum, oru kuḻantai pēciṉ tāy moḻiyilum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟāmaiyum uṇarka.
moḻi nirampāta muntukāla (Primitive) māntaṉ, karuttu veḷiyīṭṭil kuḻantaipōṉṟavaṉ. avaṉ ā ī vā pō kī mē mutaliya cila taṉi yacaikaḷālum cila caikaikaḷālumē, taṉ karuttaip pulappaṭuttiṉāṉ. ivviyalpai iṉṟum cila malaivāṇariṭamum teṉkaṇṭattīvāriṭamum kāṇalām. innilaiyai oruvāṟu vilaṅku nilaikku oppiṭalām.
ōr iyaṉmoḻi 1. acainilai 2. coṉṉilai eṉa iru nilai kaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. acainilaiyāvatu peyar viṉai iṭai eṉa ilakkaṇac coṟṟaṉmaippaṭāmal taṉittaṉi yacaikaḷ ovvoru poruḷaiyuṇarttum nilai; coṉṉilaiyāvatu ilakkaṇac coṟṟaṉmaippaṭṭa nilai. atum  1. acaiccol (kaṇ, ā) 2. puṇarccol (kaṇṇavaṉ) 3. pakucol (kaṇṇaṉ)  4. toṭarccol eṉa nāṉku nilaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. avaṟṟuḷ toṭarccol 1. tokānilaittoṭar (taṇṇīr, ceṅkāl nārai) 2. tokai nilaittoṭar (yāy, cāttantai) eṉa iru vakaiyāṉatu.
moḻi vaḷaravaḷara eḻuttukkalaiyum vaḷarntuvarum. ulakil mutalāvatu paṭa veḻuttum (Hieroglyph) piṉpu karutteḻuttum (Ideograph) tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉpiṉ acai yeḻuttum (Syllabic Character) oliyeḻuttum (Phonetic Character) muṟaiyē tōṉṟiṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉavaiyiraṇṭum karuttōṭum piṉṉavai yiraṇ ṭum moḻiyōṭum toṭar puṟṟavai. peruvaḷarcci yaṭainta oru nākarika makkaḷ moḻiyiltāṉ piṉṉīreḻuttuk kaḷaik kāṇalām.
oru moḻiyai allatu pēccaip pala vākkiyaṅkaḷākavum oru vākkiyattaip pala coṟkaḷākavum, oru collaip pala eḻuttukkaḷākavum pakuttukkoḷvatu, oru moḻi peru vaḷarcciyaṭaintapiṉ ataṉ ilakkaṇattai yeṭuttukkūṟum pōtu nikaḻvatākum. moḻittōṟṟam vēṟu: ilakkaṇat tōṟṟam vēṟu. moḻi acaiyolikaḷāyt tōṉṟiṉatē yaṉṟi eḻuttolikaḷāyt tōṉṟavillai. eḻuttē ilakkaṇa vakaiyil oru moḻiyiṉ alaku (Unit); vākkiyam alakaṉṟu. ēṉeṉiṉ, vākkiyam oru kuṟitta aḷavuḷḷataṉṟu. mūvakai vākkiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa kalappu (Complex) vākkiyam varampiṟan tōṭuvatu. maṉumuṟaikaṇṭa vācakattil pala pakkaṅkaḷ varukiṉṟa atikāraṅkaḷ cila ovvoru vākkiyamāka amain tuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam oru puttakam muḻumaiyum orē vākkiyamākkalām. puṇar (Compound)  vākkiyamum varampiṟantatē. iṉi, taṉi (Simple) vākkiyattai eṭuttukkoṇṭālum atuvum pala karuttuk kaḷait taḻuvakkūṭiyatākiṉṟatu. ikkarantai māṇavaṉ iṉiya tamiḻ naṭai eḷitāy eḻutuvāṉ eṉṉum vākkiyattil 1. karantai māṇavaṉ 2. iṉiya tamiḻ 3. eḷitāy eḻutuvāṉ eṉak kuṟaintatu mūṉṟu karuttukkaḷiruttal kāṇka. pala karuttuk kaḷaik kūṟum kalappu vākkiyaṅkaḷaiyum puṇarvākkiyaṅkaḷai yum poruḷ keṭāmal taṉi vākkiyaṅkaḷāka māṟṟavu muṭiyu meṉpatai, āṅkila vilakkaṇaṅkaḷil kaṇṭu teḷika. iṉi, mūvakai vākkiyamuṅ kalanta kalavai (Mixed) vākkiyam eṉpatum oṉṟuṇṭeṉavaṟika.
moḻinūṟpaṭi nōkkiṉ, ovvoru pakāc collum ov voru karuttaiyum ovvoru paku collum paṟpala karuttuk kaḷaiyum kuṟippaṉavākum. ā vā kaṇ eṉṉum pakāc coṟkaḷ ovvoru karuttaiyē kuṟittaṉa. ākkam varuvāṉ kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟa pakucoṟkaḷ paṟpala karuttukkaḷaik kuṟittaṉa. piṉṉa vaṟṟuḷ, varuvāṉ eṉpatu mūṉṟu karuttukkaḷaiyum ēṉaiya ivvirukaruttukkaḷaiyum kuṟittaṉa. varutaṟṟoḻilaik kuṟikkum vā eṉṉum pakutiyum etirkālattai yuṇarttum v eṉṉum iṭainilaiyum, āṇpālaiyuṇarttum aṉ eṉṉum īṟum cērntē varuvāṉ eṉṉum col uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ākavē, pakuti vikuti iṭainilai eṉṉum colluṟuppukkaḷuṅ kūṭa ovvoru poruḷaiyuṇarttuvaṉavāyiṉa. iṉi, pakāc colluṅkūṭap pala poruḷkaḷai yuṇartta iṭamuṇṭu. kaṇ (kaḷ >f©) eṉṉum col karumaiyeṉṉum kuṇamum atai yuṭaiya kaṇ eṉṉum uṟuppum ākiya iruporuḷkaḷai yuṇart tal kāṇka. karumai eṉṉum kuṇattaimaṭṭum karutuvatu poruḷum, atu karumai yuṭaiyatu eṉakkarutuvatu karuttumākum. Mfnt., ovvoru poruḷum karuttākavum māṟakkūṭiyatu eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum.
neyyē toṉṉaikkātāram eṉpatu pōlat tiripiṟ ciṟanta vaṭamoḻiyai iyaṉmoḻiyākak koḷvārē, iṅṅaṉam vākkiyattai moḻiyalakākak koṇṭu iṭarppaṭuvar. mēlum, vaṭamoḻiyai ulaka mutaṉ mūlamoḻiyākak koṇṭu valippavar moḻi nūṉ māṇavarātaṟkuṅ kūṭat takutiyaṟṟa varāvar.
ā, ī, ū eṉṉum olikaḷai moḻi mūla volikaḷākak kūṟuvatu iṉṟu malai yēṟiviṭṭateṉiṉ, atu kuṉṟiṉ mēliṭṭa viḷakkup pōla viḷaṅkutaṟku malaiyēṟi viṭṭateṉka.
moḻinūl
1. moḻi
moḻiyāvatu makkaḷ tam karuttai oruvarukkoruvar veḷiyiṭu taṟkuk karuviyākum olittokuti.
2. moḻivakai
ulakamoḻikaḷ mottam 860 eṉṟu palpi (Balbi) eṉpavar tamatu tiṇaippaṭattil (Atlas) kuṟikkiṉṟār. mākmullar (Max Muller) avai toḷḷāyirattiṟkuk kuṟaiyā eṉkiṟār. (Lectures on the Science of Language, Vol. I, p. 27). ēṟattāḻa āyiram eṉiṉum tavaṟākātu.
moḻikaḷaiyellām, palappala muṟaiyil, palappala vakaiyāka vakukkalām. av vakaikaḷāvaṉa:
1. acainilai (Monosyllabic or Isolating), puṇarnilai (Compounding), pakucoṉṉilai (Inflectional or Polysyllabic), tokunilai (Synthetic), paltokunilai (Polysynthetic) allatu koḷuvunilai (Agglutinative), pirinilai (Analytical) eṉṟu aṟuvakaiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟai. avaṟṟuḷ,
acainilaiyāvatu, coṟkaḷellām ōracaiyāka niṟpatu.
kāṭṭu: vaḷ, cel, vā, pō, tamiḻ, moḻi,
cīṉamoḻi acainilaikkuc ciṟantatākum.
puṇarnilaiyāvatu, iru taṉiccoṟkaḷ puṇarntu niṟpatu.
kā: tamiḻmoḻi, marumakaṉ.
turēṉiya moḻikaḷiṟ cīṉamoḻintavai (piṉṉiyam (Finnish), haṅkēriyam (Hungarian), turukkiyam (Turkish), tamiḻ mutaliyaṉa) puṉar nilaikkuc ciṟantavaiyāyc collappaṭum.
pakucoṉṉilaiyāvatu, puṇarnilaic coṟkaḷil oṉṟu kuṟukit taṉittaṉmaiyiḻantu, colluṟuppāy niṟpatu.
kā: marumāṉ, villavaṉ (vil + avaṉ), villāṉ, pakucoṉṉi laikku āriyamoḻikaḷ ciṟantaṉavākak kūṟappaṭum.
tokunilaiyāvatu, puṇarnilaiccoṟkaḷiṉ iṭaiyil, eḻuttuk kaḷum acaikaḷum tokkuniṟpatu.
kā: ātaṉ + tantai = āntai: peru + makaṉ (perumāṉ) - pirāṉ, allatu pemmāṉ.
paltokunilaiyāvatu, paṉmoḻit toṭariṉiṭaiyē pala acaikaḷ allatu coṟkaḷ tokku niṟpatu.
kā: nacceḷḷaiyār (nal + ceḷḷai + avar), cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar. amerikkamoḻikaḷ paltokunilaikkuc ciṟantaṉa vākak kūṟappaṭum.
mekcika (Mexican) moḻiyil ‘ achichillacachocan’ eṉṉum paltokunilait toṭar, nīr civantiruppatāl makkaḷ aḻum iṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatām . (yaṭava-nīr, uhiuhṭaivaiu -civanta,  tlactl - māntaṉ, chorea -mG) ( Historical outlines of English Accidence, p. 2.)
pirinilaiyāvatu, pakucoṉṉilaic coṟkaḷ īṟiḻantu niṟpatu.
kā: vantēṉ - vantu (malaiyāḷam); brekan (O.E.) - break (Infinitive).
2. mūlamoḻi (Parent Language), kiḷaimoḻi (Daughter Language), uṭaṉmoḻi (Sister Language) eṉa mūvakaiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟai.
tirāviṭak kuṭumpattil, tamiḻ mūlamoḻi; teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam (karunaṭam), malaiyāḷam mutaliyavai tamiḻukkuk kiḷai moḻi yum, tammuḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uṭaṉmoḻiyumākum. nīlamalai yiluḷḷa paṭakam (paṭakarmoḻi) kaṉṉaṭattiṉ kiḷaimoḻi.
3. iyaṉmoḻi (Primitive Language), tirimoḻi (Derivative Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟai.
ivaṟṟukkut tamiḻaiyum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaiyum muṟaiyē kāṭṭākak koḷka.
4. iyaṟkaimoḻi (Natural Language), ceyaṟkai moḻi (Artificial Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟai.
tamiḻ ōr iyaṟkaimoḻi. uvilkkiṉ kaṇkāṇiyār (Bishop Wilkins) eḻutiya kaṟpaṉai moḻi (‘ A Real Character and a Philosophical Language’) ceyaṟkai moḻikkuk kāṭṭākum (L.S.L. Vol.II, p.p. 50-63) kāṅkira talaivarkaḷ urutu viṟkum intikkum iṭaittaramāka, intutāṉi eṉa oṉṟai amaippatākac colvatu araic ceyaṟkaik kalavai moḻiyākum.
5. taṉimoḻi (Independent Language), cārmoḻi (Dependent Language),  kalavai moḻi (Composite or Mixed Language)vd mūvakaiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟai.
ivai mūṉṟukkum, muṟaiyē, tamiḻaiyum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷaiyum, āṅkilattaiyum kāṭṭākak kūṟalām.
6. mutumoḻi (Ancient Language), putumoḻi (New Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟai.
ivaṟṟukkut tamiḻum intiyum kāṭṭākum.
7. ilakkiyamoḻi (Classical Language), vaṟumoḻi (Poor Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟai.
ivaṟṟukkut tamiḻaiyum kuṭakaiyum kāṭṭākak kūṟalām.
8. cemmoḻi allatu tiruntiyamoḻi (Cultivated Language),  puṉmoḻi allatu tiruntāmoḻi (Uncultivated Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟai.
tirāviṭak kuṭumpattil, tamiḻ, teluṅku mutaliyavai cem moḻikaḷ; tuṭā, kōṭṭā mutaliyavai puṉmoḻikaḷ.
9. alaimoḻi (Nomad Language), nilaimoḻi (State Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟai.
alaintu tiriyum makkaḷ pēcuvatu alaimoḻi; ōriṭattil nilaitta makkaḷ pēcuvatu nilaimoḻi.
10. tāymoḻi (Mother Tongue), ayaṉmoḻi (Foreign Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu oru muṟai.
11. vaḻaṅkumoḻi (Living Language), vaḻaṅkāmoḻi (Dead Language) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vakuppatu orumuṟai.
ivaṟṟukku, muṟaiyē, tamiḻaiyum vaṭamoḻiyaiyum kāṭṭākak koḷka.
oru perumoḻiyil pala vaḻakkukaḷ (Dialect) uṇṭu. avaṟṟai moḻivaḻakkeṉalām. avai, iṭavaḻakku (Local Dialect), ticai vaḻakku (Provincial Dialect), kulavaḻakku (Class Dialect) tiṇaivaḻakku (Regional Dialect) eṉa nālvakaippaṭum.
orumoḻi nīṭittu vaḻaṅkumāyiṉ, muṟkāla (Old) vaḻakku, iṭaikkāla (Middle) vaḻakku, taṟkāla (Modern) vaḻakku eṉa mun nilaikaḷaiyaṭain tirukkum.
orumoḻi uyarntatum tāḻntatumāṉa iruvaḻakkukaḷai yuṭaiyatā yiruppiṉ, avai muṟaiyē uyar (High), tāḻ (Low) eṉṉum aṭaikaḷ peṟṟu, ammoḻip peyarāṟ kuṟikkap paṭum (kā: uyarjermaṉ - High German, tāḻ jermaṉ - Low German) tamiḻil ivai centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
oru moḻiyil, eḻuttil vaḻaṅkum vaḻakku nūlvaḻakku (Literary Dialect) eṉṟum, pēccil vaḻaṅkum vaḻakku ulaka vaḻakku (Colloquial Dialect) eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
3. moḻikkulam
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām, vērccoṟkaḷiṉ uṟavum ilakkaṇa voṟṟumaiyum paṟṟi, turēṉiyam (Turanian) āriyam (Aryan), cēmiyam (Semitic) eṉa  mukkulaṅkaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
ovvoru kulamum pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷaik koṇṭatu. ovvoru kuṭumpamum cila allatu pala moḻikaḷaik koṇṭatu.
tamiḻ turēṉiyak kulattil(tirāviṭak kuṭumpam oru ciṟu kuḻuvā yiruntālum mukkulattiṉiṉṟum vēṟākat taṉittuk kūṟappaṭaṟ kuriyatu tirāviṭak kuṭumpattaic cērntatu. turēṉiyattiṟkuc cittiyam (Scythian) eṉṟum āriyat tiṟku intō- airōppiyam (Indo - European), allatu intō- jermāṉiyam (Indo- Germanic) eṉṟum piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu.
moḻi nūl neṟimuṟaikaḷ
1. moḻi nūl oru taṉikkalai.
palar moḻinūl oru taṉikkalai yeṉpatai iṉṉum aṟintilar. paṇṭitarkaḷ potuvāyt taṅkaḷiṭam yārēṉum oru collukku mūlaṅkēṭṭāl, ataṟku ētāvatu collā tiruppiṉ iḻiveṉṟeṇṇi, poruntap puḷukal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi ētēṉumoṉṟaic colliviṭu kiṉṟaṉar. pāṇṭit tiyam vēṟu, moḻinūṟ pulamai vēṟu eṉpatai avar aṟintilar.
kālañceṉṟa oru perun tamiḻppaṇṭitar, tiruccirāp paḷḷiyil orumuṟai vaṭai eṉṉum tiṉpaṇṭap peyar, vaṭu eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉṟum, ap paṇṭattiṉ naṭuvil tuḷaiyiruppatu ataṟkoru vaṭu (kuṟṟam)  veṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. vaṭai eṉpatu vaḷ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟanta teṉpataiyum, vaṭṭamāṉatu eṉṉum poruḷuṭaiya teṉ pataiyum, uḻuntu māviṟkuḷ eṇṇey eḷitāyc cellum paṭi tuḷaiyiṭuva teṉpataiyum, tuḷaiyiṉāl cuvaiyāvatu maṇamāvatu keṭā teṉpataiyum avar aṟintilar.
ottuppārkka: peḷ + tai = peṭṭai - peṭai: vaḷ+tai = vaṭṭai - vaṭai. peḷ = virumpu, vaḷ = vaḷai.
2. moḻinūl eṉṟum oppiyal taṉmaiyuḷḷatu
aṭṭaiyāṭal eṉpataṉ poruḷ uṇartaṟkut teluṅ kaṟivum, kaṉṉaṭa aṟivum vēṇṭiyatā yirukkiṉṟatu. aṭṭa allatu aṭṭe = muṇṭam (te.ka.) (vēṅkaṭarājulu reṭṭiyār kaṭṭurai (tamiḻppoḻil).
ampu (vaḷaiyal), ampi ( paṭaku, kāḷāṉ), āmpi (kāḷāṉ) eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ vērkkaruttai lattīṉiṉuḷḷa ampi eṉṉuñ collum, kirēkkiluḷḷa ambhi eṉṉuñ collum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
amphi = round; amphitheatre eṉṉum tokaic collai nōkkuka.
3. moḻinūl piṟakalaic cārpuḷḷatu
moḻinūl, kaṇitattaippōlap piṟakalai cārāta kalaiyaṉṟu. moḻinūliṉ ovvoru kūṟṟum, avvak kalaikkup poruntiya tāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṅkilattiluḷḷa teak eṉṉuñ col, tēkku eṉṉum tamiḻccollē yeṉpataṟkut tiṇainūl (Geography) cāṉṟākum. intiyāvil, vintiya malaikku vaṭakkē tēkku vaḷarvatillai eṉṟu, rākosiṉ (Ragozin) tamatu vētakāla intiyā (Vedic Inida) eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
eḻuttolikaḷai olippatil vayiṟu, nuraiyīral, mūccukkuḻāy, toṇṭai, nā, aṇṇam, pal, utaṭu, mūkku ākiya palavuṟup pukaḷ toṭarpuṟu vatāl, uṭalnūlum (Physiology);
uḷḷakkaruttukaḷiṉ veḷippāṭē moḻiyātaliṉ, uḷanūlum (Psychology);
moḻikaḷaip pēcum makkaḷiṉ iṭamāṟṟattaiyum nākarika vaḷarcciyai yum aṟintirukka vēṇṭiyatāl, carittiramum (History);
taṭpaveppa nilaikkum avvan nāṭṭup poruḷkaṭkum takkapaṭi, oliyum collum māṟupaṭuvatāl, tiṇainūlum (Geography);
nākarika makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷ, mataviyalaip peritun taḻuviyirup patāl, matanūl (Theology), paḻamai nūl (Mythology), paṭṭāṅku nūl (Philosophy) mutaliya kalaikaḷum; iṉṉum, curuṅkakkūṟiṉ pala kalaikaḷum moḻinūṟ payiṟcikkut terintiruttal vēṇṭum.
4. moḻikaḷellām ōraḷavil toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa.
cila coṟkaḷum, avaṟṟaiyāḷum neṟimuṟaikaḷum, pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kā: tamiḻ - maṉ, E - man, A.S. - mann, Skt. -  manu. tamiḻ - tā, L. - do, Skt. - da.; tamiḻ - nāvāy, L. - navis, Skt. - nau.
taṉikkuṟilaiyaṭutta mey uyirvariṉ iraṭṭuvatu pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyirukkiṉṟatu.
kā: tamiḻ - maṇ + ulakam = maṇṇulakam, veḷ + āṭu = veḷḷāṭu.
āṅkilam - thin + er = thinner, sit + ing = sitting.
5. 	moḻikaḷukkuḷ iṉa aḷaviṟkut takkapaṭi oppumaiyirukkum.
6.	moḻikaḷai ārāyumpōtu, muṉṉai moḻiyai muṉvaittum piṉṉai moḻiyaip piṉvaittum ārāyavēṇṭum.
cilar tamiḻukku mikap piṟpaṭṭaṉavum, tamiḻiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṉavum, mikat tirintuḷḷaṉa vum, āriyak kalappu mikkuḷḷaṉavumāṉa malaiyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷukkup piṟkālattil vaṭamoḻiyaip piṉpaṟṟi eḻutiyuḷḷa kēraḷapāṇiṉīyam, karṉāṭaka saptamaṇi tarppaṇam pōṉṟa nūlkaḷait tuṇaikkoṇṭu tamiḻiyalpai ārāyvatu, narivālaik koṇṭu kaṭalāḻam pārppatum, tantaiyai makaṉ peṟṟatākak koḷvatum pōṉṟatē. an nūlkaḷaik kaṟpatāl uṇṭākum payaṉ, malaikalli eli piṭittal pōla mikac ciṟiyatākum; mēlum, uṇmai kāṇātapaṭi mayakkattaiyum ūṭṭum. moḻi nūl ārāyccikku vēṇṭiya maṉavirivaiyaṭaivataṟku, āṅkilattil māri (Morris), āṅka (Angus), kīṟṟu (Skeat), vīṟṟu (Sweet), uviṭṉi (Whitney) mutaliyōr eḻutiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaip paṭittal vēṇṭum. nuṇṇaṟiñarum perum pulavarumāṉa vēṅkaṭarājulu reṭṭiyār vaṭamoḻiyaiyum piṟkālattilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum piṉpaṟṟiya tāl tām  eḻutiyuḷḷa ilakkaṇak kaṭṭuraikaḷ, tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ mūviṭappeyar eṉṉum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ, cila cōrvupaṭṭuḷḷār. avaiyāvaṉa:
1. 	tamiḻil ukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ mūṉṟēyeṉpatu.
 	tolkāppiyattil, moḻimarapil,
  	ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē
  	uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa  (41,42)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatu, muṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟaiyēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiya lukara vīṟṟaiyaṉṟu.
kuṟṟiyalukaram īreḻuttiṟkuk kuṟaiyāta colliṉ īṟṟil valliṉa meyyūrntaṉṟit taṉittuvarutal illai eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. maṟuku, cerukku eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟ kaḷiṉiṭaiyilum, atu, poṟu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟilum uḷḷa ukarattai (ikkāla viyalpu nōkki)k kuṟṟiya lukaramēyeṉak koḷḷa iṭamiruppiṉum, u,tu eṉat taṉittum, ural, mukam eṉac coṉ mutalilum varum ukarattaik kuṟṟiya lukaramākak koḷḷa eḷḷaḷavum iṭamillai. ukaram, kuṟṟu karam, kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṉum coṟkaḷil varum ukaraṅkaḷum muṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷē, ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉa vaṟṟil kuṟu, kuṟṟiyal eṉṉum aṭaimoḻikaḷāl, kuṟukiya ukaram poruḷaḷavil kuṟikkappaṭu kiṉṟatē yaṉṟi oliyaḷavilaṉṟu. ākavē uccakāram uppakāram eṉpavai muṟṟiyalukarattaik kuṟikkumēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiyalukarattaik kuṟiyā eṉpatu mikat tēṟṟam.
mēṟkūṟiya iru nūṟpākkaḷāṉum, muṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ mūṉṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. avai ucu, mucu, tapu eṉpaṉa eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ivai cilavātaliṉ iṅṅaṉam vitantu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
cukku, kuccu, paṭṭu, pattu, kaṟpu, maṟṟu eṉpaṉapōṉṟa kuṟṟiya lukaraccoṟkaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉavātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟkut tokai kūṟiṟṟilar tolkāppiyar.
pu,tu eṉṉum īṟukaḷaikkoṇṭa eṇṇiṟanta toḻiṟ peyar kaḷum, tu,cu eṉṉum īṟukaḷaik koṇṭa eṇṇiṟanta piṟa viṉaic coṟkaḷum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷātal kāṇka. itaṉālēyē,
uyirau eñciya iṟuti yākum  (36)
eṉṟu iruvakai yukaramumaṭaṅkap potuppaṭak kūṟi, piṉpu uccakāram........ urittē eṉṟum, uppakāram .... moḻipa.... eṉṟum muṟṟukarattai vitantō tiṉār tolkāppiyar.
2. tolkāppiyar kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟākak koṇṭār eṉpatu.
tolkāppiyap puṇariyalil,
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷi yoṭu nilaiyal  (2)
kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa  (3)
eṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟṟōṭu māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiyatāl, kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟākak koṇṭaṉar tolkāppiyar eṉṟār reṭṭiyār.
kuṟṟiyalukarattaip puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyalākak kūṟiyatāl kuṟṟiyalukarattaip puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭum vaḻakkam paṇṭaik kālatti liruntateṉpatu peṟappaṭumēyaṉṟi, āciriyar kuṟṟiya lukarattai meyyīṟākak koṇṭār eṉpatu peṟappaṭātu.
paṇṭait tamiḻar nuṇṇiya cevippulaṉ vāyntiruntamaiyiṉ, kuṟṟukaram muṟṟukarattiṉum kuṟukiṉateṉpataik kāṭṭum poruṭṭup puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutiṉar.
kuṟṟukarattiṉmēl araimati (allatu kuṟumpiṟai) vaitteḻu tuvatu iṉṟum teṉmalaiyāḷa nāṭṭil uḷḷatu eṉṟu reṭṭiyārē kūṟukiṉṟār. malaiyāḷa nāṭṭil, iṟṟait tamiḻnāṭṭilillāta pala paṇṭait tamiḻ vaḻakkaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
tolkāppiyar kālattil, kuṟṟiyalukarattaip puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻu tiṉārāyiṉ, av vaḻakkam ēṉ piṟkālattilillai eṉṟu kēṭkalām. ataṟku viṭai kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
tolkāppiyar kālattil, ekara okara uyirkaḷaiyum avaiyēṟiṉa meykaḷaiyum, mēṟpuḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkamāyirunta teṉpatu,
nūṉmarapiluḷḷa,
meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal  (15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē  (16)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāṉ aṟiyalām. ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷum, avaiyēṟiṉa meykaḷum, puḷḷi peṟāmaiyālēyē, tattam kuṟilkaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iv vaḻakkam aṟṟuppōyviṭṭa teṉpatu, tamiḻil ekara okara uyirēṟiṉa meykaḷum, ēkāra ōkāra uyirēṟiṉa meykaḷum, vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yeḻutappaṭṭa maiyāl, piṉṉavaṟṟai vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭaṟku vīramā muṉivar kompukaḷai mēṟcuḻitteḻutiṉatākak koṭuntamiḻ ilak kaṇattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉamē kuṟṟukarap puḷḷiyum vaḻakku vīḻntateṉak koḷka.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi  (eḻuttu. 43)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāviṉ uraiyil, āṇṭu eṉṟa mikaiyāṉē......... kuṟṟukarak kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukku mēṟpuḷḷi koṭup pārum uḷar eṉakkoḷka eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, av vaḻakkuc cila nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭku muṉtāṉ vīḻntateṉa aṟiyalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē kalvikkaḷam pārppaṉar kaippaṭṭuviṭṭatiṉālum, tamiḻ vaṟaḷa vaṭamoḻiyai vaḷampaṭuttuvatē avar kaṭaippiṭiyātalālum, tamiḻar uyar nilaik kalviyai iḻantu viṭṭatiṉālum tamiḻ eḻuttumuṟai kavaṉippāriṉṟic cīrkēṭaṭaintatu.
puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatiṉālēyē, kuṟṟukarattai meyyīṟākak koḷḷiṉ, moḻimarapil,
meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal  (15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē  (16)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyiruppatāl, ekara okara vīṟukaḷaiyum meyyīṟākak koḷḷavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam koḷḷalākāmai veḷippaṭai.
iṉi, kaṇ+yātu = kaṇṇiyātu eṉpatupōṉṟē, cukku + yātu = cukkiyātu eṉṟu puṇarvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟākak koḷḷap paṭumeṉṟār reṭṭiyār. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, katavu + yātu = kataviyātu eṉṟu muṟṟukaramum puṇarvatāl, atuvum meyyīṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭu, ukaravuyirē tamiḻukkillai yeṉṟākum. ākaiyāl, avar kūṟṟu uṇmaiyaṉmai yaṟika.
vaṭamoḻiyil, purik, virāṭ, cat eṉṟu valliṉa meyyīṟu vantiruppatu paṟṟit tamiḻilum avvāṟē varumeṉṟum, kuṟṟiya lukaramellām meyyīṟēyeṉṟum koḷvatu tamiḻ muṟaikku nērmāṟākum. kuṟṟiyalukaramellām meyyīṟāyiṉ, eṉakku, pāyccu, kaṭṭu (kaṇ+tu), tāṭṭu (tāḷ + tu), vāḻttu, piṭippu, koṭpu, tirumpu, pāṟṟu (pāl+tu), aṟṟu (aṉ+tu) eṉpavaṟ ṟiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷ, muṟaiyē, eṉakk, pāycc, kaṭṭ, tāṭṭ, vāḻtt, piṭipp, koṭp, tirump, pāṟṟ, aṟṟ eṉpaṉa vātalvēṇṭum, aṅṅaṉamākāmai aṟika.
tolkāppiyar kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟākak koṇṭi ruppiṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram..... eḻuttō raṉṉa eṉṟu, atai ōr taṉiyeḻuttākak kūṟiyirār.
kuṟṟiyalukaramāvatu, iṭattiṉālum cārpiṉālum kuṟukiyum ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭum olikkum iyalpāṉa ukaramē. kuṟṟiyalukaram varumiṭattil muṟṟukaramirukka muṭiyātu. valliṉa meykaḷ, melliṉa meyyum iṭaiyiṉa meyyum pōlaṉṟic cikkeṉak kāṟṟait taṭuttalāṉ (checks of breath), nīrōṭṭam kallil muṭṭi vēkantaṇikiṟatupōla, ōcaiyāṉatu taṭaippaṭṭu, īṟṟiluḷḷa ukaram kuṉṟi yolikkiṉṟateṉak koḷka.
3. paṇṭait tamiḻccoṟkaḷ valliṉa meyyilum iṟṟaṉa eṉpatu.
ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum
appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti  (45)
eṉṟu, collīṟṟu meykaḷaip patiṉoṉṟēyeṉṟu tēṟṟē kāraṅ koṭuttuk kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar. ātalāl, valliṉa mey collīṟāmeṉak koḷḷa eḷḷaḷavum iṭamillai.
uriñ, porun mutaliya coṟkaḷum, maṇ, mīṉ mutaliya coṟ kaḷum ukarampeṟṟu vaḻaṅkuvatai, valliṉa meyyīṟukaḷ ukaram peṟṟut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvatākat tām koṇṭa koḷkaikkuc cāṉṟāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār reṭṭiyār.
uriñu, porunu, maṇṇu, mīṉu eṉṟu mutalil ukaravīṟā yirunta coṟkaḷ, piṟkālattil ukaravīṟu keṭṭu uriñ, porun, maṇ, mīṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉavēyaṉṟi, mutalil meyyīṟāyirunta coṟkaḷ piṟkālattil ukaravīṟupeṟṟu vaḻaṅkiṉavalla.
tamiḻar ulakil mikap paḻaimaiyāṉa maṉpataiyarātaliṉ, moḻi yolimuṟaiyil kuḻantaiyarpōlvar. mutalil, tamiḻiluḷḷa ellāc coṟkaḷum uyirīṟākavē olittaṉa. piṉpu, pulavarāṟ paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟapōtu, melliṉa iṭaiyiṉa meykaḷi ṉiṉṟu maṭṭum uyirīṟu nīkkappaṭṭatu. ivvīriṉa meykaḷum valliṉa meyyilum olittaṟ keḷitātal kāṇka.
iṉṟum, cila melliṉa iṭaiyiṉa meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ, pakāc coṉṉilaiyil aṅṅaṉam meyyīṟāka niṟkumēyaṉṟip pakucoṉṉilai yil nillā.
kā: paṇ- paṇṇukiṟāṉ, aḷ - aḷḷukiṟāṉ.
ivai paṇkiṟāṉ, aḷkiṟāṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkāmai kāṇka. itaṉāl, uyirīṟṟunilai muntiyatēyaṉṟi, meyyīṟṟu nilai muntiyataṉṟu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum.
āriya moḻikaḷ tamiḻukkup pintiṉavaiyātalāṉum, tiripu vaḷarcci mikkavai yātalāṉum, avaṟṟil valliṉa meyyum īṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
piṉṉiyattilum (Finnish), vaṭaturēṉiya moḻikaḷuḷ yūraliya (Uralic) vakuppu muḻuvatilum, coṉmutalil iṇai mey kaḷaiyum, collīṟṟil oru meyyaiyum cērppatillai. kiḷā (glas) eṉṉum jermaṉiyaccol piṉṉiyattil lāci eṉṟeḻutappaṭukiṟatu. mak (smak), ṟṟār (stor), ṟṟirāṉṟu (Stand) eṉṉum cuvīṭiyac (Swedish) coṟkaḷ muṟaiyē, makku, cūri, ranta eṉṟeḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu (L.S.L. Vol. II, P. 207) mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṟār.
tirāviṭam turēṉiyak kiḷaiyākak koḷḷappaṭuvataiyum, hāpiṟṟal, pāṅk, pvī mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ muṟaiyē, āpattiri, pāṅki, pīcu eṉṟu uyirīṟākat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvataiyum nōkkuka.
4. valliṉamey iraṭṭittal illai eṉpatu.
valliṉa mey iraṭṭittal eṉpatē yillaiyeṉṟum, āṭṭu, pōkku mutaliya piṟaviṉaikaḷ ellām, 
āṭu + tu = āṭ + tu = āṭṭu,
pōku + tu = pōk + tu = pōkku,
eṉṟa muṟaiyilēyē amaivaṉaveṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār reṭṭiyār.
taṉviṉaip pakutiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttil aḻuttam (Stress) viḻuva tāl atu piṟaviṉaiyākak kūṭumeṉpatu.
uppa kāra moṉṟeṉa moḻipa
iruvayi ṉilaiyum poruṭṭā kummē  (tol.moḻi. 43)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. āṭu, pōku mutaliya taṉ viṉaikaḷiṉ īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ mēl, aḻuttam viḻuvatālēyē, avai iraṭṭikkiṉṟaṉa. acaiyaḻuttattāṟ poruḷ vēṟupaṭu vatu, moḻinūlil oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭa uṇmai. cīṉamoḻi yil in neṟimuṟai mikutiyum payilkiṉṟatu.
acaiyaḻuttam mutalāvatu eṭuppacaiyāka iruntatēyaṉṟi, valiyiraṭṭalāka illai. nāḷaṭaivil aḻuttam mikamika vali yiraṭṭalākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. appōtu atai iraṭṭit teḻuti ṉar. ataṟkumuṉpē tapu eṉṉum viṉai vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉati ṉāl atu paṇṭai muṟaippaṭi eṭuppacaiyāl piṟaviṉaiyāva tākavē kuṟikkappaṭṭuvantatu, tolkāppiyarum muṉṉūl kaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi aṅṅaṉamē kuṟittār. itai oṉṟeṉa moḻipa eṉṟu, vaḻinūṉ muṟaiyil āciriyar kūṟuvatāl aṟiyalām.
kāṭu, āṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷ, avaṟṟoṭu poruṭ porutta muṟṟa piṟa coṟkaḷuṭaṉ puṇarumpōtu iraṭṭittal, avvap puṇarmoḻikaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷukkuḷḷa neruṅkiya allatu oṉṟiya toṭarpaik kāṭṭuvatākum.
kā: kāṭṭuvilaṅku, āṟṟunīr.
naṭṭāṟu, kuṟṟukaram eṉpaṉavum im muṟaipaṟṟiyaṉavē. itai, iru poruḷkaḷai iṇaippatāl uṇṭākum iṟukkattiṟ koppiṭalām.
puku, cuṭu mutaliya viṉaikaḷ pukku, cuṭṭu eṉa iraṭṭittu iṟanta kālam kāṭṭiṉatum, acaiyaḻuttattiṉālēyē, pukku, cuṭṭu eṉpaṉa paṇṭaik kālattil muṟṟākavum eccamākavum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. piṉpu, pāl kāṭṭum īṟu cērntu pukkāṉ (pukku + āṉ), cuṭṭāṉ (cuṭṭu + āṉ) eṉṟu muṟṟukkut taṉi vaṭivam ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, av vīṟu peṟāta paṇṭai vaṭivam eccamāka maṭṭum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
pōkku, āṭṭu mutaliya toḻiṟpeyarkaḷum acaiyaḻuttat tāl īṟu iraṭṭittavaiyē.
ciṟṟaṭi, cīṟaṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkum iru vaṭivukaḷuḷ, cīṟaṭi eṉpatē ciṟantateṉṟum, ciṟṟaṭi eṉpatu ciṟupāṉmai vaḻakkēyeṉṟum kūṟiṉār reṭṭiyār.
paccilai, pācaṟai; cevvāmpal, cētā; mutiyōr, mūturai eṉap paṇpup peyarkaḷ ciṟupāṉmai nīḷātum nīṇṭum iru vakaiyāy varutalāṉum, perumpāṉmai naṭṭāṟu, puttuyir, kuṟṟukaram eṉa nīḷātu valittē varutalāṉum, avar kūṟṟiṟ ciṟanta toṉṟumillaiyeṉa viṭukka.
5. aravu eṉṟoru toḻiṟpeyarīṟu illaiyeṉpatu.
aravu eṉṟu oru toḻiṟpeyarīṟē yillaiyeṉṟu kūṟi, kūṭṭaravu, tēṟṟaravu eṉṉun toḻiṟpeyarkaḷai, muṟaiyē,
kūṭu = kūṭ + taravu = kūṭṭaravu
tēṟu = tēṟ + taravu = tēṟṟaravu
eṉṟu pirittuk kāṭṭiṉār reṭṭiyār.
oru poyyai maṟaikka oṉpatu poy vēṇṭum eṉṟāṟ pōla, kuṟṟukarattai meyyīṟākakkoṇṭa oru vaḻuvai maṟaikka, iṅṅaṉam oṉpatu vaḻukkaḷ nērntirukkiṉṟaṉa.
aram eṉṟoru toḻiṟpeyarīṟu iruppatu, viḷamparam eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarā laṟiyappaṭum.
aram eṉṉum īṟē aravu eṉṟu tiriyum.
aram - (aramu) - aravu.
o.nō. uram - uravu, taṭam - taṭavu, puṟam - puṟavu.
6.	mūviṭappeyar viṉāppeyarkaḷiṉ amaippu, uyireḻuttu 	
	varicaimuṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉpatu.
a,i,u,e eṉṉum nāṉkuyirkaḷum, muṟaiyē cēymaic cuṭṭiṟkum, aṇmaic cuṭṭiṟkum, iṭaimaic cuṭṭiṟkum, viṉāviṟkum āki, paṭarkkaip peyar muṉṉilaippeyar iṭaimaippeyar viṉāppeyar eṉṉum nāṟpāṟ peyarkaḷum amaintatu, uyireḻuttu kaḷiṉ varicai muṟaiyai ētuvākak koṇṭateṉṟu reṭṭiyār kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēṟkūṟiya nāṟpāṟ peyar kaḷum arivarit tōṟṟattiṟku (eḻuttiṟku varivaṭivam tōṉṟu taṟku) muntiṉaveṉpatē.
7. 	moḻinūṟ payiṟcikku moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum akara mutaliyum 
	(akarātiyum) pōtiya karuvikaḷām.
oru colliṉ poruḷaiyum mūlattaiyum aṟitaṟku, ilak kaṇamum colliyalum (Etymology) terintirukka vēṇṭum. illāviṭṭāl, pōliyoppumaiyāl vaḻumuṭipiṟ kiṭamākum.
kaṇṭi, pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, condemn, Parlia-ment eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkaḷuṭaṉ oliyilum poruḷilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, colliyalai nōkkiṉ, avai ciṟitum toṭarpaṟṟavai eṉpatu teḷivākum.
Condemn - L. condemno, from con intensive, damno to damn.
Parliament - Lit. ‘a parleying or speaking,’ Fr. parlement - parler. to speak, ment, a Lat. suffix.
pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉpatu pār, āḷum, maṉṟu eṉṟa muccoṟkaḷālāṉatāyum, Parliament eṉpatu orē collāyumiruttal kāṇka.
coṟkaḷ perumpāṉ moḻikaḷiṟ kālantōṟum vēṟupaṭu kiṉṟamaiyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ mūlavaṭivai yaṟintē poruḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum.
mūlam vēr (root), taṇṭu (stem) eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. vaḷaiyam eṉṉum collukku vaḷai eṉpatu taṇṭu; vaḷ eṉpatu vēr. (vērum mutal vēr (primitive root), vaḻivēr (Secondary root) cārpu vēr (Tertiary root) eṉa mūvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, mutalvēr maṭṭum mīṇṭum mūvakaippaṭum. ivaiyellām piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum).taṇṭai aṭiyeṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.
mūlamum pakutiyum tammuḷoru vēṟupāṭuṭaiyaṉa. purantāṉ eṉṉuñ collukku, pura eṉpatu mūlamum pakutiyumākum. āṉāl, purantaraṉ eṉṉuñ collukku, pura eṉpatu mūlamum, purantā eṉpatu pakutiyumākum.
moḻikaḷukkiṭaiyiluḷḷa toṭarpaik kāṇpataṟku, mūviṭap peyar, viṉāppeyar, mukkiyamāṉa muṟaippeyar, eṇp peyar, vīṭṭiluḷḷa taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷiṉ peyar, ciṉaippeyar, vā,pō eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa mukkiya viṉaikaḷ eṉpaṉavē pōtu māyiṉum, pala coṟkaḷum terintiruppiṉ colliyalpum coṟporuḷum viḷakkam peṟutaliṉ, akarātiyaiyē tuṇaikkoḷvatu mikac ciṟantatākum.
8. oruvar tam iṭattiliruntukoṇṭē moḻinūl payilalām.
oruvar oru moḻiyai aṟiya, atu vaḻaṅkum iṭattiṟkuc celvatu iṉṟiyamaiyāta taṉṟu; tam iṭattiliruntukoṇṭē takkōr eḻutiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum akarātikaḷaiyun tuṇai koṇṭu ayaṉmoḻikaḷaik kaṟkalām.
9. oru moḻiyaṟivu piṟamoḻi yaṟivukkut tuṇaiyākum.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷiṉ mūlam piṟa moḻiyilēyē aṟiyak kiṭappatālum, oru moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇikaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkāta, ammoḻikkuriya cila ilakkaṇavuṇmaikaḷ, piṟa moḻiyilakkaṇikaḷāl kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭiruppata ṉālum, ulakat tāymoḻiyiṉ iyalpaiyum vaḷarcciyaiyum aṟiyat takka pala neṟimuṟaikaḷ pala moḻikaḷilum paravik kiṭattalā lum, moḻikaḷai aṟiya aṟiya oruvariṉ aṟivu perukuvatā lum, ovvoru moḻiyiṉ aṟivukkum piṟamoḻi yaṟivu ētēṉum oruvakaiyil utavuvatēyākum.
10. tāymoḻi muṉṉum ayaṉmoḻi piṉṉum kaṟkappaṭa vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉ kallāmalē taṉ tāymoḻiyil pēcakkūṭumāyiṉum, ataṉ marapaiyum ilakkaṇattaiyum kaṟṟē yaṟiya vēṇṭiya tirukkiṉṟatu, tāymoḻiyiyalpai yaṟiyu muṉ ayaṉmoḻiyai virivāyk kaṟpavar, ayaṉmoḻi vaḻakkait tāymoḻiyiṟ pukuttiviṭuvar.
tamiḻaik kallātu āṅkilattaiyē payiṉṟa cila tamiḻar, perum pāluṅ kiṟitavar, naṉṟāy viḷaiyāṭiṉārkaḷ eṉpatai viḷaiyāṭṭil naṉṟāyc ceytārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
11. mukkula moḻikaḷum orē mūlattiṉa. (L.S.L. Vol.I. pp. 35,375, 387)
oru marattil, orē aṭiyiṉiṉṟu pala kavaikaḷum, akkavai kaḷiṉiṉṟupala kiḷaikaḷum, ak kiḷaikaḷiṉiṉṟu pala kompu kaḷum, ak kompukaḷiṉiṉṟu pala kappukaḷum, ak kappu kaḷiṉiṉṟu pala vaḷārkaḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē, ulaka mūla moḻiyiṉiṉṟu, pala kulanilaikaḷum, ak kulanilaikaḷi ṉiṉṟu, pala kuṭumpa nilaikaḷum, ak kuṭumpa nilaikaḷiṉiṉṟu pala moḻikaḷum, am moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu pala kiḷai moḻikaḷ allatu moḻi vaḻakkukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ mukkulat tāykaḷum orē mūlattiṉa eṉṟu, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē, mākkacu mullar tēṟṟamāyk kūṟiviṭṭār.
paṇṭaikkālattil, āriyar varumuṉ, intiyā muḻutum, pala moḻivaḻakkukaḷāka vēṟupaṭṭa orē moḻi (tamiḻ) vaḻaṅkiṉa tākac cila tolkāppiyak kuṟippukaḷālum moḻinūlārāyc ciyālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. āriyar vanta piṟakuṅkūṭa, uṇmaiyāṉa moḻikaḷāka oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭavai, vaṭakkil cama kirutamum teṟkil tamiḻumāka iraṇṭē yeṉpatu, vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyeṉṉum avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷālēyē aṟiyap paṭum. iṉṟum, teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷai yellām tamiḻukkuḷḷum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷaiyellām camakirutattikuḷḷum aṭakkiviṭalām. tirāviṭak kuṭumpam muḻutum tamiḻukkuḷ aṭaṅkuvatu pōlap piṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷum ovvoru moḻik kuḷ aṭaṅkiviṭum. inta muṟaiyaiyē toṭarntu piṉpaṟṟi ṉāl, iṟutiyil, turēṉiyam āriyam cēmiyam eṉṉum mukkula nilaikaḷē eñci niṟkum. avaṟṟaiyum oṉṟāyaṭakkuvataṟkuriya neṟimuṟaikaḷ illāmaṟ pōkavillai.
(nōvāvukku muntiya kālattil, ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ) ulaka muḻuvatum orē moḻiyum orē pēccumāy iruntatu eṉṟu, kiṟitava maṟaiyil ātiyākamam 11ām atikāram mutaṟ kiḷaviyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉṟum, oru ulaka mūlamoḻiyait tēṟṟañ ceyvataṟkāṉa pala cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaiyāvaṉa:
1.		makkaḷ tokai varavara mikutal.
2. 		ellāmoḻikaḷiṉ arivariyum a āvil toṭaṅkal.
3. 	ammā, appā eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, mūviṭappeyar viṉāp peyar vērkaḷum, pala coṟkaḷum pala moḻi kaṭkum potu vāyiruttal.
4. 		eṇkaḷ pattuppattākavē pala nāṭukaḷil eṇṇappaṭal.
5.	neruppu, cūriyaṉ, nākam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaḻipāṭu paṇṭai yulakeṅku miruntamai.
6. 	neṭṭiṭaiyiṭṭa nāṭṭārkkum karuttorumippu.
kā:	āṅkilam	tamiḻ
	tonic(from tone)	olittal (taḻaittal)
	to catch fire	tīppaṟṟu
	to fall in love	vīḻ(tāmvīḻvār....cyF”)
	blacksmith	karumāṉ
mutalāvatu kaṇavaṉum maṉaiyiyumāka illaṟan toṭaṅkum iruvar, piṉpu periya kuṭumpamākap perukuvataiyum, nāḷaṭai vil ciṟṟūr nakarum nakar mānakarumāvataiyum, kuṭi matippaṟik kaikaḷil varavara makkaṭṭokai mikuntu varuvataiyum kāṇ pārkku, makkaḷ ellām orutāy vayiṟṟiṉar eṉpatu pulaṉā kāmaṟ pōkātu. makkaḷ orutāy vayiṟṟiṉarāyiṉ, avarkaḷ pēcum palvēṟu moḻikaḷum orē mūlattiṉavātal vēṇṭum.
makkaḷtokai vaḷarccikkuk kāṭṭāka, mākkacu mullariṉ moḻinūṟ kaṭṭuraikaḷiṉ mutaṉ maṭalattil, 62ām pakka aṭikkuṟippiṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷa oru kuṟippai iṅkuk kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
“oru naṅkai patiṉāṟu piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟṟu, avaruṭ patiṉoruvar maṇantapiṉ iṟantāḷ. avaḷ iṟakkumpōtu, 144 pērappiḷḷaikaḷum, 228 pērap pērappiḷḷaikaḷum, 900 pērap pērap pērappiḷḷaikaḷum avaḷukkiruntaṉar.”
muṇṭāmoḻiyiṉarkkuḷ irupatirupatāy eṇṇum vaḻakkam iruppatu, kaiyaiyuṅ kālaiyum oṉṟupōṟ karutattakka anākarika nilaiyil, avar muṉṉōr iruntataik kāṭṭum.
makkaḷ perumpālum taṭpaveppa nilai vēṟupāṭṭālēyē, pala vakuppārāka vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟum nākarika vāḻkkaiyum kalviyumiruppiṉ pala vakuppārum oru nilaiyarā kavum otta karuttiṉarākavum vāḻak kūṭum.
12. ōr iyaṟkaimoḻi tōṉṟum vaḻikaḷ palvakaiya.
ulakat tāymoḻi tōṉṟiya vaḻiyaippaṟṟip palarum pala vakaiyāṉa karuttuk koṇṭaṉar. cilar oppolik koḷkai (Onomatopoeic theory)yum, cilar uṇarcci yolikkoḷkai (Interjectional theory) yum, cilar cuṭṭaṭik koḷkai (Demonstrative theory) yum eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar. ōr iyaṟkaimoḻi oru vaḻiyilaṉṟip pala vaḻikaḷil tōṉṟumeṉpatu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
moḻi iyaṟkaiyāy tōṉṟiyateṉṉum iyaṟkaik koḷkai (Natural theory) yum, iṭukuṟikaḷāyt tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟum iṭukuṟik koḷkai  (Arbitrary theory) yum, palar kūṭi iṉṉa poruṭ kiṉṉa colleṉṟu muṭivu ceytatākak kūṟum kūṭṭu muṭivuk koḷkai (Conventional theory) yum, kaṭavuḷē amaittār eṉṉum teyvikak koḷkai (Divine theory) yum ikkālattiṟ kēlā.

13. iyaṟkaimoḻiyil iṭukuṟiccol irātu.
cila moḻikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷ mūlan teriyātapaṭi mikat tirintu pōyirukkiṉṟaṉa. poruḷaṟiya vārāmaiyāl avaṟṟai iṭukuṟi yeṉṟaṉar ilakkaṇikaḷ. vaṭanūlār irākaṅkaḷukkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum peyariṭumpōtu, avaṟṟait tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirittuk kāṭṭavēṇṭi iṭukuṟippeyarkaḷai yiṭṭaṉar. ittakaiya nilaimaikaḷ iyaṟkai moḻikku nērā.
ovvoru collum poruḷ kuṟikkavēṇṭumeṉpatu, māntaṉ pēcciṉ mukkiyamāṉa ilakkaṇamākum. oru moḻiyil olittiripu tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉ, iv vilakkaṇattai am moḻi yiḻatuviṭukiṟatu eṉṟu mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṟār. (L.S.L. Vol. I. p. 47).
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (tol. 639)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
14.	ulaka mutaṉmoḻi tōṉṟiya muṟaiyai ōr iyaṟkai moḻiyiltāṉ  kāṇamuṭiyum.
tiripu vaḷarcciyil mika mutirnta camakirutattai, iṉṟum, cilar tirāviṭat tāyākavum, ataṟkumēlum, ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yākavum mayaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. tiripiṟkuc ciṟanta āriyakkulat tiluṅkūṭa atu mikap pintiyateṉṟu, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē taḷḷiviṭṭaṉar moḻinūl vallār. aṅṅaṉamirukka, mika iyaṟkaiyāṉa tamiḻukku atu eṅṅaṉam mūlamāka muṭiyum?.
ulaka mutaṉmoḻikku neruṅkiya moḻi, eḷiya olikaḷai yuṭaiyatāy, acainilai mutaliya aivakai nilaikaḷaik koṇṭa tāy, iṭukuṟiyillatāy, taṉimoḻiyā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
15. carittirattāl ēṉai moḻikkuḷ aṭakkappaṭāta muṉṉai moḻikkē 
	mūlamoḻi āyvu ēṟkum.
malaiyāḷam, inti, āṅkilam pōṉṟa moḻikaḷuḷ, mūla moḻikkuriya iyalpukaḷ iraṇṭoṉṟu tōṉṟiṉum avai koḷḷappaṭā. ēṉeṉil am moḻikaḷ ovvoṉṟum ēṉaiya moḻikkuḷ aṭaṅkiviṭutal carittirattāl aṟiyappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
16. moḻi māntaṉ mik¥ng. (L.S.L. Vol. I, p. 31)
moḻi eṉṟu potuppaṭak kūṟuvatellām ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyaiyē, eppikkūra (Epicurus), ariṟṟāṭṭil (Aristotle) mutaliya kirēkka meypporuṇūlār, moḻi aimpūtampōla iyaṟkaiyāṉateṉṟu koṇṭār. intiya muṉṉōrukkum ak karuttē yiruntatu.
oli eṉṟumuḷḷatēṉum, ataik karuttaṟivikkum karuviyākak koṇṭatu māntaṉ vēlaiyē. itaṟkuc cāṉṟāka vilaṅku paṟavai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuk kaṭavuḷ peyariṭṭatākak kūṟāmal, ātām peyariṭṭatākak kiṟitava maṟaiyiṟ kūṟiyiruppataik kāṭṭiṉar mākkacu mullar.
tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu ulakattil oru moḻiyumillai.
17. moḻi makkaḷ kūṭṭuṟavā lāyatu.
moḻi oruvaṉāl amaintataṉṟu. oruvaṉ taṉittu vāḻvāṉā yiṉ avaṉukku moḻiyē vēṇṭiyatillai. makkaḷ oṉṟāyk kūṭi uṟavāṭumpōtē taṅkaḷ karuttaip piṟarkkut terivikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. ataṉāl moḻi piṟakkiṟatu.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷil, cila toḻilpaṟṟiyavai; cila tiṇaipaṟṟiyavai; cila kulampaṟṟiyavai; cila vaḻipāṭu paṟṟiyavai. iṅṅaṉam palavakaiyil pala vakuppārāl tōṟṟappeṟṟa coṟkaḷellām oṉṟucērntatē moḻiyākum.
ikkālattil uvilkkiṉ pōṉṟa oruvar, iṭukuṟikaḷaik koṇṭu oru putu moḻiyamaikka muṭiyumāyiṉum, iyaṟkai yāyp poruḷ kuṟitteḻunta ulaka mutaṉmoḻi oruvarāl taṉippaṭa iyaṟṟappaṭṭatākātu.
18. moḻi acaikaḷākavum coṟkaḷākavum tōṉṟiṟṟu.
moḻiyiṉ kaṭaicic ciṟṟuṟuppu eḻuttākak kūṟuvatu, eḻuttum ilakkaṇamum ēṟpaṭṭa piṟkālattatākum.
tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭum nālvakaic coṟkaḷuḷ, ilakkaṇavakaic coṟkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟattakkavai peyar viṉai iṭai eṉṉum mutal mūṉṟumē. iṟutiyiluḷḷa uriccol ilakkaṇavakaic collaṉṟeṉpatu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
mūvakaic coṟkaḷuḷ muntit tōṉṟiyatu iṭai; ataṉ piṉṉatu viṉai; ataṉ piṉṉatu peyar.
kā:
kū(eṉṟu kattiṉāṉ)	-	iṭaiccol
kūvi			-	viṉaiccol
kūval			- 	peyarccol
tamiḻukku ilakkaṇam ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, immūvakaic coṟ kaḷum tarukkanūṉ muṟaippaṭi, kīḻiṟakkap perumai muṟaiyil māṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
moḻittōṟṟam mutalāvatu coṟṟoṭar amaippait taḻuviyateṉṟu cāy kūṟuvatu, moḻinūṉ muṟaikku māṟāṉatu.
19. moḻi orē camayattil uṇṭāṉataṉṟu.
makkaṭku varavarak karuttu vaḷarkiṉṟatu. ataṉāl moḻiyum vaḷarkiṉṟatu. mutal māntaṉ kuḻantaipōlvaṉ. avaṉukku ūṇuṭai mutaliya cila poruḷkaḷiruntāl pōtum. nākarikam vaḷara vaḷarattāṉ karuttukaḷ palkum. appōtu coṟkaḷum palkum.
māntaṉ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiyataṟkum, makkaṭkuḷ kūṭṭuṟavuṇ ṭāṉataṟkum iṭaiyil, neṭuṅkālam ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭu mātaliṉ, akkālamellām vaḷarcci yiliruntutāṉ piṉṉar moḻi uruvākiyirukkum.
20. moḻikkalai ōr ākkak kalaiyākum.
kalaikaḷ iyaṟkalai (Physical Science), ākkakkalai (Historical Science) eṉa iruvakaippaṭum, pūtanūl ōr iyaṟkalai. ōviyam ōr ākkakkalai. atupōla moḻikkalaiyum ōr ākkak kalaiyē.
21. moḻi potumakkaḷālum nūlkaḷ pulavarālum ākkap peṟṟavai.
22. orumoḻi cila kāraṇattāl palavāyp piriyalām.
oru perumoḻi pala ciṟumoḻikaḷākap pirintu pōvataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa:
1. maṉpataipperukkam
oru moḻiyaip pēcuvōr, mikappalki oru viyaṉilap parappil paraviviṭuvārāyiṉ, taṭpaveppanilai vēṟupāṭu cēymai, mutaliyavaṟṟāl, pala moḻivaḻakkukaḷ tōṉṟi, nāḷaṭaivil vēṟu moḻikaḷākap pirintuviṭum.
potuvāy, 100 mail cēymaiyil iraṇṭoru coṟkaḷum, 200 mail cēymaiyil cila coṟkaḷum, 400 mail cēymaiyil moḻivaḻakkum, ataṟkappāl moḻiyum vēṟupaṭuvatiyalpu. ip potuvitikkuc cila tavircci (exception) kaḷumuṇṭu.
2. olittiripu (Phonetic Decay and Phonetic Variation)
1. cōmpalāl.
kā. paḷam (paḻam)
ii. uṟuppu vēṟupāṭṭāl.
taṭpaveppanilai, nilacciṟappu, ūṇ, toḻil, paḻakkavaḻakkam, iyaṟkai mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, eḻuttolikkuk karuviyākum uṟuppukaḷ, ovvoru nāṭṭāriṭattum nuṭpamāy vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl, olippumuṟaiyum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. malaiyāḷattil vali meliyātalum (Nasalization) kaṉṉaṭattil pakaram hakaramātalum, teluṅkil ḻakaram ṭakaramātalum kāṇka.

5. coṟṟerintu kōṭal (Natural Selection)
teluṅku il eṉṉuñ collaiyum, kaṉṉaṭam maṉai eṉṉuñ collaiyum tamiḻiṉiṉṟum terintukoṇṭatu coṟṟerintu kōṭal.
6. vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkal (Colonial Preservation)(English, past and present by Trench. p. 55)
nōlai eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ mūlam teluṅkil nūṉa (eḷney) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu vaḻakkaṟṟa col vaḻaṅkal.
7. putuccollākkam.
tāyvaḻakki lillāta col, tokaiccol, īṟu, oṭṭuc coṟkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip putitāy ākkik koḷḷal.
8. tāyvaḻakkoṭu toṭarpiṉmai.
cēranāṭṭut tamiḻ, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉārkkup piṉ, tamiḻoṭu toṭarpiṉmaiyālēyē vēṟu moḻiyāyp piriya nērntatu.
9. piṟamoḻik kalappu (Admixture of foreign elements)
mēṟkūṟiya tiripu muṟaikaḷellām, cōmpalālum aṟiyāmai yālum nērntu oru payaṉum payavātiruppiṉ, moḻiccitaivu (Linguistic Corruption) eṉṉum kēṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇamākum; aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi, ataṟku maṟutalaiyāyiruppiṉ, moḻi vaḻakku vaḷarcci (Dialectic Regeneration) eṉṉum ākkattiṟkuk kāraṇa mākum.
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉatu, tirāviṭar tavaṟumaṉṟu, āriyar tavaṟumaṉṟu, moḻi vaḻakku vaḷarcciyēyākum. āṉāl, am moḻikaḷil vaṭacoṟ kaḷaik kalantatum, am moḻikaḷait tamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭut tiṉatum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku vaṭamoḻiyait tāyākak kāṭ ṭiyatum, tamiḻait tāḻttiviṭṭu vaṭa moḻiyait talaimai yākkiyatum, āriyattāl tirāviṭarkku nērnta tīmaikaḷākum, mikap periya tirāviṭa maṉpataiyai āriyar mutalāvatu pala pirivākap pirittatu moḻiyiṉāl eṉpatu, itaṉāl viḷaṅkum. moḻiyiṉāl tirāviṭaraip pala pirivākap pirittapiṉ, matattālum varaṇavoḻukkattālum tamiḻaraip pala pirivākap pirittaṉar. (itupōtu nāṭṭiyaliyakkattālum tamiḻar pirikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉar.)
teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷ, centamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuc cēyavātalālum, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya āṭcikaṭkappāṟpaṭṭa vātalālum, cila nalla colvaṭivukaḷai yuṭaiyavātalālum,  tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintu kiḷaimoḻikaḷāy, vēṟāka veṇṇap paṭuvataṟkut takutiyuṭaiyaṉavē. āṉāl. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai cēranāṭṭut tamiḻāyirunta malaiyāḷa moḻi. tamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟāka eṇṇappaṭuvataṟkuc caṟṟum takutiyuṭaiyataṉṟu. malaiyāḷa moḻiyaik keṭutta āriyaruḷ talaimaiyāṉavar, tamiḻeḻuttiṉ vakaikaḷāṉa vaṭṭeḻuttilum kōleḻut tilumē taṅkaḷ moḻiyai eḻutivanta malaiyāḷikaṭku, āriyaveḻuttai yamaittuk koṭutta tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉ eṉpataṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamēyillai.

32.	makkaḷ nākarikattaik kāṭṭuvataṟku moḻiyē ciṟanta aṭaiyāḷam.
vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum māntaṉai vēṟupaṭuttuvatu moḻiyoṉṟē. am moḻiyum palatiṟappaṭṭu, avaṟṟaip pēcum makkaḷiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷaik kāṭṭuvatākum.
33.	moḻinūṟ kalaikku naṭuvunilaiyum poṟumaiyum vēṇṭum.
iv viti ellāk kalaikkum potuvāyiṉum, moḻinūṟ kalaikkuc ciṟappāy vēṇṭumeṉpataṟku iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. oruvar perumai pārāṭṭikkoḷḷum poruḷkaḷuḷ, tāy moḻiyum oṉṟu. ākaiyāl, ataṉ mītuḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl naṭuvu nilaiyai nekiḻaviṭak kūṭātu.
tamiḻnāṭṭil, naṭuvunilai yārāyccikkut taṭaiyāka īruṇarccika ḷiruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, āriyattiṟku māṟāka iruppatu uṇmaiyāyiṉum kūṟakkūṭāteṉpatu. iṉṉoṉṟu matappaṟṟu, cuvāmināta tēcikar tiruvācakam, periya purāṇam mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṭikkalāmeṉṟum, cīvaka cintāmaṇi, cilappati kāram mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṭikkakkūṭāteṉṟum kūṟiyatu matap paṟṟākum. ōr ārāycciyāḷaṉ oṉṟai ārāyumpōtu, taṉṉai matamaṟṟavaṉpōlak karutiṉāloḻiya, ataṉ uṇmai kāṇamuṭiyāteṉpatu tēṟṟam.

āytal aṟivuṭaiyār kaṇṇatē - kāyvataṉkaṇ
uṟṟa kuṇantōṉṟā tākum uvappataṉkaṇ
kuṟṟamun tōṉṟāk keṭum.
iṉi, cilar kālṭuvel kūṟiyavāṟu, oppiyalaṟiviṉṟi, oru tuṟai yilēyē amiḻntu, uḷḷam parantu viriyāmal kuvin toṭuṅkap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. nuṇṇiya matiyum ciṟanta ārāycci yiyalpum uḷḷa, kālañceṉṟa pā,vē. māṇikkanāyakar oppiyalaṟi viṉmai yāṟ pala ceytikaḷai uṇmaikku māṟākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar.
ōṅkāra vaṭiviṉiṉṟum tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ vantaṉa veṉṟum; ō eṉṉum vaṭivam uma eṉṉum malaimakaḷ peyarum liṅkak kuṟiyum cērntateṉṟum, civāyanama eṉṉum tiruvainteḻut til, vakaram aruḷaiyum yakaram uyiraiyum nakaram tirōtattai yum makaram malattaiyuṅ kuṟikkumeṉṟum, āytam mūccoliyeṉ ṟum, ataṉ tuṇaikoṇṭu arapi, hepirēyam pōṉṟa moḻi kaḷait tamiḻil eḻutalāmeṉṟum, uṭampaṭu mey eṉpatu uṭampu + aṭum + mey eṉṟu pirintu, piṟaviyaik keṭuppa teṉṟu poruḷpaṭumeṉṟum, okarattiṉ mēl paṇṭaik kālattiṟ puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutiṉatu, civa perumāṉiṉ takkaṇa vaṭivattaik kāṭṭutaṟkeṉṟum, palavāṟu mayaṅkik kūṟiṉar pā,vē. māṇikkanāyakar.
ivaiyellām palavakai malaivukaḷuṭaiyaṉa eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar ellārkkum pulaṉākum. āyiṉum, ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaimaṭṭum, ēṉaiyōrkkum teriyumpaṭi iṅkuk kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
y, v eṉṉum meykaḷ tamiḻil maṭṭumalla, enta moḻiyil īruyirkaḷaip puṇarttālum, iṭaiyil uṭampaṭu (uṭaṉ paṭuttum) meykaḷāyt tōṉṟattāṉ ceyyum. itu ellā moḻikkum potuvāṉa olinūl viti.

Drawer, sower mutaliya āṅkilaccoṟkaḷil, vakaravuṭampaṭu meykkup patilāka yakaravuṭampaṭumey varukiṟatēyeṉiṉ, vakarattiṉum yakaram olittaṟkeḷitātalpaṟṟi, piṟkālattil yakaravuṭampaṭumey peruvaḻakkāyiṟṟeṉak koḷka. tamiḻilum iṅṅaṉamē. āyiṭai, kōyil, cummāyiru mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷaik kāṇka.
māṇikka nāyakaraip piṉpaṟṟuñ cilar, avaraip pōṉṟē pala coṟkaṭkut tavaṟāṉa poruḷum, tamatu karuttiṟkicaiyāta nūṟpākkaḷaiyum colvaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum iṭaiccerukalum pāṭavēṟupāṭu meṉṟum kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. avar viri nōkkaṭaivārāka.
mēṉāṭṭār naṭunilaiyum oppiyalaṟivum uṭaiyarāyiṉum, tamiḻiṉ uṇmaittaṉmaiyaik kāṇa muṭiyāmaikkup piṉ varupavai kāraṇaṅkaḷākum;
1.	tamiḻ taṟpōtu intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil oru ciṟu nilapparappil vaḻaṅkal.
2.	(tamiḻuṭpaṭa) intiya moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum vaṭa coṟkaḷ kalantiruttal.
3.	(kaṭalkōḷālum, āriyattālum, tamiḻar tavaṟṟālum paṇṭait tamiḻnūlkaḷ aḻintupōṉapiṉ) intiyak kalai nūlkaḷellām ippōtu vaṭamoḻiyiliruttal.
4. 	itupōtu pala tuṟaikaḷilum pārppaṉar uyarvāyum tamiḻar tāḻvāyumiruttal.
5. 	pārppaṉar tamiḻariṉ matattalaivarāyiruttal.
6. 	tamiḻaippaṟṟit tamiḻarē aṟiyātiruttal.
7. 	airōppiyar tamiḻaic cariyāyk kallāmai.
kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻit tuṇai vēṇ ṭāta taṉimoḻi yeṉṟaṟintum, tamiḻar uyarnilaik kalaikaḷai āriyariṭattiṉiṉṟum kaṟṟatākat tavaṟṟeṇṇaṅ koṇṭamai yāl, tamiḻil maṉam, āṉmā eṉpavaṟṟaik kuṟikkac col lillaiyeṉṟum, tiraviṭam, cēraṉ, cōḻaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟum, iṉṉa eṉṉum vaṭiviṟkotta akarac cuṭṭaṭiccol tamiḻilillai yeṉṟum, piṟavāṟum piḻaipaṭak kūṟiṉar.
uvilliyam (William), kōlpurūk (Colebrooke), mūyr (Muir) mutaliyavarōveṉiṉ, tamiḻaic ciṟituṅ kallātu, vaṭa moḻiyilēyē mūḻkik kiṭantu, intiya nākarikamellām āriya vaḻiyākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar.
moḻinūṟkalaiyum nūlārāycciyum varavara vaḷarntu varu kiṉṟaṉa. ākaiyāl, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya pala moḻi nūṟ karuttukaḷ in nūṟṟāṇṭil aṭipaṭum. pārppaṉar, āriyattai uyarttik kūṟiya mēṉāṭṭār cilariṉ kūṟṟukaḷait taṅkaṭkēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷākap paṟṟikkoṇṭu, avaṟṟai māṟṟu kiṉṟa puttārāycci tōṉṟātapaṭi, pala vakaiyil tamiḻarai maṭṭantaṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar. mēṉāṭṭil, uṇmai kāṇa vēṇṭumeṉṟu peru muyaṟci naṭantu varukiṉṟatu; āṉāl  kīḻ nāṭṭilō uṇmaiyai maṟaikkavēṇṭu meṉṟē perumuyaṟci naṭantuvarukiṉṟatu. kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyāriṉ tirāviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṉ mutaliru patippukaḷilum, illāta (tirāviṭa nākarikattai yiḻittuk kūṟum) cila mēṟ koḷkaḷ, mūṉṟām patippiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai yellām moḻi nūṟ kalaiyiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattaik kuṟiyātu piṟpōkkaiyē kuṟikkum.
moḻinūliṉ mutaṉmai
oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu, 1. eḻutappaṭṭa varalāṟu (Written History), 2. eḻutappaṭā varalāṟu (Unwritten History) eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa nāṭāyiṉ, perumpālum eḻutap paṭṭirukkum; muṟpaṭṭatāyiṉ, eḻutappaṭṭō paṭātō irukkum. eḻutappaṭā varalāṟu, 1. aṟiyappaṭṭa varalāṟu (Known History) 2. aṟiyappaṭā varalāṟu (Unknown History) eṉa iru tiṟattatu. varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷum karuvikaḷum cāṉṟu kaḷum pōtiya aḷavu iruppiṉ, aṟiyappaṭum; iṉṟēl illai. iṉi, eḻutappaṭṭa varalāṟum,
mey varalāṟu (True History), poy varalāṟu (False History) eṉa iruvakaittu: oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu annāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṟṟum naṭunilaiyum uḷḷavarāl eḻutappaṭiṉ perumpālum meyyā yirukkum: vēṟṟiṉap pakaivarālum taṉṉiṉak koṇṭāṉ mārālum (Quislings) eḻutappaṭiṉ perumpālum poyyāyiruk kum. tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyaruḷ P.T. cīnivācaiyaṅkārum, irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum, T.R. cēca aiyaṅkārum tavirap piṟarellārum perumpālum āriyac cārpāṉavarum naṭunilai tiṟampiyavarumātaliṉ, kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa varalāṟṟaip perumpālum uḷḷapaṭiyum, muṟpaṭṭa varalāṟṟai muṟṟum tirittum eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuk karaṇiyam, kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭatu perumpālum āriyak kalappu illatāyum, tamiḻarkkup perumai taruvatāyum, piṟpaṭṭatu āriyak kalappu uḷḷatāyum, āriyarkkē perumai taruvatāyum iruppatē.
eḻutappaṭā varalāṟṟai eḻutuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭum cāṉṟukaḷ, ilakkiyam, veṭṭeḻuttu (Inscriptions) paḻamporuḷ nūlkaḷ (Archaeology) eṉa mūvakaippaṭum. ilakkiyattuḷ tolkāp piyamum iraṇṭoru puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuṭkaḷum tirukkuṟaḷum tavira maṟṟap paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlkaḷellām aḻiyuṇṭu pōṉamaiyāl kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟai eḻutuvataṟku vēṇṭiya ilakkiyac cāṉṟu illātē pōyiṟṟu; itu pōtuḷḷa veṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ perumpālum ki.pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavaiyātaliṉ, avaiyum payaṉpaṭa villai. iṉi, paḻam poruṭ kalaiyōveṉiṉ, kaṟkālattu iruppuk kālattup parupporuṭ ceytikaḷait tavira, oḻuṅ kāṉa varalāṟṟiṟkuriya nuṇ kuṟippukkaḷait terivikkātatā yiruppatālum, ataiyum ārāytaṟkuriya mutaṟkālat tamiḻaka mākiya paḻam pāṇṭiya nāṭu (kumarikkaṇṭam) muḻutum muḻukip pōṉamaiyālum, atuvum payaṉpaṭātatē. ākavē, eñci niṟkum orē cāṉṟu moḻiyē.
tamiḻ, āriyar varukaikkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumarik kaṇṭattil tāṉākat tōṉṟi muḻuvaḷarcci tiripu uṟavu mutaliyavaṟṟai viḷakkum moḻinūliṉ tuṇai koṇṭē, tamiḻiṉ iyalpaiyum paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟaiyum oruvāṟu aṟiyamuṭiyum.
moḻinūliṉ varaiyaṟai (Definition)
moḻikaḷ tōṉṟiya vakai, avai vaḷarnta muṟai, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟukkuḷḷa toṭarpu mutaliyavaṟṟai viḷakkum nūl moḻinūlām (Philology).
orē moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷaiyellām ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭu, amaippu, piṟappu, poruḷurttumuṟai, tiripu mutaliyavaṟṟai āḷum neṟimuṟaikaḷai viḷakkum nūl col liyal nūlām. (Etymology). ākavē, colliyalnūl moḻi nūliṉ uṭkūṟām. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu piṉṉataṉuḷ aṭaṅkum; piṉṉatu muṉṉataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātu.
moḻinūl iṉṉum uruvākāmai
moḻinūlaippaṟṟi makkaḷukku iṉṉum cariyāṉa uṇarcciyum nampikkaiyum ēṟpaṭavillai. kaṇitam, vāṉūl mutaliya kalaikaḷaippōla moḻinūlum oru tiṭṭamāṉa neṟimuṟaip paṭṭa kalaiyē. cilar moḻinūlaic colliyal nūlākak koṇṭum coṟkaḷiṉ piṟappu vaḷarccikaḷaippaṟṟiya neṟi muṟaikaḷai aṟiyātum, oli yoppumaiyum poruntap pukalal eṉṉum uttiyumpaṟṟic coṟkaṭkut tāṟumāṟāyp poruṭ kāraṇamum mūlamum kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, moḻinūlē ōr uṉṉa allatu kaṟpaṉaik kalaitāṉ eṉkiṟa muṭipaip palar koṇṭuviṭṭaṉar. palakalaikaḷ āyvu (Empirical) nilaikaṭantu uruvākiyuḷḷaṉa. moḻinūl iṉṉum āyvu nilaiyiltā ṉuḷḷatu; āyiṉum, mika aṇukiya kālattil uruvākiviṭum eṉpatu tiṇṇam. mēṉāṭṭu vāṉūṟkalai car aicakku niyūṭṭaṉ kālattiltāṉ avarāl uruvāyiṟṟu. ataṟkumuṉ atu vaḻivaram piṉṟip paḻipaṭu nilaiyiltā ṉiruntatu. atu pōṉṟē iṟṟai moḻi nūlum.
ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷellām ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu pala muṟaiyākap paravip pōṉavarkaḷē eṉpataṟku maṟukka muṭiyāta cāṉṟukaḷuḷa. aṅṅaṉam paravicceṉṟavarkaḷ moḻi tōṉṟumuṉ ceṉṟavarum moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ ceṉṟavarum eṉa irutiṟattār. moḻi tōṉṟumuṉ ceṉṟavar tām kuṭiyēṟiya nāṭṭil nāḷaṭaivil tattam cuṟṟuc cārpiṟkum karuttiṟkum olikkun tiṟaṉukkum ēṟṟavāṟu ovvōr moḻiyai ākkik koṇṭaṉar. attakaiya moḻikaḷiṭaiyē cila moḻiyākka neṟimuṟaikaḷaiyaṉṟic colloppumai kāṇpatu mikavum aritu. teṉ āppirikka moḻikaḷum ciṉam cappāṉ mutaliya kīḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷum, amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷum ivvakaik keṭuttuk kāṭṭām. āriyarum tirāviṭarum moḻi tōṉṟiyapiṉ pirintu pōṉavarātaliṉ, avvirucārār moḻikaṭkum collilum neṟimuṟaiyilum periyatōr nerukkamuṇṭām. āyiṉum, avar iṭattil neṭṭiṭaiyiṭṭup pirintu kālattil neṭ ṭiṭaiyiṭṭuk kūṭiyamaiyiṉ, iṭaikkālattil olimuṟaiyilum collamaippilum coṟṟoṭar ilakkaṇattilum mikunta vēṟupāṭu uṇṭāyviṭṭatu.
oru cikkuppaṭṭa nūṟkaṇṭaic cikkaleṭuppataṟku mutalāvatu ataṉ iru muṉaikaḷil oṉṟaik kāṇavēṇṭum. oru poru ḷurimai paṟṟiya vaḻakkait tīrppataṟku mutalāvatu, mutaṉmutal atai vaittiruntavaṉ yār eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamē. moḻinūlai uruvākkuvataṟku, mutalāvatu, māntaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭam etu veṉṟum toṭarpuḷḷa moḻikaṭkuḷ etu muntiyatu eṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum, potuvāka, muntiya moḻikaḷil colvaṭivam iyalpāyum pintiya moḻikaḷil tirintum iruk kum. tirintamoḻiyai muntiṉatākavum iyalpāṉa moḻiyaip pintiṉatākavuṅ koṇṭu ārāyappukiṉ, pēraṉaip pāṭṭa ṉākak koṇṭu marapuvaḻi kūṟuvatum ciṟitu citaintupōṉa ēṭṭai allatu kalveṭṭait talaikīḻ vaittut tavaṟākap poruḷ koḷvatum pōṉṟatēyākum. mēṉāṭṭār iṉ ṉum tamiḻaic cevvaiyāy ārāyāta tiṉālēyē, avarkaḷ moḻinūl iyaṉmoḻit tōṟṟattai allatu vērccol tōṟṟattai aṟiyamuṭiyāta colliyal nūlāyum iṉamoḻi nūlāyum niṉṟuviṭṭatu.
moḻinūlukkut tamiḻiṉ mukkiyam
māntaṉ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiyaviṭam kumari nāṭē (Lemuria) yeṉpatu tamiḻaṟiyāmalē cila mēṉāṭṭukkalai vallār kaṇṭa muṭipākum. tamiḻc carittiramum tiraviṭa moḻinūlum itaṟkut takka cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. tiraviṭarukkuḷ moḻiyilum ilakkiyat tilum nākarikattilum tūymaippaṭṭavar iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭi luḷḷa tamiḻarē. tamiḻnāṭṭuḷḷum moḻit tiruttam teṟku nōkkiyatē. tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr iruntatu teṉperuṅ kaṭalil muḻukiyuḷḷa kumari nāṭu. atu oru kālattil kōṭikkaṇak kāṉa allatu ilakkakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ nilai peṟṟirun tatu. paṉimalai (imayam) tōṉṟiyataṟku muṉpē atu toṉmutu nāṭāyiruntatu. tamiḻ vaṭakku nōkkic cellac cellac ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tirintu piṉpu tiraviṭamākiṉṟatu. tiraviṭamum vaṭakku nōkkic ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tirintu piṉpu āriya mākiṉṟatu. tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ airōppāvarai ceṉṟu ilattīṉ kirēkkam mutaliya mutuperumoḻikaḷilum kalantuḷḷaṉa. intiyāviṉ paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ tiraviṭarē. ivaṟṟai nōkkumpōtu, tamiḻc carittiram intiya carittirattiṟkum tiraviṭamoḻi nūlukkum maṭṭumaṉṟi, ulaka carittirattiṟkum intiya airōppiya oppiyaṉ moḻi nūlukkumē aṭippaṭaiyāku meṉpatu pulaṉām.
tamiḻ kumari nāṭṭil tāṉē tōṉṟiya moḻiyātalāṉum, tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyākavum āriyattiṟku mutaṟṟāyākavum iruttalāṉum, colliyal tiṟavukōlaiyum moḻinūl tiṟavukōlaiyum taṉṉakattēyē koṇṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ tiraviṭat tāyeṉpatu ippākattiṉ piṟpakutiyilum, tamiḻc collākka muṟaikaḷ centamiḻc colliyal neṟi muṟaikaḷ eṉṉum nūlilum kūṟappaṭum.
tamiḻiṉ talaimaiyai aṟitaṟkut taṭaikaḷ
ulaka moḻikaḷukkuḷ tamiḻ talaimaiyāṉatāyiruppiṉum, 1. carittiramaṟiyāmai, 2. colliyalakarātiyiṉmai, 3. mutunūlka ḷiṟantupaṭṭamai, 4. moḻipaṟṟiya tavaṟāṉa araciyaṟ kaṭcik koḷkai, 5. kalavai moḻinaṭai, 6. tamiḻaṉ aṭimaiyuṇarcci, 7. tamiḻp paṟṟillātār kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷilum āṭciyiṭaṅ kaḷilumiruttal, 8. ārāycciyiṉmai, 9. matappaṟṟiṉāl piṟamoḻi taḻuval, 10. perumpāṉmait tamiḻariṉ kallāmai mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl tamiḻiṉ perumai tamiḻarālum aṟiyap paṭāmal irukkiṉṟatu. intiya carittirattait teṟkiruntu toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum; intiya nākarikam tiraviṭam eṉpataiyum; tiraviṭa uṭalamaippait tiraviṭa maṉṉar, tamiḻa vēḷir, vēḷāḷar mutaliyār mutaliya kulattiṉar eṉṟivariṭait tāṉ kāṇa muṭiyumeṉpataiyum; tiraviṭaṉaik kāṭṭu mirāṇṭi yākac carittira nūlkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiyiruppatum kūṟiyiruppatum perun tavaṟeṉpataiyum; āriyar varumuṉpē, aṉṟaṉṟu, paṉi malai tōṉṟumuṉpē, kumari nāṭṭil caivamum (cēyōṉ vaḻipāṭu) māliyamum (māyōṉ vaḻipāṭu) muṟaiyē kuṟiñci mullait tamiḻar mataṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa eṉpataiyum; ulakiyalum mataviyalum paṟṟiya tiruntiya paḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭi lēyē uḷḷaṉa eṉpataiyum; ennāṭṭilum makkaḷuḷ tāḻntōr uyarntōr eṉa iru cārār iruppatu pōl tamiḻ nāṭṭilum toṉṟu toṭṭu uḷḷaṉareṉpataiyum; avaruḷ uyarntōriṉ mataṅkaḷaiyum paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyumē āriyar mēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟait tamavākak kāṭṭiṉar eṉpataiyum; moḻikaḷil tēvamoḻiyeṉa oṉṟillai yeṉpataiyum; aṅṅaṉamiruppiṉ, atu iyaṟkaiyum eḷimaiyum veḷip paṭai yum naṭunilai aṉpu piṟappoppu vēḷāṇmai mutaliya karut taṟivippum paṟṟit tamiḻēyām eṉpataiyum; paḻantamiḻar piṟa tuṟaikaḷiṟ pōṉṟē moḻi, ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉpavaṟ ṟilum talaiciṟantiruntaṉar eṉpataiyum; tām karutiya ellāk karuttukkaḷaiyum aṟinta ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum kuṟikkac collamait tiruntaṉar eṉpataiyum aṟital vēṇṭum.
itaṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyai mutaṉmoḻiyāyum vaṭanūlai muta ṉūlāyum vaittut tamiḻārāyappukiṉ, uṇmaikku nēr māṟāṉa muṭipukaḷē tōṉṟu meṉpataiyum, vīra cōḻiyam ilakkaṇakkottu pirayōkavivēkam mutaliyaṉa aḷavai nūl kaḷākā eṉpataiyum uṇartal vēṇṭum.
iyalpāṉa moḻikaḷum coṟkaḷum oru neṟippaṭṭē tōṉṟi iyaṅkukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, muṟaippaṭiyārāyiṉ avaṟṟiṉ neṟimuṟaikaḷai yellām kaṇṭu koḷḷalām eṉpatu, ic cuṭṭu viḷakkattai naṭunilaiyāy nuṇuki nōkkuvārkku iṉitu pulaṉām.
eṉatu moḻiyārāycci kuṉṟāvāṟu iṭaiyiṭai ūkkivarum eṉ naṇpar tirumāṉ va. cuppaiyā piḷḷai avarkaṭkum piṟarkkum yāṉ mikamikak kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ.
ivvārāyccikkum ataṉ veḷiyīṭṭiṟkum tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāyiruntutaviyaruḷum ellā valla iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭi kaḷai neñcāra niṉaittut talaiyāra vaṇaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
moḻinūṟ kulaivu
moḻinūlāṉatu varalāṟṟiyal, vaṇṇaṉaiyiyal, oppu nōkkiyal eṉṉum mūviyalkaḷaiyum oruṅkēyuṭaiyatēṉum, mēlaimoḻinūlār iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻai aṭippaṭai yākak koḷḷātu tiripil tiripākiya āriyattai aṭippaṭai yāka vaittu āyntatiṉāl, mūlamoḻiyaiyum atu tōṉṟiya vakaiyaiyuṅ kāṇātu kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kurī ippōl iṭarp paṭṭu, varalāṟṟiyalai aṭiyōṭu vilakkiviṭṭu vaṇṇaṉai yiyalaiyum oppiyalaiyumē kaiyāṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.
oru kuṭumpattiluḷḷa makkaḷuḷ ovvoruvaraiyum paṟṟi, ivar iṉṉapāliṉar; iṉṉa niṟattar; iṉṉavaḷarttiyar; iṉṉa tōṟṟattar; iṉṉa iyalpiṉar; iṉṉa tiṟamaiyar eṉṟu kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatu vaṇṇaṉai moḻiyiyal (Descriptive Linguistics).
oru kuṭumpattār ellāraiyum oppunōkki, ikkuṭumpattil ittaṉaiyar āṭavar; ittaṉaiyar peṇṭir; ittaṉaiyar civappar; ittaṉaiyar karuppar; ittaṉaiyar neṭiyar; it taṉaiyar kuṟiyar; ittaṉaiyar ottavar; ittaṉaiyar vēṟupaṭṭa var eṉṟuraippatu pōṉṟatu oppiyaṉ moḻinūl. (Comparative Linguistics).
oru kuṭumpattāruḷ, iṉṉār tantaiyar; iṉṉār tāyār; iṉṉār putalvar; iṉṉār putalviyar; iṉṉār tamaiyaṉār; iṉṉār tampimār; iṉṉār tamakkaiyār; iṉṉār taṅkai mār eṉṟiṅkaṉaṅ kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatu varalāṟṟu moḻinūl (Historical Linguistics.).
varalāṟṟu moḻinūl moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟa vaḷarccikaḷaik kūṟuvatāl ēṉaiyīriyalkaḷiṉuñ ciṟantatām. oppiyaṉ moḻinūl varalāṟṟai aṟiyat tuṇaiceyvatāl, ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākac ciṟantatām. vaṇṇaṉaimoḻinūl piṟamoḻit toṭarpu nōkkātu oru moḻiyai vaṇṇippatu maṭṭum ceyvatāl, uṇmaiyil ilakkaṇattiṉpāṟ paṭuvatē. iṅṅaṉam im mūṉṟiyalpum muṟaiyē talaiyiṭai kaṭaiyām.
mēlaiyar mūlamoḻiyaiyum moḻi tōṉṟiya vakaiyaiyum aṟiyāmaiyāl, avar varalāṟṟu moḻinūleṉṟu koḷvatu, ilakkiyat tuṇaikoṇṭu oru moḻiyiṉ allatu moḻik kuṭumpattiṉ palvēṟu nilaikaḷait toṭarcciyākak kāla muṟaippaṭi kūṟuvatēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. itu oru moḻiyiṉ muḻuc coṟṟokutiyai maṭṭumaṉṟit taṉic coṟkaḷaiyun taḻuvum.
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutivarai ulaka mutaṉmoḻi etuveṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭāviṭiṉum, eṉṟēṉum orunāḷ atu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭum eṉṉum nampikkaiyō nampācaiyō mēṉāṭukaḷil iruntu vantatu, innūṟṟāṇṭilō atu muṟṟuṅ kaiviṭappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. ataṉ viḷaivē vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl. itaic ciṟappāka vaḷarttu varupavar amerikkarē. avar tamiḻaic ceṅkoḷkait tamiḻp pulavar vāyilākak kaṟkum varai, ivvaḻuviyal moḻinūl mēṉāṭukaḷil toṭa rattāṉ ceyyum. āyiṉum tamiḻar āriya aṭimait taṉattāṟ piṟanta tam tāḻvuṇarcci nīṅkip pakuttaṟivuk kaṇ koṇṭu pārppārāyiṉ, tamiḻiṉ muṉmaiyaiyum piṟamoḻi kaḷiṉ vaḻinilaittaṉmaiyaiyum kāṇat tavaṟār eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
moḻi paṟṟiya cila vuṇmaikaḷ
1. moḻimāntaṉ amaippē.
ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ etuvum iṟaivaṉ paṭaittataṉṟu. iyaṟkai yolikaḷāṉa kiḷavikaḷum ceyaṟkai yolikaḷāṉa coṟkaḷu māka, ovvoṉṟāyp paiyap paiyap pallāyira māṇṭuk kālañ celaviṭṭu, oru makkaṭ kūṭṭattār kūṭṭuṟa vāl ōr oḻuṅkupaṭa amaittukkoṇṭa olittokutiyē moḻi.
ulaka moḻikaḷ ēṟattāḻa mūvāyiram mutal nālāyiram varai vevvēṟu tokaiyiṉavākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mānta rellārum ōriṇaip peṟṟōriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟip palki vēṟu paṭṭiruttal pōṉṟē, moḻikaḷum orē mūla moḻiyiṉiṉṟu tirintu palvēṟu vakaiyil vēṟupaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. karuttunikaḻviṟku moḻi tēvaiyillai.
nilaittiṇai tavira maṟṟellā uyiriṉaṅkaṭkum karuttuṇṭu. moḻipēcāta ūmaiyarum pēcuvār pōṉṟē karutuvar. ciṟappākak kaṭṭapommaṉ iḷavalai nōkkuka.
uṇarcci, viruppu, cūḻvu, tuṇipu, makiḻcci, iraṅkal, niṉaippu eṉṉum eḻuvakaiyē karuttiṉ iyalkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṟku moḻi tēvaiyillai.
3. tāymoḻiyuṅ kaṟkappaṭuvatē.
oru kuḻaviyai araiyāṇṭiṟkuḷ tāyiṉiṉṟu pirittu makkaḷ moḻit toṭarpillātu vaittu vaḷarttāl, ettaṉaiyāṇ ṭāyiṉum emmoḻiyum pēcātu.
oru tamiḻak kuḻaviyaip piṟantavuṭaṉ ōr āṅkila mātiṭam vaḷarkkaviṭṭāl, atu nāḷaṭaivil āṅkila moḻiyē pēcum; ōr arapi mātiṭam vaḷarkka viṭṭāl, vāyamaippu nuṇ vēṟupāṭṭāl olippu muṟaiyiṟ caṟṟu iṭarppaṭiṉum, arapi moḻiyē pēcum.
ovvoru piḷḷaiyum, kuḻantaip paruvattiṟ peṟṟōriṭamum, piḷḷaip paruvattiṟ peṟṟōr uṟṟōriṭamum, paiyaṟ paruvattiṟ peṟṟōr uṟṟōr maṟṟōriṭamum, ovvoru collāka muyaṟci varuttamum viṉaiyuṇarcciyu miṉṟik kaṟṟē taṉ tāy moḻiyaip pēcap payilkiṟatu.
tāymoḻiyai naṉku pēcak kaṟṟapiṉpum, atiṟ pēcuvatai niṟuttiviṭṭup pallāṇṭu ayaṉmoḻiyilēyē pēcivariṉ, tāymoḻip pēccāṟṟal mikakkuṟaiyum.
4. moḻivāḻiṭam nāvē.
moḻiyeṉpatu, oru vilaṅkum pūtamum pōṉṟu makkaṭku appāṟpaṭṭa ōr uruvamaṉṟu. oru makkaḷ vakuppār tam karut tukkaḷaip piṟarkkut terivikkum vāyilākap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭum olittokutiyē moḻi. oli nikaḻvatu vāyil. ākavē, moḻivāḻiṭam nāvē. moḻivalar nāvalar. moḻit teyvam nāmakaḷ. oru collai vaḻaṅkāviṭiṉ, atu oliyiḻakkum. ataṉāl iṟantupaṭum. atu ilakkiyattil eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉ, piṇampōṟ kiṭappatumiṉṟi maṟain toḻiyum, pala coṟkaḷ oliyiḻappiṉ oru pakutiyum, ellāccoṟkaḷum oliyiḻappiṉ muḻuvatum, oru moḻi yaḻiyum. orumoḻi āṭcimoḻiyāyiṉ, potumakkaḷ ataṉ coṟkaḷaiyē āḷa virumpuvar; ataṉāl, intiyāl tamiḻ keṭātu eṉṟu pērāyattalaivar colvatu pitaṟṟalē.
āṅkilam āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu tamiḻil illāta aṟiviyal ilakkiyac ciṟappiṉālēyē. avviyalpu intikkillai. mēlum, āṅkilarkkut tamiḻai yoḻikka vēṇṭum eṉṉum kuṟik kōḷillai. vaṭamoḻiyārkkum intiyārkkum aḵtuṇṭu.
moḻiyeṉpatu uṇmaiyil olittokutiyē. makkaḷ aṟivuṅkaruttum cēymaiyarkkum piṟkālattārkkum payaṉ paṭu māṟu, moḻiyiṉ oliyuṟuppukkaḷaik kaṭpulaṉākiya varivaṭi vil eḻutikkāṭṭuvatē ilakkiyam eṉṉum eḻuttu moḻi. ākavē, makkaḷiṉṟik karuttillai; karuttiṉṟi oliyillai; oli yiṉṟi varivaṭiveṉṉum eḻuttillai; eḻuttiṉṟi eḻutap paṭṭa ilakkiyamillai.
ākavē, āriyavētam māntaṉālum iṟaivaṉālum iyaṟṟap paṭātu tāṉē tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟum, maraikkāṭu eṉṉum ūrp peyarai maṟaikkāṭu eṉṟu māṟṟi, vētaṅkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭut tirukkāppucceyta iṭameṉṟum, kūṟi makkaḷai yēmāṟṟuvatu, tuṇiccalāṉa poyyum kaṭun taṇṭaṉaikkuriya kuṟṟamumākum.
5. camaṟkirutam ilakkiya moḻiyē.
intiya āriyar moḻi vaḻakkaṟṟapiṉ eḻunta vēta ilakkiya moḻi tamiḻoṭu kalantuṟṟa virivē camaṟkirutam. atu oru kālum pēcappaṭṭataṉṟu. ātalāl, atu piṟantatumillai; iṟantatumillai; ceyaṟkaiyākap puṉaiyappaṭṭa pāvai pōṉṟatē. itai mēlaiyar iṉi aṟivar.
6. ellā moḻiyum kaṭaṉ koṇṭu vaḷarcci peṟā.
uyaravuyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākātu. kaṭivāḷamuñ cēṇamum iṭṭālum kaḻutai kutiraiyākātu. oru moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭaiya, ēṟkeṉavē coṟperukkum vērkcol vaḷamuṅ koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum; allatu, āṅkilampōṟ putuppuṉaivāḷar moḻiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum. iraṇṭu millā inti pōṉṟa intiya moḻikaḷellām āṅkilam pōl aṟiviyal moḻikaḷākap paṇpaṭuttap paṭavēṇṭu meṉṟu, aḷaviṟantu potumakkaḷ paṇattaic celaviṭṭu, āṅkilattai yoḻikkap pārkkum mūṭak koḷkaiyaiyum muṭṭāḷ taṉattaiyum, pakaṭit taṉamāyp paḻittukkāṭṭat teṉṉāliyirāmaṉ pōṉṟa tiṟamikka nakaiyāṇṭi nāṭṭi lillai.
7. centamiḻum nāppaḻakkam
tāymoḻiyil tērcci peṟutaṟkē pēccup paḻakkam vēṇṭu meṉiṉ, ayaṉ moḻiyil tiṟampeṟa ataṉ iṉṟiyamai yāmaiyaic colla vēṇṭuvatillai. āṅkilaṅ kaṟkum māṇavarkku ilakkaṇaṅ kaṟpittal maṭṭum pōtātu; pēccup payiṟcikkum pōtiya vāyppaḷittal vēṇṭum.
moḻipeyar muṟai
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷai allatu kuṟiyīṭukaḷai vēṟu moḻiyil peyarkkum muṟaikaḷ aintām. avaiyāvaṉa:-
1. nērccol (e.kā: Pen -ötš)
2. vērpporuṭ col. (e.kā: motor - iyaṅki)
3. ciṟappiyaṟ col. ( e-kā: train - pukai vaṇṭi)
4. oppolic col 
5. mutaleḻuttuc col
ivaṟṟuḷ, mutalatu muṉṉamēyuḷḷa col, ēṉaiyavellām putitāy ākkappeṟuvaṉa.
iv ai vakaiyuḷ, mutal mūṉṟum tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkumā? eṉṉum muntiya kaṭṭuraiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. ēṉaiyiraṇṭē iṅkuk kūṟappeṟum.
oppolic collāvatu, oliyilum poruḷilum okkumāṟu amaittukkoḷḷap peṟum col.
e-kā:		āṅkilam		tamiḻ
			Parliament		pārāḷumaṉṟu
			bracket		piṟaikkōṭu
Parliamentv‹gJ pēcuṅkaḷam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum taṉiccol (oru col). pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉpatu pārai āḷum maṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muccoṟṟoṭar.
Parler eṉpatu pēcu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum pireñcu viṉaic col; ment eṉpatu oru pireñcut toḻiṟ peyarīṟu.
ākavē, ivviru coṟkaṭkum eḷḷaḷavum toṭarpillai āyiṉum,oliyilum poruḷilum pēraḷavu ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.
braccal eṉpatu breeches (kuṟuṅkāṟ caṭṭaikaḷ) eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum ilattīṉ col. braga eṉpatu ataṉ icuppāṉiyat (Spanish) tiripu. bragueta eṉpatu ataṉ kuṟumaipporuḷ vaṭivam (diminutive). ataṉiṉṟu mutalil bragget eṉṟum, piṉṉar bracket eṉṟum āṅkilac col tirintatu.
piṟaik kōṭu eṉpatu piṟaipōl vaḷaintuḷḷa kōṭu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum iru coṟṟoṭar.
ivaṟṟiṟkum oru toṭarpumillai. āyiṉum, oliyum poruḷum ottuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam amaital mika arumaiyātaliṉ, oru cila coṟkaḷ tām im muṟaiyil amaikkappeṟum.
mutaleḻuttuc collāvatu, oru poruḷaip paṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉaic coṟkaḷ mutaleḻuttukkaḷai maṭṭum cērttu oru peyar allatu kuṟiyīṭu amaittal.
e-kā: Radar.
itu, radio detection and ranging eṉṉum nāṟcol mutaleḻuttuk kōppu. immuṟai paṟṟiyē, katiriyat tuppaṟivum vīccīṭum eṉṉum muṟcoṟkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttuk kaḷaic cērttuk katuvī eṉṟō, (vāṉūrti marakkalam kaṭaṟkarai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ) vākkum (direction) vīccum (range) kāṇi eṉṉum poruḷil vāvīṇi eṉṟō, amaittukkoḷḷalām.
āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷellām poruḷaḷavil peruvaḷaṅ koṇṭaṉa vēṉum, collaḷavil mika eḷiya muṟaiyilēyē amaintuḷḷaṉa. mēṟkūṟiya aim muṟaikaḷaiyum kaiyāḷiṉ ellā aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum tamiḻil eḷitāy moḻi peyarttuviṭalām. tāymoḻip paṟṟoṉṟē tēvai.  (teṉmoḻi.)
moḻiyārāycci (COMPARATIVE PHILOLOGY)
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām Turanian, Semitic, Aryan eṉa mupperuṅ kavaikaḷā yaṭaṅkum. ivaṟṟuḷ ovvoru kavaiyum paṟpala kiḷaikaḷāyp piriyum,. ovvoru kiḷaiyum ovvoru moḻit tokutiyākum. ivaṟṟuḷ, turēṉiyak kavaiyil tirāviṭak (Dravidian)»is¡F¤ talaimaiyākak karutappaṭuvatu amiḻtiṉumiṉiya tamiḻmoḻiyē.
oru marattil, ciṟu kiḷaikaṭku nēr iyaipu iṉṟēṉum avaṟ ṟait tāṅkum peruṅkavaikaṭku aṭimarattuṭaṉ nēr iyai piruppatupōla, ulaka moḻittokutikaṭkuḷ ciṟu pirivukaṭku nēr iyaipiṉṟēṉum avaṟṟiṟku mūlamāṉa perum pirivukaṭku nēriyaipu tiṭṭamāyuḷḷatām. ellāk kiḷaikaṭkum mūlamāṉa ōr aṭimaramiruppatupōla ellā moḻikaṭkum mūlamāṉa oru tāymoḻiyu miruttal vēṇṭum. ulakattiluḷḷa makkaḷel lām oru tāy vaḻiyiṉarātaliṉ avar vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷum orutāy vayiṟṟu vaḻiyilātal vēṇṭum.
ulakattiṉ makkaḷ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiṉaviṭam ki.mu.2000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ intumakā camuttirattil amiḻntupōṉatum talaiccaṅkamiruntatumāṉa kumarināṭē. āttirēliyā, teṉṉin tiyā, teṉṉāppirikkā eṉṟa mūṉṟum ātiyil nilattālicain tiruntaṉa veṉṟu mōṉāṭṭup paṇṭitarkaḷ kaṇṭa vuṇmaiyē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām. ākavē, makkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ pēciya moḻi tamiḻēyātal vēṇṭum.
ellā moḻikaḷum iyaṟkai ceyaṟkaiyeṉa irupālā yaṭaṅkum. ivaṟṟuḷ tamiḻ oṉṟumē iyaṟkai. ēṉaiyavel lām ceyaṟkai. ātalāṉ, tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻi (Primitive Language) eṉṟum, ēṉaiya tirimoḻi (Derivative Languages) eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ellā moḻikaṭkumuḷḷa perumpāṟ coṟkaḷaip pakutip poruḷuṭaṉ iyaṟkai vaṭivil vaḻaṅkuvatu tamiḻē yeṉṟu moḻinūlāl (Philology) viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ āṅkilam (English), kirēkku (Greek), ilattīṉ (Latin) eṉṟa mum moḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum teṉcoṟkaḷ āyirakkaṇak kiṉa veṉpatai ainnūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭavaṟṟāl veḷḷiṭai malaipōl teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭutum:

moḻiyārāycciyum moḻiyakaḻvārāycciyum oṉṟē
moḻi yeṉpatu, oru makkaḷiṉ pēcum pēccilum iyaṟṟum ilakkiyattilum āḷum coṟkaḷiṉ muḻumaiyāṉa coṟ ṟokuti, coṟkaḷaṉṟi, moḻiyeṉṟu taṉi vēṟāka oṉṟu millai. ātalāl collārāycciyē moḻiyārāyccikku aṭippaṭai. collārāycci oru allatu pala coṟkaḷiṉ varalāṟupaṟṟi yirukkalām. aṅṅaṉamē, moḻiyārāycciyum oru allatu pala moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟupaṟṟi yirukkalām. moḻiyeṉpatu, collaiyum oru makkaḷiṉattiṉ coṟ ṟokutiyaiyum kuṟittalāl, moḻiyārāycci eṉpatu collā rāycciyaik kuṟittaṟkum iṭamuṇṭu.
moḻikaḷuḷ, intiyum malaiyāḷamum pōṉṟu putiyavum uḷa: kirēkkamum ilattīṉamum pōṉṟu mutiyavum uḷa. tamiḻō, mutiyavaṟṟuḷḷum mutiya mutaṉmoḻi. putumoḻikaḷum mutumoḻikaḷiṉ tiripākavē tōṉṟiyiruttalāl, collā rāycciyaip poṟuttamaṭṭil, iruvakai moḻiyum oṉṟē.
āytal eṉpatu, oru poruḷai nuṇuki nōkki ataṉ uṇmait taṉmaiyai uṇartal.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam  (tol. uri. 32)
ārtal niramputal. āra āytal ārāytal.
akaḻtal eṉpatu tōṇṭutal. mēṉmaṭṭattil tōṉṟātu putaintu allatu maṟaintu kiṭappataik kalliyeṭuttal tōṇṭutal. atupōṉṟu āḻntu ārāytalum tōṇṭutal.
o.nō: tōṇṭu - tēṇṭu - tēṭu - tēṭṭam.
nōṇṭu - nōṭu - nōṭṭam, nōṭu - nāṭu - nāṭṭam.
nōṇṭu - nēṇṭu - nēṭu - nēṭṭam.
potuvāka, coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷait teḷivākak kāṭṭakkūṭiya moḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē. ataṉāl.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (peya.1)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam terivikkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, tamiḻilum, ellāc collum kaṇṭamaṭṭil allatu kēṭṭamaṭṭil vērpporu ḷuṇarttuvaṉa alla. pala coṟkaḷai āḻn takaḻntārāyntē, avaṟṟiṉ vērccollaiyum mūlapporu ḷaiyum aṟital kūṭum. ataṉāl
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (uri. 96)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamē kūṟukiṉṟatu.
coṟkaḷ, oru poruṭcol eṉṟum, palporuṭcol eṉṟum, poruḷuṇarttum vakaiyil iruvakaip paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. palporuṭ collum, oru vērccol eṉṟum, palvērc col eṉṟum, irutiṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ātalāl, vaṭi voppumai yoṉṟē nōkkip palvērc collai oru vērc col leṉṟu mayaṅkutal tavaṟām.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, veḷ eṉṉum iyaṟcollai eṭuttuk koḷḷalām. itu veppam, veṇṇiṟam, veṟumai, viruppam mutaliya palporuḷuṇarttuvatu. avaṟṟuḷ, veppamum virup pamum, namnāṭṭut taṭpaveppa nilaiyum ataṉ viḷaivāka eḻum makkaṭ karuttum nōkkiṉ, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muraṇpaṭ ṭaṉavākum.
namnāṭu potuvāka veppanāṭāyiruppiṉum, kōṭaik kālat tiṟ kuḷirnta kuṭippaiyum mārikkālattil veppak kuṭippai yum virumpukiṉṟōm. cama taṭpaveppa nāṭukaḷilum miku taṭpa nāṭukaḷilum eṉṟum kuḷirntiruppatāl, an nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷ veppattai virumpuvatu iyalpē. ataṉāl, tiyāka rācap pāṭakar pāṭṭaic cevikuḷirak kēṭṭu iṉpuṟṟēṉ eṉṟu namar colvatu pōṉṟu, amerikka nāṭṭut talaivarkkuc cerumaṉiyil vetuvetuppāṉa (warm) varavēṟpiruntatu eṉṟu kuḷirnāṭṭār kūṟak kēṭkiṉṟōm.
tamiḻil vemmai eṉṉum colliṟku veppam, viruppam eṉṉum iruporuḷum uḷḷaṉa.
urumamum karumamum uruppamum veppamum
aḻaṉamum kōramum aḻalum vemmai  (7:105)
eṉpatu piṅkalam.
vemmai vēṇṭal  (tol. uri. 36)
veḷ - veṭṭai = 1. veppam. aṉal veṭṭaiyāṟ curuṇṭu (irāmanā. yut. 14) 2. nilakkoti (W). 3. kāmaccūṭu. kāma veṭṭaiyilē matimayaṅki  (taṉippā.) veṭṭaiccūṭu eṉpatu peruvaḻakku.
te. veṭṭa, ka. veṭṭe.
veḷ eṉṉum iyaṟcollaṭip piṟanta veyya, veyyōṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷ veppattaip pōṉṟē viruppattaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr oru kālattil kuḷirnāṭṭilum vāḻntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu col lārāycci yillār muṭivukoḷḷa nērkiṉṟatu. kaṭal koṇṭa kumarināṭṭiṉ teṉkōṭi veppa maṇṭalattiṉ teṉṉellaik kuṭpaṭṭatē.
tamiḻccoṟkaḷ perumpālum ukarac cuṭṭaṭiyiṟ piṟan tuḷḷaṉa. u- ul eṉṉum mūla aṭi, kul, cul, tul, nul, pul, mul eṉṉum āṟu vaḻiyaṭikaḷākap pirintum, tirintum, pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷaip piṟappittuḷḷaṉa. vakaram ukaramutal koḷḷāmaiyāl, vukara aṭiyākap piṟanta col oṉṟumillai. ātalāl, olikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷoḻinta vakara meymmutaṟ coṟkaḷellām pakara mutalaṭiyiṉiṉṟō makara mutalaṭiyiṉiṉṟōtāṉ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
veḷ eṉṉum iyaṟcol, veppap poruḷil meḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟum, viruppap poruḷil peḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟum tirintuḷḷatu.
muḷ - muḷi. muḷital = erital. muḷi - miḷi - miḷir. miḷirtal = erital. viḷaṅkutal. miḷ - miṉ = oḷi. oḷi neruppiṉ iyal. muḷ - miḷ - meḷ - veḷ - vey - veyya = veppamāṉa. 
veyyōṉ = veppamāṉavaṉ, katiravaṉ, koṭiyōṉ.
ḷakaramey yakaramākat tirital perumpāṉmai.

koḷ - koy, toḷ - toy. piḷ - piy. poḷ - poy - pai.

pullutal = poruntutal, virumputal. pul - pur - puri. purital = virumputal.
