tiricoṟkaḷil yakaramey tokuvatu iyalpē.
e-kā: vēy - vēyntōṉ - vēntaṉ. tēy - tēyvu - tēvu - tēvaṉ.
iṅṅaṉam, coṟkaḷiṉ mūlattai āḻntāyntutāṉ kāṇa voṇṇum,
tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr kumarināṭṭil naṇṇilakkōṭṭai aṭuttē vāḻntavar. nāḷ muḻutum veyilil uḻaippavar karuttum, niḻalil vāḻpavar veḷuttum, iruppar. veṇ kaḷamar X karuṅkaḷamar, veḷḷāḷar X kārāḷar eṉṉum peyariṇaikaḷai nōkkuka. uḻutuṇpavar veyililum, uḻuvittuṇpōr niḻalilum iṉṟum vāḻtal kāṇka.
iṉi, uṇavuc ciṟappiṉālum uṇṇā vaṟumaiyālum veḷuttum karuttum pōvatum uṇṭu. veḷḷokkal X kārokkal eṉṉum peyariṇaiyai nōkkuka.
tamiḻar veppamikka kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟē mēṉāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟamaiyai,  V.R. irāmaccantira tīṭcitariṉ origin and Spread of the Tamils eṉṉum maṟukkoṇā ariya ārāycci varalāṟṟu nūlai nōkkik kāṇka.
māntaṉ mutaṟpeṟṟōr naṇṇilakkōṭṭai yaṭutta nāṭṭilēyē vāḻntiruttal kūṭum.
mēlaināṭṭu moḻiyārāycciyāḷar eṉṉeṉṉa uṇmai kāṇpiṉum, avaiyellām kumarināṭṭu māntaṉ tōṟṟattiṟ kō tamiḻaṉ tōṟṟattiṟkō muraṇāka iruttal muṭiyāteṉ pataiyum, moḻiyārāycciyum moḻiyakaḻvārāycciyum oṉṟēyeṉpataiyum, itaṉāl tiṭṭavaṭṭamāyt terintu koḷka.  (centamiḻc celvi akutōvar 1980)
moḻiyiṉa vuṇarvum paṭimuṟaik kalappum
intiyāvil, ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭil, iṉṟuḷḷa piṟavik kulaṅkaḷ piṟanāṭukaḷil illāviṭiṉum, celvanilai, paṭṭam, toḻil, matam, kaṭci, paḻaṅkuṭi eṉpaṉa paṟṟiya potuvakai yāṉa vakuppukkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. iruppiṉum, avaiyellām nāṭṭumoḻi vakaiyil ōriṉamāy oṉṟupaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru nāṭṭiṟ paṉmoḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ taṉittaṉip peruntokai makkaḷaik koṇṭiruppiṉ, ap paṉmoḻikaḷum aṅku nāṭṭu moḻikaḷākac camanilaiyaṭaikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭilō, āriya vaḻiyiṉarukkum tamiḻē tāymoḻiyāyiruntum, avaratu iraṇṭakattālum avarkkaṭimaippaṭṭa araciyaṟ kaṭciyāḷariṉ pētaimaiyālum, vēṟṟu moḻikaḷē pōṟṟappaṭṭu nāṭṭu moḻi tūṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ik kēṭṭunilai evvakaiyilum uṭaṉē māṟṟappaṭal vēṇṭum. allākkāl, tamiḻ vāḻavum tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟavum eḷḷaḷavum iṭamillai.
kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭākat tamiḻa ruḷḷattil ūṭṭappaṭṭa āriya nañcait tiṭumeṉṟu veḷiyēṟṟa muṭiyātātaliṉ, paṭippaṭiyākattāṉ tamiḻar kumukāyam tirunti varal vēṇṭum.
mutaṟkaṇ, orē kulattiluḷḷa akamaṇap pirivukaḷellām puṟamaṇap pirivukaḷākalām.
ataiyaṭuttu, teṟkum vaṭakkum vevvēṟu peyar koṇṭuḷḷa orē kulattār orē peyar koḷḷalām. e- ṭu: nāṭārum kirāmaṇiyārum, kaikkōḷarum ceṅkuntarum.
ataṉpiṉ, vēḷāṇ kuṭiyiṉiṉṟē paṭaittalaimaip patavi paṟṟip pirinta mutaliyār kulattiṉar, piḷḷaip paṭṭamuḷḷa marakkaṟi veḷḷāḷaruṭaṉ maṇavuṟavu koḷḷalām.
ataṉpiṉ, mukkulattār (kaḷḷar, maṟavar, akampaṭiyar) orē kulattārākalām.
ataṉpiṉ, uṇṭāṭṭiṟ kalantukoḷḷuṅ kulaṅkaḷ maṇa vuṟavilum kalakkalām.
ataṉpiṉ, caṟṟē ēṟṟat tāḻvuḷḷa kulaṅkaḷ, muṉpu uṇṭāṭṭiṟ kalantu piṉpu koḷvaṉai koṭuppaṉaiyuñ ceytu koḷḷa lām. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyumpōtu, oru kulattāriṉ uṇavumuṟai iṉṉoru kulattārkku ēṟkāviṭiṉ, atai māṟṟiviṭa vēṇṭum.
iṟutiyil, kalviyilum nākarikattilum tuppuravilum uyarntu, otta pataviyil allatu nilaimaiyiluḷḷa tāḻttappaṭṭa vakuppuk kuṭumpaṅkaḷuṭaṉ, uyarnta vakuppārum iruvakai yilum uṟavāṭalām.
tāḻttappaṭṭōriṉ uyarviṟku ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkak kalvi iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. ataṉāltāṉ, taṉ māṉavuṇarcciyum tuppuravum uṇṭākum. tuppuravum nākarikamum iṉṟēl tīṇṭāmai oḻiyātu. tīṇṭāmai oḻinta piṉṉum, calakkup purai vārutalum muṭi tiruttutalum pōṉṟa toḻil ceyvār vīṭṭiluṇṭal ellārkkum ēṟkātu.
tamiḻar tamiḻaruṭaṉ uṟavu kalantapiṉ, teluṅkar kaṉṉaṭar mutaliya tiraviṭaroṭum uṟavu kalakkalām. ataṉpiṉ, tamiḻp paṟṟuḷḷa inti makkaḷoṭum maṇattoṭarpu koḷḷalām.
makkaḷ tāmākat tiruntāviṭiṉ, etirkālattil uṇmaiyāṉa potuvuṭaimaiyāṭci vantu viraintu tirutti viṭum. iṉṟu potuvuṭaimaiyeṉṟu ulakil vaḻaṅkuvatu kūṭṭuṭaimaiyiṉ (Socialism) muṉainta vakaiyē yaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
mōtiram vaittal viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭuvārellārum irukaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, kaṭcik koruvarāka iruvaroḻiya ēṉaiyarellām, kaṭci vāriyāy iru varicaiyāka etiretir uṭkārntu koḷvar. uṭkārāta iruvarum tattam kaṭci varicaiyiṉ piṉṉāl niṉṟu koṇ ṭiruppar. avaruḷ oruvar oru mōtirattaik kaiyil vaittuk koṇṭu kuṉintu, tam varicaiyil ovvoruvar piṉṉālum atai vaippatāka naṭittu, yārēṉum oruvar piṉṉāl vaittuviṭṭu varicai neṭukalum ceṉṟapiṉ nimirntu niṟpar. etir varicaikkup piṉṉāl niṟpavar, mōtiram vaikkappaṭṭa iṭattai iṉṉārukkup piṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭikkūṟa vēṇṭum. cariyāyc colliviṭiṉ, aṭuttamuṟai etir varicaiyāḷ mōtiram vaittal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ muṉvaittavarē vaittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu āṭappeṟum. kīḻē yiruppavar āṭṭu neṭukalum uṭkārntu koṇṭē yiruppār.  (ta.nā.vi.)
mōriyap paṭaiyeṭuppu
(ki.mu. 3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
tamiḻakattiṉ mēl mutaṉmutalākap paṭaiyeṭukkat tuṇinta vaṭaintiya (makatanāṭṭu mōriya) aracaṉ pintucāraṉ (ki.mu. 301 -273), oru perumpaṭaiyait teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kaṉuppiṉāṉ. appaṭai kōcar eṉṉum oruvakaip porunarait tuṇaik koṇṭu, teṉkaṉṉaṭam eṉṉum koṇkāṉattiṉ kaṭaṟkaraip pakutiyāṉa tuḷunāṭṭiṟ pukuntu, atai yāṇṭukoṇṭirunta naṉṉaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ maṉṉaṉai nāṭṭaiviṭṭu ōṭṭi viṭṭatu. itaṉai,
aḻiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉṉaṉ
naṟumā koṉṟu nāṭṭiṟ pōkkiya
oṉṟu moḻik kōcar pōla
eṉṉum kuṟuntokaip pāṭṭāl (73) aṟiyalām.
piṉṉar, kōcar teṉkiḻakkāka vantu koṅkunāṭṭup paḻaiyaṉ mōkūrait tākkiṉar. avaṉ avaraip puṟaṅkaṇṭu turattiṉāṉ. ataṉpiṉ kōcarukkut tuṇaiyāka makatattiṉiṉṟu oru putuppaṭai vantatu. atu varumpōtu, ataic cērnta tērkaḷum carakku vaṇṭikaḷum varuvataṟkut taṭaiyāyirunta pāṟaikaḷai yellān takarttu, pātaiyaic cevvaip paṭuttikkoṇṭu vantatu. atu,
tuṉaikā laṉṉa puṉaitērk kōcar
toṉmū tālat tarumpaṇaip potiyil
iṉṉicai muracaṅ kaṭippikut tiraṅkat 
temmuṉai citaitta ñāṉṟai mōkūr
paṇiyā maiyiṟ pakaitalai vanta
mākeḻu tāṉai vampa mōriyar
puṉaitēr nēmi yuruḷiya kuṟaitta
ilaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi yaṟaivāy
eṉṉum akappāṭṭup pakutiyāl (251) aṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi, appaṭai vaṭukaraiyum tuṇaikkoṇṭu vanta teṉpatu,
muraṇmiku vaṭukar muṉṉuṟa mōriyar
teṉṟicai mātiram muṉṉiya varaviṟku
viṇṇuṟa vōṅkiya paṉiyiruṅ kuṉṟat
toṇkatirt tikiri yuruḷiya kuṟaitta
aṟai yiṟantu  (akam. 281)
eṉṉum māmūlar kūṟṟāl teriya varukiṉṟatu.
ākavē, vaṭukar kōcar mōriyar eṉṉum mūviṉa maṟavaraik koṇṭatu appaṭai eṉpatai aṟiyalām. vaṭukaraiyum kōcaraiyum puṟaṅkāṇiṉ mōriyar tāmē puṟaṅkāṭṭuvar eṉpatai aṟinta cōḻaṉ iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi, taṉ nāṭṭaik kākkum kaṭamaiyai yuṇarntu, muṉpu vaḷaṅkeḻukōcar viḷaṅkupaṭai nūṟi (akam 205), piṉpu koṇkāṉañ ceṉṟu pāḻiyaraṇai yaḻittu orē yaṭiyāka mōriyarait tamiḻakatti ṉiṉṟu turattic ceruppāḻi yeṟinta eṉṉum virutaṭai moḻiyum peṟṟāṉ. itaṉai,
eḻāat tiṇitōṭ cōḻar perumakaṉ
viḷaṅkupukaḻ niṟutta iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi
kuṭikkaṭa ṉākaliṟ kuṟaiviṉai muṭimār
cempuṟaḻ puricaip pāḻi nūṟi
vampa vaṭukar paintalai cavaṭṭi  (akam. 375)
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyalām. avaṉ vaṭavaṭukar vāḷōṭṭiya (puṟam. 378) ceyal caṟṟup pintiṉatā yirukkalām. vaṭa vaṭukar kaliṅkanāṭṭut teluṅkar.
kīrantaiyiṉ vīṭṭuk katavait taṭṭi, ataṉāl vēṇṭātu taṉ kaiyaik kuṟaittukkoṇṭa koṟkaip pāṇṭiyaṉum, makaṉai muṟai ceyta maṉumuṟai kaṇṭa cōḻaṉum, ki.mu. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarā yiruntirukkalām.
maṉumuṟai eṉṟatu vaṭamoḻi maṉutaruma cāttira muṟaiyai yaṉṟu. annūliṉ kālam ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. ataṟku muṉ tōṉṟiya āriyataruma nūlkaḷum, naṭunilaiyiṉṟik kulattiṟ korumuṟai kūṟuvaṉavē. kaṉṟaik koṉṟataṟkuk kaḻuvāy allatu taṇṭaṉai yeṉṉeṉṟu cōḻaṉ viṉaviyapōtu, poṟkaṉṟu ceytu pirāmaṇarkkuk koṭuttu, āniraiyoṭu kāṭu ceṉṟu oru mātam puṉmēyntu varavēṇṭumeṉṟu, pirāmaṇar kūṟiṉar. atai avaṉ oppuk koḷḷātu, uyiruk kuyirē yīṭeṉṟu taṉ makaṉ mēl tērēṟṟik koṉṟāṉ. vaṭa nūlkaḷiṟ katiravaṉ kula aracaruḷ mutalvaṉākak kuṟikkap peṟum maṉu, tamiḻppeyar koṇṭa oru cōḻaṉā yiruntirut tal vēṇṭum. avaṉ kaṇṭa naṭunilai muṟaiyaiyē, makaṉai muṟai ceyta cōḻaṉ ciṟappup peyar kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
tillaiyiliruntu pāṇiṉīyattiṟku virivurai (pāṣyam) varainta patañcaliyār ki.mu. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarē. pēcu - va. - pāṣ - pāṣyam.
āriyam tamiḻakattil vērūṉṟi, nālvaraṇamum piṟappilamain tavai yeṉṟum, pirāmaṇaṉ nilattēvaṉ eṉṟum, tuṟavāl maṭṭum vīṭupēṟeṉṟum, tuṟavu pirāmaṇaṉukkē vuriya teṉ ṟum, pirāmaṇaṉ vēḷvi vaḷarppataṉālēyē ulakam naṭai peṟukiṉṟa teṉṟum, pirāmaṇaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyvataṉā lēyē ēṉai mūvaraṇattārum maṟumaiyil naṉṉilai yaṭaiva reṉṟum, pala tīya koḷkaikaḷtamiḻaruḷḷattiṟ patikkappaṭṭu varuvatu kaṇṭa tiruvaḷḷuvar, tamiḻariṉ kaṇ tiṟakkavum avarai muṉṉēṟṟavum tam tirukkuṟaḷai iyaṟṟiya ruḷiṉatu ki.mu. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. eṉ tirukkuṟaḷ tamiḻ marapuraiyaip pārkka.
pirāmaṇarkku muṟṟum aṭimaiyākit tamiḻakattaip pāḻākkiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ talaiciṟanta palvēḷvic cālai (yākacālai) mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti, ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉāyirun tirukkalām.
iṟaiñcuka perumaniṉ ceṉṉi ciṟanta
nāṉmaṟai muṉiva rēntukai yetirē  (puṟam.6)
eṉṟu, vētamōtiya pirāmaṇarai yellām muṉivar eṉṟu kūṟi, avarukkumuṉ taṉ kuṭumiyaviḻntu viḻumāṟu talai kuṉintu vaṇaṅkumpaṭi, mutukuṭumip pāṇṭiyaṉai vēṇṭiya kārikiḻār eṉṉum tamiḻat tamiḻp pulavaṉ nilaimaiyai nōkkumpōtu, ettuṇai yuṇavuttaṭ ṭiruppiṉum ikkālamē naṟkālam eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. iṉi,
palcālai mutukuṭumiyiṉ
nalvēḷvit tuṟaipōkiya
tollāṇai nallāciriyar
puṇarkūṭ ṭuṇṭa pukaḻcāl ciṟappiṉ
nilantaru tiruviṉ neṭiyōṉ pōla  (maturaik. 759 - 763)
eṉṉum aṭikaḷ, tolkāppiyam araṅkēṟiya kālattu nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉum, nāṉmaṟai muṟṟiya ataṅ kōṭṭācāṉaik koṇṭu palvēḷvi ceytavaṉ tāṉō eṉṟu aiyuṟac ceykiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉāṉa karikāl vaḷavaṉ,
irunila maruṅkiṟ porunaraip peṟāac
ceruveṅ kātaliṟ ṟirumā vaḷavaṉ
vāḷuṅ kuṭaiyu mayirkkaṇ muracum
nāḷoṭu peyarttu naṇṇārp peṟukavim
maṇṇaka maruṅkiṉeṉ valikeḻu tōḷeṉap 
puṇṇiya ticaimukam pōkiya vannāḷ
acaivi lūkkattu nacaipiṟak koḻiyap 
pakaivilak kiyatip payaṅkeḻu malaiyeṉa 
imaiyava ruṟaiyuñ cimaiyap piṭarttalaik
koṭuvari yoṟṟik koḷkaiyiṟ peyarvōṟku
mānīr vēli vaccira naṉṉāṭṭuk 
kōṉiṟai koṭutta koṟṟap pantarum
makatanaṉ ṉāṭṭu vāḷvāy vēntaṉ
pakaippuṟattuk koṭutta paṭṭimaṇ ṭapamum
avanti vēnta ṉuvantaṉaṉ koṭutta
nivantōṅku marapiṉ tōraṇa vāyilum
poṉṉiṉum maṇiyiṉum puṉaintaṉa vāyiṉum
nuṇviṉaik kammiyar kāṇā marapiṉa
tuyarnīṅku ciṟappiṉavar tollō rutavikku
mayaṉvitittuk koṭutta marapiṉa vivaitām
oruṅkuṭaṉ puṇarntāṅ kuyarntō rēttum
arumpeṟaṉ marapiṉ maṇṭapam  (cilap. 5: 89-110)
niṟuvi,
kāṭukoṉṟu nāṭākki
kuḷantoṭṭu vaḷamperukki

kōyiloṭu kuṭiniṟīi.  (paṭṭiṉap. 283 -6)
kāvirikkuk karaikaṭṭi vēli nilam āyiraṅ kalam nel viḷaiyac ceytu,
nīriṉ vanta nimirparip puraviyum
kāliṉ vanta karuṅkaṟi mūṭaiyum
vaṭamalaip piṟanta maṇiyum poṉṉum
kuṭamalaip piṟanta āramum akilum
teṉkaṭal muttuṅ kuṇakaṭal tukirum
kaṅkai vāriyum kāvirip payaṉum
īḻat tuṇavuṅ kāḻakat tākkamum
ariyavum periyavum neriya vīṇṭi  (paṭṭiṉap. 185 - 192)
paṭṭiṉum mayiriṉum parutti nūliṉum
kaṭṭu nuṇviṉaik kāruka māntarum,  (cilap. 5: 16-7)
kañca kārarum cempucey kunarum
maraṅkol taccarum karuṅkaik kollarum
kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñarum maṇṇīṭ ṭāḷarum
poṉcey kollarum naṉkalan tarunarum
tuṉṉa kārarum tōliṉ tuṉṉarum
kiḻiyiṉum kiṭaiyiṉum toḻilpala perukkip
paḻutil ceyviṉaip pālkeḻu mākkaḷum  (cilap. 5:29-34)
vēṟu pala toḻilāḷarum taḻaittōṅkac ceytāṉ. āyiṉ, avaṉum āriyac cāykaṭaiyil vīḻntu,
aṟamaṟak kaṇṭa neṟimā ṇavaiyattu
muṟainaṟ kaṟiyunar muṉṉuṟap pukaḻnta
tūviyaṟ koḷkait tukaḷaṟu makaḷiroṭu
paruti yuruviṟ palpaṭaip puricai
yeruvai nukarcci yūpa neṭuntūṇ
vēta vēḷvit toḻiṉmuṭit tatu  (puṟam. 224)
mikamika varuntat takkatē.
ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa, āriyappaṭai kaṭanta neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉum, kaṇṇakikkup paṭimai camaitta cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉum, tamiḻiṉattiṉ perumaiyaik kāttataṉāṟ pārāṭṭat takkavarē.
āriyap paṭai eṉṟatu āntirap pēraraciṉ (ki.mu. 567 - ki.pi.220) vaṭukap paṭaiyai. vaṭanāṭṭil allatu vaṭakkil uḷḷavarai yellām āriyareṉṉum vaḻakku iṭaikkālattil eḻuntu viṭṭatu. āriyak kūttāṭi ṉālum kāriyattil kaṇṇā yiru. eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyil, āriyak kūtteṉṟatu vaṭukariṉ kaḻaikkūttaiyē.
putaiya leṭuttava ṉeṉṟu ciṟaiyiliṭappaṭṭa vārttikaṉai viṭutalai ceytapiṉ, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ
nīrttaṉ ṟituveṉa neṭumoḻi kūṟi
aṟiyā mākkaḷiṉ muṟainilai tirintaveṉ
iṟaimuṟai piḻaittatu poṟuttalnum kaṭaṉeṉa
maṉṉippuk kēṭṭatuṭaṉ,
taṭampuṉaṟ kaḻaṉit taṅkāl taṉṉuṭaṉ
maṭaṅkā viḷaiyuḷ vayalūr nalkiyatē
aḷaviṟku miñciṉatām. ataṟkum mēl, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ vārttikaṉ kālil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkiyatu tamiḻiṉattiṟkē aḻiyāp pēriḻivām. itaṉāl aṟṟaip pirāmaṇar koṭṭamum tamiḻar aṭimaittaṉamum teḷivām.
kārttikai kaṇavaṉ vārttikaṉ muṉṉar
irunila maṭantaikkut tirumārpu nalki
eṉṟu (cilap. 23:120 -1) iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ paṭimāṟṟaṇiyāl maṟaittuk kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
kōpperuntēviyiṉ ūṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭa maṉakkalakka nilaiyil, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āyntupārātu kōvalaṉaik koṉṟatu kaṭuṅkuṟṟa māyiṉum,
poṉcey kollaṉ ṟaṉcoṟ kēṭṭa
yāṉō varacaṉ yāṉē kaḷvaṉ
maṉpatai kākkun teṉpulaṅ kāval
eṉmutaṟ piḻaittatu keṭukaveṉ ṉāyuḷeṉa
maṉṉavaṉ mayaṅkivīḻn taṉaṉē teṉṉavaṉ
kōpperun tēvi kulaintaṉaḷ naṭuṅkik
kaṇavaṉai yiḻantōrkkuk kāṭṭuva tilleṉ
ṟiṇaiyaṭi toḻutuvīḻn taṉaḷē  (cilap. 20:75 -81)
eṉṉuñ ceyti, vaḷaintakōlai uṭaṉē nimirttiviṭṭatu.
itaṉāṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ ceṅkōṉmaiyum veḷiyāyiṟṟu.
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, tamiḻaracarai yikaḻnta kaṉaka vicayarmēl, paṉimalaiyileṭutta pattiṉitteyvap paṭimaik kallai ēṟṟik koṇarntatu, eṉṟum tamiḻarkkup perumai taruvatē. āyiṉum, akkallaik kaṅkaik karaiyil nīrāṭṭiya pōtu, māṭalaṉ eṉṉum pirāmaṇaṉukkut tulainiṟait tāṉamāka 50 tulām poṉ koṭuttatum, koṭuṅkōḷūril avaṉ coṉṉavuṭaṉ vēḷvi ceytatum, avaṉatu āriya aṭimait taṉattait teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pattiṉip paṭimaikkuk kalleṭukka vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟapōtu pēriyāṟṟaṅ karaiyiliruntu orēnāḷil taṉ talainakarkkut tirumpiya taṉālum, nīlamalaiyil taṅkiṉataṉālum, tirumpi vantakkāl neytal nila makaḷir varavēṟṟup pāṭiya taṉālum, avaṉ kālattil koṭuṅkōḷūrē talainakarāka iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. tamiḻakam maṭṭumaṉṟi intiyā muḻutum avaṉ atikārattiṟ kuṭpaṭṭiruntataṉāl, avaṉ virumpiyiruppiṉ koṅku nāṭṭuk karuvūrai mīṇṭum talai nakarākak koṇṭiruttalkūṭum. āyiṉ, avaṉ muṉṉōrē atai viṭṭuviṭṭataṉālum, nāṭṭai vaḷampaṭuttum nīr vaṇikattiṟkup pūmpukār pōl oru tuṟainakarē ēṟṟatā yiruntataṉālum koṭuṅkōḷūri lēyē nilaittu viṭṭāṉ. tirumāl kōvilākiya āṭakamāṭam, karuvūriṟ pōṉṟu koṭuṅkōḷūrilum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. ak kālattiṟ poṉṉiṟkup pañcamillai. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattiṟ koṅku nāṭṭup pakutiyai yāṇṭa cērar kuṭiyiṉar, takaṭūr atikamāṉ caravaṭiyiṉarē.
neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āriyappaṭaiyai veṉṟiruntataṉālum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ paṭai kaṅkaiyaik kaṭattaṟku nūṟṟuvar kaṉṉar (cātakarṇi? cātavākaṉar?) utaviyataṉālum, ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil āntirap pēraraciṟkut tamiḻnāṭu uṭpaṭṭirunta teṉṟu koḷḷa iṭamillai.

vañcaṉam-vaṇcaṉa
iru moḻiyilum vaṅku eṉpatē mūlam. āyiṉ, vaṭavar vakku (vak) eṉṉum valittal vaṭiviṟ kāṭṭuvar. itiṉiṉṟē vakra, vakrīkaraṇa mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ piṟakkum.

circumvent eṉṉum āṅkilac collaiyum nōkkuka.  (va.va. 246)
vañci
vañci-vañc
vaṅku-vañcu-vañci. o.nō: aṅku-añcu.  poṅku-poñcu.
vañci-vañcakam, vañcakam, vañcaṉam, vañcaṉai.
vañcu=vañcakam. "vañcē vallarē" (tēvā. 828:3)  (va.va.245)
vañci :  vaṅkutal vaḷaital, vaṅku-vañcu-vañci. o.nō: aṅka (vaḷai)-añcu, poṅku-poñcu.
oruvaṉai vañcippatu avaṉ tappamuṭiyāvāṟu vaṭṭañcuṟṟi vaḷaivatu pōliruttalāl, vaḷaitaṟ karuttil vañcittaṟ karuttup piṟantatu. Circumvent eṉṉum āṅkilac collai nōkkuka.
vañci-vañcam, vañcakam, vañcaṉam, vañcaṉai.
vañci : vañc(va.), vañcakam-- vañcaka (va.), vañcaṉam-- vañcaṉa (va.).  (ti.ma : 753.)

vaṭṭam
vaṭṭam-vrutta (i.vē.).
val-vaḷ-vaṭṭu-vaṭṭam=1. vaḷaivu.
villai vaṭṭappaṭa vāṅki  (tēvā. 5: 9).
2. vaḷaitaṭi.
pukariṉar cūḻ vaṭṭattavai  (paripā. 15 : 61)
3. vaṭṭavaṭivam.  4. kativaṉai allatu tiṅkaḷaic cuṟṟiya kōṭṭai.
5. vaṇṭiccakkaram.  6. tirikai,  7. tōṭkaṭakam (piṅ.).
8. vaṭṭamāṉa appavakai.
pākoṭu piṭitta viḻaicūḻ vaṭṭam  (perumpāṇ. 378).
9. vaṭṭakkal.
vaṭavar tanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭam  (neṭunal. 51).  (va.va.)
10. niṟaikōl taṭṭu.
vaṭṭam tottatu vāṇipam vāyttatē (tiruman. 1781).
11. vaṭṭak kēṭakam.
aiyiru vaṭṭamo ṭeḵkuvalan tirippa  (tirumuru. 111).
12. ālavaṭṭam (vaṭṭapperu viciṟi.)
13. vaṭṭamāṉa nīrccāl (piṅ.).
14. vaḷainta kuḷam (piṅ.). 14. cuṟṟup pakkam
kōyil vaṭṭamellām  (cīvaka. 949).
15. cilavūrkaḷaik koṇṭa nāṭṭup pakuti.
16. vaṭṭamāṉa celavu.  (va.va.)
puravivaṭṭan tāṉpukak kāṭṭukiṉṟāṟku  (cīvaka. 442).
17. oru cuṟṟu.  18. oru kōḷ vāṉamaṇṭalattai oru muṟai cuṟṟivaruṅ kālam.  19. oru taṭavai.
vināyakar nāmattai nūṟṟeṭṭu vaṭṭañ ceytu.  (vināyakapu. 74:214)
20. tōṟum. "āṭṭai vaṭṭam kācu oṉṟukku" (S.I.I.ii, 122, 27).
iccoṟku iṉṉum palaporuḷkaḷ uḷa. mutaṉmai yāṉavaiyē iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa.
vaḷ-vaḷku-vaṭku.  vaṭṭutal=vaḷaital, vaṇaṅkutal.
vaṭkār=vaṇaṅkār, pakaivar.  vaṭkār-vaṭkar.
vaṭku eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭimoḻiyilillai.
vaṭṭam-vaṭṭakai=nāṭṭuppakuti, vaṭṭil, ciṟu kiṇṇam, vaṭ ṭakai eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyi lillai.  (va.va: 246-247)
vaṭṭaṇam (1)
vaṭṭaṇam-aṭṭaṉa
vaṭṭam-vaṭṭaṇam=kēṭakam
iṭṭa vaṭṭaṇaṅkaṇ mēleṟinta vēl  (kaliṅ. 413).
vaṭṭaṇam-vaṭṭaṇi.  vaṭṭaṇittal=vaṭṭamātal, vaṭṭa mākkutal.
vaṭṭaṇi eṉṉum viṉai vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyi lillai.  (va.va:247)
vaṭṭaṇam (2)
vaṭṭaṇam-varttaṉa
vaṭṭaṇam-vaṭṭaṇai=vaṭṭam, vaṭṭamāṉa celavu, iṭam valam cuṟṟukai, vaṭṭamāṉa tāḷak karuvi, vaṭṭak kēṭakam, uruṇṭai.
vaṭṭaravu=vaṭṭa vaṭivu.
vaṭṭu-vaṭṭaṉ=vaṭṭuppōl uruṇṭu tiraṇṭavaṉ.
kuṟuvaṭṭā.......... kūṉiṉpiṟappu  (kalit. 94).
ivvaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
vaṭṭam-vaṭṭāram=cuṟṟuppuṟa nāṭṭuppakuti.
te. vaṭṭāramu, ka.tu. vaṭṭāra.
vaṭṭāram eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
vaṭṭu-vaṭṭā=vaṭṭamāṉa kalam
vaṭṭāvun tāmpūlamun tukilun tāṅkiniṟpa.  (cītak.). (va.va: 247-248)
vaṭṭi
vaṭṭi-vartti
vaṭṭu-vaṭṭi.  vaṭṭittal-vaṭṭamātal, cuḻalutal, cuḻaṟṟutal, cuṟṟik kaṭṭutal, vaḷaittal, vaḷaitteḻututal, uruṭṭutal.
ka. paṭṭicu (bad@d@isu).
vaṭṭi=kaṭakappeṭṭi, kūṭai.  (va.va: 248).
vaṭṭikai
vaṭṭikai-varttikā
vaṭṭi-vaṭṭikai = 1. cittiram.
vaṭṭikaip pāvai nōkki (cīvaka. 2085).
2. cittirameḻutuṅkōl.
vaṭṭimaic ceytiyiṉ  (maṇi. 4 : 57)
3. vaṭṭam.  4. cuṟṟaḷavu.  5. kūṭai.  6. parical.  7. oruvakai virutu.
ivaṟṟuḷ mutaliru poruḷkaḷē vaṭamoḻiyil uḷa.
vaṭṭi-vaṭṭil=vaṭṭamāṉa uṇkalam, kiṇṇam, kaṭakap peṭṭi, nāḻikai vaṭṭil, oruvakai virutu.
ka. paṭṭalu (b)
ivvaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
vaṭṭu=vaṭṭamāṉa cil, vaṭṭamāṉa cūtāṭṭuk karuvi, vaṭṭamāṉa karuppukkaṭṭi.
ivvaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
vaṭṭai=cakkarattiṉ cūṭṭu, vaṭṭakai, pirā. vaṭṭā. ivvaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.  (va.va: 248)
vaṭṭu
vaṭṭu
vaḷ--vaṭṭu=vaṭṭamāṉa viḷaiyāṭṭu allatu cūtāṭṭak karuvi.  vaḷ + tu = vaṭṭu.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭam - vrutta (va.).  (ti.ma : 753)
vaṭakam
vaṭakam -vaṭaka (t@)
vaṭṭam-vaṭam-vaṭaku-vaṭakam=araitta paruppuṭaṉ kaṟic carakkuc cērttularttiya tāḷippuruṇṭai.  (va.va : 248)
vaṭacol teṉcol kāṇumvaḻikaḷ
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutivarai vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām perumpālum vaṭacollē eṉṉum tava ṟāṉa karuttu, tamiḻaruḷḷattilum nilaittiruntatu. vaṭamoḻi yilum  teṉcoṟkaḷuṇmai kaṇṭu av vuṇmaiyai mutaṉ mutal veḷippaṭuttiyavar paṇṭārakar (Dr.) kuṇṭarṭṭu, kālṭu vel, pōppu eṉṉum mēlaiyaṟiñarāvar. ivaruḷ iṭaiñar, talaiciṟantavar. avarum, teṉ collai vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷaik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi, vaṭacollait teṉcolli ṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷaik kūṟiṉārallar. ēṉeṉiṉ atu avaratu tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṟkut tēvaiyāṉataṉṟu. āyiṉum, avar teṉ coṟkuk kūṟiya viti kaḷait tuṇaikkoṇṭē vaṭacoṟkum cilavaṟṟai vakuttuk koḷḷalām. avai varumāṟu.
vaṭa coṟkaḷ :
1.	mūlavaṭivil āriyaveḻuttum āriya meymmayakkamum amaintiruppaṉa.
2.	paṇṭait tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟakālat tamiḻil vaṭamoḻi yiṉiṉṟu vantu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
3.	tamiḻil vēriṉṟi āriyattilēyē vēr koṇṭiruppaṉa.
4.	tamiḻum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta tiraviṭamumāṉa ellā moḻi kaḷilum vaḻaṅkātu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟilmaṭṭum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
5.	mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
6.	āriyattiṟ ciṟappup poruḷ allatu ciṟappu viṉait toṭarpu koṇṭiruppaṉa.
cila teṉ coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peruvāriyāy vaḻaṅkuvatāl perumpāṉmai vaḻakku vaṭacoṉmaikkuc cāṉṟākāteṉa vaṟika.
mēṟkūṟiya vitikaḷ allatu nilaippāṭukaḷ oṉṟiṟku mēṟ poruntiruppiṉ, avaṟṟilē mikutikkut takkavāṟu vaṭa coṉmai tēṟṟamām. āyiṉum, avai kaṇita muṟaippaṭṭa tulliya vitikaḷalla. ātalāl, paranta moḻinūṟ kalviyum neṭunāṭ collārāycciyum ciṟnata naṭunilaiyumilavēl avai peritum payaṉpaṭā. uṇmaiyāṉa teṉcollait teṉcolleṉṟu kūṟuvatum naṭuviṉmaiyil etirikaḷ kūṟa lām. āyiṉ āḻnta ārāycciyum tūya  maṉaccāṉṟum vali yuṟuttiṉ evarukkum eḷḷaḷavum añcavēṇṭuvatiṉṟu. cilar aṟivārāycci valimaiyiṉṟit tam paṭṭam patavic ciṟappāl oru muṭipiṟku varukiṉṟaṉar. atu mikat tavaṟām.
paṇṭait tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālat tamiḻil iruvakai vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷuḷ `pacu' eṉpatu oṉṟākum. itu vaṭacolleṉpatu veḷippaṭaiyāyiṉum, orucār tamiḻp pērāciriyar itait teṉcolleṉpatāl, māṇavarum ārāycci yillātārum mayaṅkuvataṟku, iṭaṉāvatuṭaṉ, avaravar virumpiyavāṟē pala vaṭa col teṉcoleṉavum pala teṉ col vaṭacoleṉavum paṭṭu, uṇmaiyāṉa ārāycci valiyaṟṟup pōyviṭukiṉṟatu. pala aṭippaṭait teṉcollai vaṭa colleṉṟu kūṟum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṟṟum valiyuṟṟuviṭukiṉṟatu.
pacuvaik kuṟikkum tūyateṉcoṟkaḷ, ā (āṉ), kaṟavai. kuṭañcuṭṭu, kurāl, kō, curai, peṟṟam (irupāṟpotu) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, kaṟavai eṉpatu pāl kaṟakkappaṭuvatu. curai eṉpatu maṭi peruttatu; kuṭañcuṭṭu eṉpatu oru kuṭam pāl kaṟappatu: kurāl eṉpatu pukār (kapila) niṟantatu. peṟṟam eṉpatu mikap paruttuyarntatu: ā, kō eṉpaṉa potuppeyar. ivaṟ ṟuḷ ā eṉpatu tamiḻilum tiraviṭattilum maṭṭum vaḻaṅkum: kō eṉpatu āriyattilum vaḻaṅkum.
E.cow, A.Sax, cu, G. kub, D. and Dan. koe, Ice. ku, Sc. kye, skt. go, gaus.
kōvai mēyppavaṉ allatu vaḷarppavaṉ kōvaṉ, kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kōṉāṉ. o.nō : āyaṉ - āvai vaḷarkkum iṭaiyaṉ.
pacu eṉṉum vaṭacol, pācattiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum vērp poruḷatu. pācam = kayiṟu. āriya vēḷviyiṟ kaṭṭap paṭum uyirikaḷ. māntaṉ, āṉ, kutirai, veḷḷāṭu, cemmaṟi yāṭu eṉṉum ainteṉṟu atarvavētam (xi, 2, 9 etc) kūṟuvatāka, māṉiyar villiyamcu tam vaṭamoḻiyāṅkila vakarātiyiṟ kāṭṭuvar. (pak-611).
pacu eṉpatu mutaṟkālattil, potuvāka, kaṭṭappaṭṭa oru vilaṅkai maṭṭum taṉippaṭavum tokutiyākavum (irupāṟ potup peyarākak) kuṟittu. piṉpu vēḷvi yuyirip peyarāka vaḻaṅki, ataṉpiṉ āviṟkuc ciṟappāka vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu.
āriya vaḻakkait taḻuviyē. pacu eṉṉum col tamiḻilum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil vilaṅku. kāḷai eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.
"pacuvēṟit tirivāṉōr pavaṉ" (tēvā. 760.4) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷaiyaiyum, "pacuk kaḷaip pōlac cellum naṭaiyāl" (patiṉo. paṭṭiṉattiruvēkam. 32) eṉṉumiṭattil vilaṅkai yum, pacu eṉṉum col kuṟittal kāṇka. āṉ eṉṉum tūya peṇpāṟ col, tamiḻ marapiṟku māṟāka.
āṉmukattaṉ  aṭaṟkaṇnāyakaṉ.  (kantapu. pāṉukō. 95)
eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷaiyaik  kuṟippatum āriya vaḻakkait taḻuviyē.
pacu eṉpatu āvai maṭṭuṅ kuṟippatu vaḻakkamākum.
vēta āriyar tamiḻarōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu civaneṟik koṇ muṭipu (caiva cittāntam) kaṟṟapiṉṉarē, pācam, pacu, pacupati eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, muṟaiyē. taḷai (malam), pulampaṉ (āṉmā). iṟai eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil aṇiyiyaṉ muṟaiyil āḷap peṟṟaṉa.
pacu eṉṉum coṟku iṉamāka, ilattīṉil, peck (cattle) bos eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, kirēkkattila bous eṉṉum collum uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉiraṇṭaiyum gaus eṉṉum vaṭa collōṭiṇaippar mēlai moḻi nūlārum akarāti yāḷarum. itu attuṇaip poruttamākat tōṉṟavillai. āyiṉum kakarattiṟkum pakarattiṟkum pōlit toṭar piruppatāl, atai muṟṟum maṟattaṟkillai.
Pecu eṉṉum iyaṟ colliṉiṉṟu tirintu paṇattaik kuṟikkum Pecunia (money) eṉṉum ilattiṉ peyarc collum, Pecuniary eṉṉum āṅkilap peyareccamum, celvattaik kuṟikkum māṭu eṉṉum colloṭu oppunōkkaṟ kuriyaṉa.
kēṭil viḻuccelvam kalvi yoruvaṟku
māṭalla maṟṟai yavai  (kuṟaḷ. 340)
kō (āṉ) eṉṉum teṉcol mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum, pecunia eṉṉum ilattīṉ col māṭu eṉṉum teṉcollottuc celvattai yuṇarttuvatum, pērāciriyar pi.ṭi. cīnivāca aiyaṅkārum, paṇṭārakar irāmac cantira tīṭcitarum kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻaruḷ oru kūṭṭattārē mēlai nāṭu ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟi yuḷ ḷamaiyai uṇarttum.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, pacu eṉpatu vaṭacollēyeṉṟum, atait teṉcolleṉpatāl poyyoṭu kalanta meyyum poyyākip pala tūya teṉ collum vaṭacollākat tōṉṟu meṉṟum, ātalāl ataṉāl tīmaiyē yaṉṟi naṉmaiyillai yeṉṟum, pacumpullait tiṉpatu pacuveṉpatu caṟṟum poruntā teṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
iṉi, pācam eṉṉum vaṭacol, pacumai, pacai, pāci eṉṉum teṉ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ tiripeṉak koḷḷa iṭamirup piṉum, intiyat tōlāṟ ceyyappaṭum pātakkūṭu (boot) intiyak kālaṇiyākāmai pōṉṟē. bj‹ brhšÈÅ‹W âÇªJ xU áw¥ò¥ bghUis íz®¤J« tlbkhÊ¤ âÇ brhšY« bj‹ brhšyhfhik f©LbfhŸf.(jÄœ¥ghit 4ām āṇṭuc ciṟappumalar) (maturai eḻuttāḷar maṉṟam)
vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkun tolkāppiyat teṉcoṟkaḷ
iṟṟai muḻuttamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ muṉmaiyatu tolkāppiyamāta liṉ atil vantuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷ ācai, vaiciyar pōṉṟa aintāṟē yākum. vaṭacolleṉa mayaṅkum ataṉ piṟa coṟ kaḷellām tūya teṉcollē yātal veḷḷiṭaimalai. āyiṉum ārāycci yillārkku aṟivippāṉ vēṇṭi avaṟṟai muṟaiyē ovvoṉṟā yeṭuttu moḻinūṟ paṭi viḷakkuvām.
paṭimai
palpukaḻ niṟutta paṭimai yōṉē.  (tol. pāyiram)
mēviya ciṟappiṉ ēṉōr yaṭimaiya  (tol. akat. 30)
paṭimai eṉṉuñ col paṭi eṉṉum viṉaiyaṭiyākap piṟantatu. paṭi eṉpatu paṭu eṉpataṉ maṟuvaṭivam. ivviraṇṭum paṭ eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyavai.
paṭutal = paṭital i. = . vīḻtal.  ii. olittal.
vīḻtaṟporuḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyākap paṭi eṉpataṉiṉṟum pala karuttukkaḷum coṟkaḷum piṟakkum.
paṭital = 1. viḻutal : kīḻttaṅkal, nilaipeṟutal, paḻakutal.
pēci muṭivātal. (e-ṭu) vilai paṭintatu.
2. viḻutal.  - oruvariṉ aṭivaṇaṅkal, aṭippaṭutal, kīḻp paṭital. tāḻmaiyā yiruttal.
3. viḻutal : cāyntu allatu maṭaṅki viḻutal, nilattil allatu uṭampiṟ paṭutal.
(e-ṭu) payir nilattiṟ paṭintirukkiṟatu.
caṭaināyiṉ urōmam paṭintirukkiṟatu.
mayir talaiyiṟ paṭintirukkiṟatu.
paṉṟiyiṉ vayiṟu nilattiṟ paṭikiṟatu.
4. viḻutal : oṉṟil oṉṟu viḻuntu ataṉ uruvam amaital.
paṭi (peyar) = oṉṟiloṉṟu viḻuntataṉālāṉa uruvam, ataṉ vaṭivam, aḷavu, mātiri, vakai.
paṭi = 1 a copy (e-ṭu) paṭiyōlai opposed to  mūvavōlai.
mūṭciyiṟ kiḻitta vōlai paṭiyōlai mūla vōlai.  (taṭuttāṭkoṇṭa purāṇam)
an article, uruppaṭi
2. aḷavu (potu) grade, paṭippaṭiyāy, vāyiṟpaṭi, paṭikkaṭṭu, paṭi, paṭikkācu.
niṟai - paṭikkal
mukattal - oru paṭi, paṭiyaḷakkiṟavaṉ - kākkiṟavaṉ.
paṭi = bata, allowance.
3. vitam allatu vakai.
appaṭi, ippaṭi, eppaṭi, ataṉpaṭi, coṉṉapaṭi, mēṟpaṭi.
ellāp paṭiyālum eṇṇiṉāl ivvuṭampu  (auvaiyār)
coṉṉapaṭi kēḷātavaṉ.
eṉakkoru paṭiyāy varukiṟatu. paṭiyāy - vaṭiyāy (pōlittiripu).
4. Form. paṭi + vu = paṭivu + am = paṭivam (pa-va. pōli) E. body; a.S. bodig; Hind. pataṉ.
paṭi - (n.) copy, duplicate, anything of equal value, exchange, answer, return, substitution.

kuṭumpam, il, kuṭi, kulam , varaṇam, marapu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, muṟaiyē oru ciṟiya allatu periya (oru talaimuṟaiyuḷḷa allatu pala talaimuṟai yaṭaṅkiṉa) kuṭumpattaiyum, tāyvaḻi tantaivaḻikaḷaiyum, kōttiraṅkaḷaiyum, jātiyaiyum, niṟam paṟṟiya (veṇkaḷamar, karuṅkaḷamar, allatu āriyar, tirāviṭar eṉpaṉapōṉṟa) perum pirivukaḷaiyum, kulavaḻit toṭarpai yum (descent) kuṟippaṉavākum.24 āṉāl, ipporuḷ vēṟu pāṭu iṉṟu ellārkkum pulaṉāvatillai. pacumpāl eṉpatu kāyccāta pālaiyum, paciya niṟamāṉa veḷḷāṭṭup pālaiyum kuṟippatākum25 pacuviṉ pāl pacuppāl eṉṟē kūṟat takkatu. pacuppālai āviṉpāl eṉṟaṉar muṉṉōr.

kōṭi (t@)

aṭukkiya kōṭip peyarkaḷākiya kumutam, caṅkam mutaliyaṉa tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭavaiyē. samutra eṉpatu vāraṇam eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppākum.  (va.va.)
vaṭam
vaṭam-vaṭa (t@)
vaṭṭam-vaṭam=1.  vaṭṭamākap paravum ālamaram.
vaṭaniḻaṟkaṇ ṇūṭirunta kuruvē (tāyu. karuṇā. 41).
2. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kayiṟu. kamalai (kammālai) vaṭam, tērvaṭam mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkuka.
3. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa poṟkoṭi allatu maṇikkōvai.

vaṭamoḻi
camaṟkirutattai vaṭamoḻiyeṉṟum tamiḻait teṉmoḻi yeṉṟum toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki viṭuvatāl, muṉṉatu vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu vanta ayaṉ moḻi eṉṟum, piṉṉatu mutalil iruntē teṟkiṉkaṇ vaḻaṅki vanta nāṭṭumoḻiyeṉṟum teḷḷat teḷivāka aṟiyalām.
moḻikkuc coṉṉatu moḻiyāḷarkkum okkum cilar varalāṟṟuṇmaikku māṟāka vaṭamoḻiyum muṟkālattil iruntē teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta teṉpar. avarkku vaṭamoḻi eṉṉum peyarē vāyaṭaittal kāṇka. (col. 21).
1. vaṭamoḻi: eḻuttiyal
(1) vaṇṇamāla (varṇa mālā)
vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakku allatu kuṟuṅkaṇakku varṇamālā eṉappeṟum. vaṇṇam-varṇa. mālai-mālā. varṇa, akṣara eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉ potup peyarkaḷ. ivaṟṟuṭ piṉṉatu acaiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.  (va.va.)

pākupāṭum peyarum
uyireḻuttu ac (ac) eṉṟum vara eṉṟum pūta (bhu#ta) eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ kuṟil hrava eṉṟum neṭil tīrkka (di#rgha) e eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum.
meyyeḻuttu hal eṉṟum vyañcaṉa eṉṟum peyar peṟum . avaṟṟuḷ aivarkkamum parcā (Mutes) eṉṟum, iṭaiyiṉam. antatā (antastha# : Semi-vowels) eṉṟum, iraippiṉmum mūccoliyum (Aspirate) uṣmāṇ : (Sibilants) eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
aivarkkaṅkaḷum, muṟaiyē, olipaṟṟik kavarkkam kavarga), cavarkkam (ca#varga), ṭavarkkam (t@avarga), tavarkkam (tavarga), pavarkkam (pavarga) eṉṟum; piṟappiṭampaṟṟik kaṇṭyā : (Gutturals), tālavyā : (Palatals), mūrtaṉyā : (Murdhanya#:- Linguals or Cerebrals) tantyā : (dantya# :- Dentals), ōṣṭyā : (Labials) eṉṟum, collappeṟum.
(2) oliyum piṟappum
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ; rukaram kuṟṟiyalukaram cērnta rakaramākum; rūkāram ataṉ neṭil allatu nīṭṭam. lukaram kuṟṟiya lukaram cērnta lakaram.
aṉuvāra eṉpatu muntiṉa vuyirai yaṭuttolikkum makara vakai.
visarkkam (visāka) eṉpatu āytap pōli; āyiṉ vari vaṭiviṉṟi uyirēṟap peṟuvatu; sakarattiṉ tiripāka varakkūṭiyatu.
k, kh ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉum p, ph ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉum vantu arai visarkkamāka olikkum olikku, muṟaiyē, jihvā mulīya eṉṟum upatmāṉīya (dh) eṉṟum peyar. muṉṉataṟku miṭaṟum piṉṉataṟku itaḻum piṟappiṭamām.
cilaviṭattu, uyirkaḷum ya pa la ākiya iṭaiyiṉa meykaḷum mūkkoliyuṭaṉ icaikkum. aṉṟu avai aṉunācikam eṉappaṭum.
ūṣmāṇaṅkaḷuḷ : hakarattiṟ miṭaṟum, cakarattiṟku iṭai yaṇṇamum, ṣakarattiṟku muṉṉaṇṇamum, sakarattiṟkup pallaṇṇamum, piṟappiṭamām.
ai, au eṉṉum iru neṭilum mutaṟkālattil āy, āv eṉa nīṇṭolittaṉa veṉṟum, āriyam tamiḻoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ, avai tamiḻiṟpōl ay, av eṉak kuṟuki yolik kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, kūṟuvar.
tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvāṉa piṟa eḻuttukka ḷellām, tamiḻiṟpōṉṟē olikkumeṉa aṟika.
ē, o iraṇṭum tamiḻil ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ nīṭṭamāṉa taṉi yolikaḷākak (Simple vowels) koḷḷappeṟum; camaṟkirutat til tavaṟākap puṇarolikaḷākak (Diphthongs) koḷḷap peṟu kiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku ammoḻiyil ekara okara miṉmaiyum kuṇacan tiyumē kāraṇam.
a,ā+i,ī - ē. e-ṭu : mahā + intira(ṉ) = mahēntira (ṉ)
a, ā+u, ū=o.  e-ṭu : kula + uttuṅkaṉ = kulōttuṅkaṉ
(3) eḻuttuc cāriyai
kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu civaṇi
nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyai eṉpatu tol kāppiyam (134)
uyireḻuttiṉ utaviyiṉṟit tāmāka volikkāta mey yeḻuttukkaḷai olippittaṟ poruṭṭum, tāmāka volik kum uyireḻuttukkaḷaiyum eḷitāka olippittaṟ poruṭṭum, cila tuṇaiyolikaḷaip paṇṭait tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlār amaittuḷḷaṉar. avai cāriyai eṉappeṟum.
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ kuṟiṟkuk karamum neṭiṟkuk kāramum cāriyaiyām. aikāra aukāraṅkaṭkuk kāṉ eṉpatu ciṟappuc cāriyai. itaik kuṟiṟkum meykkum payaṉpaṭuttiyatu piṟkālat tatākat terikiṉṟatu.
meyyeḻuttukkaṭku avvum akaramum cāriyai.
meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum. (tol. 46)
e-ṭu:  valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.  (tol. 19)
vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu. (kṣa. 81).
vaṭamoḻi meykaḷum tamiḻ muṟaippaṭiyē akaramoṭu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. uyirmey kaṭkuḷ kuṟiṟkuk karamum neṭiṟku meyc cāriyaiyoṭu kūṭiya uyirneṭiṟ cāriyaiyum cāriyaiyām.
e-ṭu : kakaram (ka), kakara ākāram (kā).
āytattiṟku a-kēṉam eṉpatu cāriyaiyām.
e-ṭu. aḵkēṉam. itu tolkāppiyattiṟ collappeṟa villai.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻeḻuttuc cāriyai yoḻuṅku niṟaivāka amaintuḷḷatu.
vaṭamoḻiyār karam, kāram eṉṉum iraṇṭaiyē tamiḻi ṉiṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. atōṭu kuṟiṟku iraṇṭaiyum vēṟu pāṭiṉṟi āḷvar. itaṉāṟ cāriyai yamaippiṉ tamiḻ mūlam teḷivākat terikiṉṟatu. karam, kāram eṉpaṉa vaṭamoḻiyil kara, kāra eṉṟu īṟukeṭṭu niṟkum. cāriyai eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭum vaṭa moḻiyilillai.
rakāra ḻakāram kuṟṟoṟ ṟakā.  (tol. 49).
uū kāram navavoṭu navilā.  (tol. 74)
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟaic ceyyuḷ tiripil aṭakkutal vēṇṭum.
karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻut tilavē.  (tol.. 135) 
eṉṟu vilakkiyapiṉ,
aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum. (tol. 137)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyarē muraṇpaṭak kūṟuvatāl,
varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟum kuṟṟeḻut tuṭaiya.  (tol. 136).
eṉṟuavar kuṟittiruppatu kumarināṭṭu vaḻakkoṭu porun tiyatāyt tōṉṟavillai.
(4) muṟai
mēlaiyāriya moḻikaṭkuḷ oṉṟiṟkāvatu muṟai eṉṉum eḻuttilakkaṇam iṉṟumillai. vēta āriyar eḻuttum ilakkiyamumiṉṟi intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar. vēta moḻikkum vētattiṟkum eḻutākkiḷavi eṉṟu peyar. vētattaik kuṟikkum cruti (kēḷvi) eṉṉum peyarē āriyarkku eḻuttiṉmaiyai uṇarttum.
intiyāvil maṭṭumiṉṟi ulakattilēyē mutaṉmutal neṭuṅ kaṇakku vakuttatum ataṟku muṟaiyamaittatum tamiḻarē.
vēta āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpukoṇṭa piṉṉarē, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkaip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻikkuk kiranta vaṇṇamālaiyai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar.
uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum, uyiruḷ ā ī ū muṉṉum ē ō piṉṉum, ovvōr uyirilum kuṟil muṉṉum neṭil piṉṉum, ēyiṉ piṉ aiyum ōviṉ piṉ auvum, āyta motta visarkkam uyirkkum meykkum iṭaiyum, meyyuḷ vali vakai muṉṉum meli piṉṉumākak kacaṭatapa muṟaiyilum, avaṟṟiṟkup piṉ iṭaiyiṉam yaralava muṟaiyilum, amaintiruppatu, vaṭa moḻi vaṇṇamālai tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaiyai muṟṟun taḻuviya teṉpatai, ettuṇait teḷḷat teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṉṟatu! iḵtoṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyiṉ piṉmaiyaik kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
uyir varicaiyuṭ cerukappaṭṭa ciṟappeḻuttukkaḷai nōkki ṉum, muṟṟukarattai yaṭuttuk kuṟṟukaramum, rakarak kuṟṟukarattiṉ piṉ lakarak kuṟṟukaramum vaikkappeṟṟiruppatu, ettuṇait tamiḻ muṟaiyoṭṭiyatu!
(5) aḷapu
aḷavu eṉpatu eḻuttoliyiṉ māttirai allatu aḷavu. aḷapu, māttirai eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu tamiḻc ciṟappuc col; piṉṉatu teṉmoḻikkum vaṭa moḻikkum potuc col.
meykku araiyum kuṟiṟku oṉṟum neṭiṟku iraṇṭum māttirai eṉpatu irumoḻikkum potu. vaṭamoḻiyil plutam eṉṉum aḷapeṭaiyaiyum uyir allatu uyirmey vakaikaḷuḷ oṉṟākac cērttu, ataṟku mummāttirai eṉa vakuttuḷḷaṉar.
aḷapeṭai tamiḻilum uṇṭu. āyiṉ atai oḻuṅkāṉa uyir vakaiyākak koṇṭilar. ēṉeṉiṉ, atu ulakiyal allatu iyaṟkaiyaḷapeṭaiyil mummāttirai mutal pattu allatu paṉṉiru māttiraivarai nīṇṭaḷapeṭukkum. ceyaṟkaiyākiya ceyyuḷiyal aḷapeṭai kuṟukiyolippiṉum, arukiyum tiṭṭamāṉa māttirai varampiṉyumē nikaḻum.
aḷapeṭaiyaiyum ōr uyir vakaiyākak koṇṭatu vaṭa moḻiyilakkaṇattiṉ piṉmaiyaiyē kāṭṭum.
(6) vaṭivam
vaṭamoḻikku mutaṉmutal eḻuttu ēṟpaṭṭatu tamiḻ nāṭṭil tāṉ. atu tamiḻ ēṭṭeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tirinta kiranta veḻuttu. ataṉ kālam tōrā. ki.mu. 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
aintiram eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi viyākaraṇam tolkāppiyat tiṟku muntiya nūlātalāl, vaṭamoḻik kirantaveḻuttu ataṟku ōriru nūṟṟāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
kirantam eṉpatu nūl. vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkuc conta vaḻakku moḻiyiṉmaiyāl, nūliṟku maṭṭum payaṉpayaṭuttappeṟṟa eḻuttaik kirantāṭcaram eṉṟaṉar.
iṉṟuḷḷa tēvanākari ki.pi. 4-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ karuk koṇṭu 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil muḻuniṟaivaṭaintatu. ataiyum uṟṟu nōkkiṉ, ataṟkum kirantaveḻuttiṟkumuḷḷa nuṇṇiya vaṭivoppumai pulaṉākum.
tēvamoḻi yeṉṉum vaṭamoḻikku nakaraṅkaḷil āḷap peṟṟa eḻuttu tēvanākari. tēvar nakaraṅkaḷil āḷappeṟṟatu tēva nākari eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. avar tēvareṉṟu nāṇātu kuṟippatu pirāmaṇarai.
eḻuttu		vaṭivam
aṉunācikam
jihvāmūlīya	-	(k, kh ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ)
upatmāṉīya	-	(p, ph ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ)
tamiḻk kaiyeḻuttil cila kūṭṭeḻuttukkaḷai āḷvatu pōl, vaṭamoḻi acceḻuttil ērāḷamāṉa kūṭṭeḻuttuk kaḷai amaittirukkiṉṟaṉar. avai cantiyakṣaram eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu : kṣa-kṣa, crī-srī
ivai kirantam. iṅṅaṉam tēvanākariyil ērāḷamākavuṇṭu.
meyyaik kuṟikkak kirantattil mēṟpuḷḷiyum tēvanākariyiṟ kīḻiḻuppum iṭṭatum, iruvakaiyeḻuttilum uyirmeykkut taṉi vaṭivamaittum, muṟṟum tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiyē.
(7) puṇarcci
eḻuttup puṇarcci, coṟpuṇarcci eṉap puṇarcci iru vakaippaṭum.
tamiḻilakkaṇattai mutaṉ mutal āyntu kaṇṭu nūliyaṟṟiyavar talaiciṟanta muṉivar eṉpatu,
viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (1594) viḷaṅkum.
muṉivar meypporuḷuṇarvu mutirntavarātaliṉ, ulakiluḷḷa ellāp poruḷ kaḷum uyir, uyirillatu (mey), uyiruḷḷatu (uyirmey) eṉa mūvakaiyāka irukkakkaṇṭu, avaṟṟai yotta mūvakai eḻuttolikaṭkum avaṟṟiṉ peyaraiyē uvamaiyāku peyarāka iṭṭu, uyirmeykkut taṉi vaṭivum amaittaṉar.
itaṉāl, uyirmeyyoliyai uyirum meyyumāka mutaṉ mutaṟ pakuttavar tamiḻar eṉpatum, uyirmeyyilēyē eḻuttup puṇarcci tōṉṟiviṭṭateṉpatum, teḷivām.
coṟpuṇarcci ellā moḻikaḷilumuṇṭu. āyiṉ, iyaṉ moḻiyilēyē puṇarcci oḻuṅkākavum puṇarccoṟkaḷ eḷitāyp pakukkak kūṭiyaṉavākavumirukkum. tirimoḻikaḷil taṉiccollum kūṭṭuc collum perumpālum tirintiruppa tālum, tiripup puṇarcci nikaḻavēṇṭiya viṭattum iyalpup puṇarcciyē perumpāṉmaiyāy  nikaḻvatālum, tiripup puṇarcci nikaḻntaviṭattum atu varivaṭiviṟ kāṭṭap peṟāmai yālum, avaṟṟiṟ puṇarcciyilakkaṇam vakukkap peritum iṭamillai.
camaṟkirutam tirimoḻiyāyiṉum, toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭamaiyāṉum, aintiliru pakutikkuk kuṟaiyātu tamiḻāyiruttalāṉum. tamiḻait taḻuviyē ilakkaṇañ ceyyap peṟṟamaiyāṉum, puṇarcciyilakkaṇam ataṟkamaintateṉka. āyiṉum, tirimoḻiyātaliṉ, tamiḻp puṇarccipōṟ perumpālum oḻuṅkupaṭātu ettuṇaiyō vilakkukkaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷateṉa aṟika.
vaṭamoḻip puṇarcciyum tamiḻp puṇarcci pōṉṟē uyirīṟum meyyīṟum paṟṟi iruvakaippaṭum. uyirīṟṟup puṇarcciyai acsanti allatu varasanti eṉṟum, meyyīṟṟup puṇarcciyai halsanti eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
uyirīṟṟup puṇarcciyil, kuṇam, virutti eṉṉum iruvakai uyirttiripukaḷ vaṭamoḻikkuc ciṟappāṉavai. iṉṉiṉṉa ciṟṟuyirkaṭku (Simple Vowels) iṉṉiṉṉa vuyirkaḷ allatu acaikaḷ kuṇam eṉṟum, iṉṉiṉṉa vuyirkaḷ allatu acaikaḷ virutti eṉṟum, vakukkakppaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟaik kīḻvaruṅkaṭṭamaippiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
iyaluyir	a	i,ī	u,ū	ru,rū	lu
kuṇavuyir	a	ē	ō	ar	al
viruttiyuyir	ā	ai	au	ār	āl
tīrkkasanti eṉṉum mūvakai neṭiṟpuṇarcci, kuḷāmpal (kuḷa āmpal), putūr (putu ūr) eṉṉum marūup puṇarcciyot tavaiyē.
tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉṉum muppuṇarccit tiripukaḷum, ākama(m), ātēca(m), lōpa(m)eṉamoḻipeyarkkappaṭṭuḷa.
eṇpeyar muṟaipiṟap puruvam māttirai
mutalī ṟiṭainilai pōli yoṉṟā
patampuṇar peṉappaṉ ṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē.
eṉa naṉṉūlār (57) kūṟum paṉṉiruvakai eḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kaḷuḷ, mutal īṟu iṭainilai eṉṉum mūṉṟum tirimoḻi yākiya vaṭamoḻikkillai.
(8) vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttup perukkam
kirēkkak kuṟuṅkaṇakku (24)
A (a and a#), B,G (g and n(), D, E, Z, E#, TH, I (i and i), K, L, M, N, X, O, P, R, S ( s and z), T, U (u and u#)))), PH, KH, PS, O.
ivaṟṟuḷ TH, X (ks), PH, KH, PS eṉṉum aintē puṇar meykaḷ; X (ks) vaṭamoḻiyil kṣa (ks) āka vaḷarntuḷḷatu.
ilattīṉ kuṟuṅkaṇakku (21)
A, B, C, D, E, F, Z, H, I (J), K, L, M, N, O, P, Q, R, S, T, V (U), X
ivaṟṟuḷ X (ks) oṉṟē puṇarmey. atuvum mūccoliyaṟṟatu. C=G. J=Y.
tīyūttāṉiyak kuṟuṅkaṇakku (āṅkilam)
A, B, C, D, E, F, G, H, I, J, K, M, N, O, P, Q, R, S, T, U, V, W, X, Y, Z.
 ivaṟṟuḷ X (ks) oṉṟē puṇarmey.
kirēkka arivari piṉīciyariṭattiṉiṉṟum, ilattīṉ arivari kirēkkariṭattiṉiṉṟum,  peṟappaṭṭavaiyākum.
iṉṟu airōppā muḻutum vaḻaṅkum āriyamoḻi arivarikaḷ, ilattīṉ arivariyait taḻuviyavaiyē.
āṅkilam piṟkāla moḻiyātalāl, cila piṟkāla volikaḷ iṇaivarikaḷāl (Diagraphs) kuṟikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu : ch=¢r, sh=õ.
camaṟkirutam āriyamum tiraviṭamuṅ kalanta moḻiyātaliṉ, pala mutirolikaḷaik koṇṭiruppatuṭaṉ, valliṉ aivakai yuḷḷum poli (Voiced) yoliyuṭaṉum poliyā (Voiceless) voliyuṭaṉum mūccoliyai oḻuṅkākac cērttu avaṟṟiṟkut taṉi vaṭivam amaittuḷḷatu.
kh, gh; ch, jh; t@h, dh; th, dh; ph, bh eṉṉum pattuk kūṭṭu mey kaṭkum, vaṭamoḻi yil taṉi vaṭivam amaintiruttal kāṇka. innilaimai vēṟem moḻiyilumillai.
(9) vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamālaiyiṉ piṉmai
vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamālaiyiṉ piṉmai piṉvaruñ cāṉṟu kaḷāl tuṇiyappeṟum.
(1) vaṭamoḻiyiṉ piṉmai.
(2) āriya vētam paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu eḻutāk kiḷaviyāyiruntamai.
(3) tamiḻeḻuttaip piṉpaṟṟiya kirantam tēvanākarikku muṟpaṭṭamai.
(4) oli vaṭivu, māttirai, cāriyai, varivaṭivu, muṟai mutaliya vaṟṟil iyaṉṟavarai tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiyiruttal.
(5) olipperukkam.
(6) kūṭṭu meykaṭkut taṉi vaṭivu koṇṭuḷḷamai.
aivarkkaṅkaḷum kavarkkam cavarkkam (e) eṉa mutaṉmeyyā lēyē peyarpeṟṟi ruppatāl, ammutaṉmeyyiṉ tiripē piṟpaṭṭa mūṉṟum eṉpatu peṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, tamiḻppoliyā volic coṟkaḷ tiraviṭattiṟ poli voliccoṟ kaḷākavum, āṅkilap poliyāvolic coṟkaḷ ilattīṉ kirēkkattiṟ polivolic coṟ kaḷākavum, tirintiruttalaiyum nōkkit teḷika.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyaivarukka mutaṉmeykaḷum tamiḻ valli ṉattiṉ valitta vaṭivē yeṉpataiyum kaṇṭaṟika.
(7) mēlaiyāriyampōṟ kuṟuṅkaṇakku maṭṭuṅ koṇṭirātu tamiḻpōl neṭuṅkaṇakkuṅ koṇṭiruttal.  (va.va.)
(10) vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamālaiyiṉ oḻuṅkiṉmai
(1) ru, rū, lu eṉṉum uyirmeykaḷ uyiriṉattiṟ cērkkap paṭṭiruttal
(2) tēvanākariyiṟ cila eḻuttukkaṭku oṉṟōṭoṉṟai mayakkaṟkēṟṟavāṟu vaṭivamaintiruttal.
(3) makaravoliyākiya aṉuvārakkuṟi melliṉap potuvā yiruttal.
(11) vaṭamoḻik kuṟuṅkaṇakku nūṟpākkaḷ
1. "a i u ṇ," 2. "ruluk," 3. "ēōṅ."  4. "aioḷac" (na). 5. "hayavaraṭ"  (va@). 6. "laṇ." 7. "ñamaṅaṇanam" 8. " tuḻaha kṣaḻaha ñ."  9. " ḻuḻaha ṉuḻaha ṉuḻaha ṣ." 19. "ja kṣaha  ḻuha  ṉuha  ṉuha c" 11. "muḻaha  ñaḻaha ūḻaha kūḻaha kūḻaha ūha kū@ha kūha v"  12. " muha ñaha y." 13. "caṣasar." 
14. "hal."
	ippatiṉāl nūṟpākkaṭkum makēcuvara cūttiraṅkaḷ allatu civacūttiraṅkaḷ eṉṟu peyar. ivai pāṇiṉīyattoṭu potu vākac cērttuk kūṟappaṭiṉum, civaperumāṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa vaiyeṉṟum, avar tuṭiyaip patiṉāṉmuṟai iyakka ippati ṉāl olittokutikaḷum tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi yāḷar kūṟuvar. vaṭamoḻiyaiyē tēvamoḻi yeṉpār iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟuvatil viyappoṉṟumillai. āyiṉum tuṇicca lāṉa ēmāṟṟē.
aṣṭātyāyī eṉṉum pāṇiṉiyilakkaṇattiṉ mūlap pāṭattil ivai cērkkap peṟāmaiyālum,  pāṇiṉīya nūṟ pākkaḷ pōl atikāraviyal eṇ peṟāmaiyālum, naṉṉūṟ potup pāyira nūṟpākkaḷpōl muṉṉarē palarāṟ ceyyap paṭṭu vaḻivaḻi tirunti vantavaiyāyumirukkalām.
innūṟpākkaḷiṉ iṟutiyeḻuttukkaṭku it eṉṟu peyar. oru nūṟpāviṉ mutaleḻuttaiyum ataṉpiṉ varum orittai yum cērppiṉ pratyākāra eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭākum. akkuṟiyīṭu. ataṉ mutaleḻuttaiyum, ataṉ īreḻuttiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa ittallā ellā nūṟpā eḻuttukkaḷaiyum kuṟikka utavum.
immuṟaiyil, aṇ eṉpatu akarattaiyum akara ikara vukaraṅ kaḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. ac eṉpatu ellā uyirkaḷaiyum, hal eṉpatu ellā meykaḷaiyum, al eṉpatu ellā uyir kaḷaiyum meykaḷaiyum kuṟikkum.
itaṉāl, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉāl nūṟpākkaṭkum pirattiyākāra cūttiraṅkaḷ eṉṟum peyar.
2. vaṭamoḻi: colliyal
ellā moḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇavakaic coṟkaḷ peyarviṉai iṭai eṉa mūṉṟē. ivai cikṣai, niruktam eṉṉum toṭakka vaṭa nūlkaḷil, nāma (m), ākyāt (m), nipāt (m) eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. pāṇiṉi ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē cupantam, tiṅantam avyaya(m) eṉak kuṟittār.
upasarkkam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu nipātattuḷ aṭaṅkum.
āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉpē, nāmam eṉṉuñ col mēlaiyāriyattil namon, nomem eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyatākat terivatālum, tiyūttāṉiyattil viṉaiyākavum vaḻaṅkivaruvata ṉālum, oru kāl atu mēlaiyāriyac collāka iruppiṉum irukkalām. ākyāta (kh) eṉṉumcol, col eṉṟē poruḷ paṭuvatu. itu poruḷilum āṭciyilum verb (L. verbum) eṉṉum āṅkilac collaiyottatu. kiyā=col. verb=brhš. nipāta eṉpatu nipat eṉṉam kūṭṭu mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu tirintatu. ni=kīḻ. pat=viḻu. nipāta=neṟi tiṟampiyatu, oḻuṅkaṟṟu. paṭi (ta.) - pat.
vēṟṟumai yurupēṟṟa peyarum kālaveṇṇiṭaṅ kāṭṭum īṟēṟṟa viṉaiyum patam eṉappaṭum.
(11) peyarccol (supanta)
tātu eṉappaṭum mutaṉilaiyoṭu, kirut eṉappaṭum peyaraṭi yiṭaiyoṭṭum tattitaṉ eṉappaṭum vaḻinilai yiṭai yoṭṭum cērnta vaṭiviṟkup pirakiruti allatu pirātipatikam eṉṟu peyar. ippirātipatikattuṭaṉ vēṟṟumai yurupu cēriṉ peyarppatamām. atu cupantam eṉappaṭum. cup (sup) eṉpatu vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷaiyellām potuvākak kuṟikkum pāṇiṉīyak kuṟiyīṭu. antam īṟu.
kirut īṟu kirutantam eṉṟum. tattita īṟu tattitāntam eṉṟum collappaṭum. ivaiyiraṇṭiṟkum piratyam eṉpatu vaṭa moḻip potuppeyar.
pirātipatikattilum peyarppatattilum krut, tattitaṉ ākiya iruvakaiyiṭai yoṭṭum cērntumirukkalām; avviraṇṭi loṉṟu taṉittuyirukkalām.
pāl (liṅka)
vaṭamoḻiyil tiṇaiyillai; pālmaṭṭum uṇṭu. atu pulliṅkam (āṇpāl), tirīliṅkam (peṇpāl), napumsaka liṅkam (alippāl), eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
vaṭamoḻip pālvakuppu iyaṟkaiyāṉa poruḷiyalpu paṟṟiya taṉṟu; perumpālum ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkaṇa muṟai paṟṟiyatu. cila peyarkaḷē uṇmaiyāṉa pālkāṭṭum.
e-ṭu : putakam (ā. gh.), cilā (pe.pā.) = kal.
maṉaiviyaik kuṟikkum tāram (ā. pā) pāriyā (pe. gh.), kaḷattiram (a. pā.) eṉṉum muccoṟkaḷum, pāl vēṟupaṭum.
eṇ (vacaṉa)
vaṭamoḻiyil, ēkavacaṉam (orumai), tvivacaṉam (irumai), pahu vacaṉam (paṉmai) eṉa eṇ mūvakaippaṭum.
mēlaiyāriyattaic cērnta tiyūttāṉiyattilum ilattīṉilum irumaiyeṇ illai; camaṟkirutattiṟku mika neruṅkiya kirēkkattil tāṉ uṇṭu.
iṭam (puruṣa)
iṭam tamiḻiṟ pōṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyilum mūṉṟām.
avai uttama puruṣa (taṉmai), matyam puruṣa (muṉṉilai), pratamapuruṣa (paṭarkai) eṉappaṭum.
vēṟṟumai (vipakti)
kirēkka ilakkaṇattil, eḻuvāy vēṟṟumai viḷivēṟṟumai, ceyporuḷ vēṟṟumai, kiḻamai vēṟṟumai, koṭai vēṟ ṟumai eṉṉum aivēṟṟumaikaḷum, ilattīṉ ilakkaṇattil eḻuvāyvēṟṟumai, kiḻamaivēṟṟumai, koṭai vēṟṟumai, ceyporuḷ vēṟṟumai, viḷivēṟṟumai, nīkkavēṟṟumai, eṉṉum aṟuvēṟṟumaikaḷumē, kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. mutaṟkāla āṅkilattil eḻuvāy, viḷi, ceyporuḷ, kiḻamai, koṭai, karuvi eṉṉum aṟuvēṟṟumaikaḷ iruntatākac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. āyiṉ mēlaiyāriya moḻi oṉṟilāvatu eṇ vēṟṟumai collappaṭavillai.
vaṭamoḻiyilō tamiḻiṟpōl eṇvēṟṟumai kāṭṭap peṟuvatuṭaṉ, avaṟṟiṉ varicaiyum peyarum poruḷum muṟṟum tamiḻai ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.

iṅṅaṉam muṟṟum ottiruppatāl, vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamālai pōṉṟē vaṭamoḻi vēṟṟumaiyamaippum tamiḻait taḻuviya teṉpatu veḷḷiṭaimalai. vipakti eṉṉum collē viḷ-paku eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa vi-paj eṉṉum kūṭṭumutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu tirintu vēṟu pirippu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum moḻipeyarp pākum.
tamiḻil eṇvēṟṟumaiyum eṇvaricaiyālmaṭṭumiṉṟi, eḻuvāy, ceyporuḷ, karuvi, koṭai, nīkkam, kiḻamai, iṭam, viḷi eṉap poruḷpaṟṟiyum; peyar, ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ, viḷi eṉa urupupaṟṟiyum; peyarpeṟum.
vaṭamoḻiyil eṭṭām vēṟṟumai eṇṇāṟ peyarpeṟā tiruppatum kavaṉikkat takkatām.
tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻiyātalāl atil mutal vēṟṟumaikku urupillai; camaṟkirutam tirimoḻiyātaliṉ, ataṟku aḵtuṇṭu.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ īṟṟiṟ kēṟpa vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ ciṟitum peritum vēṟupaṭum. iṅku akaravīṟoṉṟu kāṭṭappeṟum.

(2) viṉaiccol (tiṅanta)
tātu eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyoṭu kālaveṇṇiṭaṅ kāṭṭum īṟu cēriṉ viṉaippa tamām. atu tiṅantam eṉappaṭum. tiṅ eṉpatu viṉaiyīṟukaḷaiyellām potuppaṭak kuṟikkum pāṇiṉiyak kuṟiyīṭu.
camaṟkiruta viṉaikaḷ, āṟukālaṅkaḷum (Tenses) nāṉku paṭicu kaḷum (Moods) mūṉṟu uṟavukaḷum (Voices) koḷḷum. kālam kāla eṉṟum, paṭicu artta eṉṟum, uṟavu prayōka eṉṟum, peyar peṟum.
āṟukālaṅkaḷum nāṉku paṭicukaḷum lakkaraṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil ellā viṉai vakaikaṭkum iṭukuṟiyāṉa kuṟiyīṭuṇṭu.

camaṟkiruta viṉaimuṟṟīṟukaḷ, taṟporuṭṭuviṉai (ātmaṉē pata) īṟukaḷ eṉṟum, maṟporuṭṭuviṉai (paramaipata) īṟukaḷ eṉṟum, iruvakaippaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila viṉaikaḷ taṟporuṭṭu viṉaiyīṟukaḷaiyum, cila viṉaikaḷ maṟporuṭṭu viṉaiyīṟu kaḷaiyum, cila viṉaikaḷ iraṇṭaiyum ēṟkum. muṉṉoṭṭuc cērkkaiyāl, ta. po. viṉai ma. po. viṉaiyākavum, ma. po. viṉai ta. po. viṉaiyākavum māṟuvatuṇṭu.
ta.po. viṉaippayaṉ ceyvāṉaiyum, ma.po. viṉaippayaṉ piṟaṉaiyuñ cārum.
e-ṭu : 
tēvatatta : yajatē (t@)= tēvatattaṉ taṉakku vēṭkiṟāṉ.
tēvatatta : yajati (t@) = tēvatattaṉ piṟaṉukku vēṭkiṟāṉ. vēṭṭal vēḷvi ceytal.
ellā viṉaikaḷum pattu lakkaraṅkaḷilum mūviṭattum mūveṇṇilum puṭaipeyarum.
nikaḻkālam, iṉṟalā iṟantakālam, ēvalviṉai, āṟṟal viṉai ākiya nālilakkaraṅkaḷilum, viṉaikaḷ cila vēṟupāṭaṭai kiṉṟaṉa. avvēṟupāṭu vikaraṇa(m) eṉappaṭum. vēṟupāṭaṭai yum lakkaraṅkaḷ sārvatātuka eṉṟum, vēṟu pāṭaṭaiyā lakkaraṅkaḷ artta tātuka eṉṟum, collappaṭum.
vikaraṇampaṟṟi viṉaikaḷ pattuk kaṇaṅkaḷāka vakukkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ippattuk kaṇaṅkaḷum mīṇṭum iru tokuti kaḷākap pakukkappaṭṭuḷa. mutalām iraṇṭām mūṉṟām pattāṅ kaṇaṅkaḷ mutal tokutiyum, ēṉaiya iraṇṭām tokutiyum ākum. mutal tokuti viṉaiyaṭikaḷ akarattil iṟṟu eṉṟun tiriyātu niṟkum; iraṇṭān tokuti viṉaiyaṭikaḷ akarattil iṟātu tirintuvarum.
vaṭamoḻic ceyappāṭṭiṟantakāla viṉaiyeccavīṟākiya `ta', tamiḻiṟantakāla viṉaiyecca vīṟākiya tuvvai orupuṭai yottatu.

cuṭṭum viṉāvum
cuṭṭum viṉāvum, āriyamum cēmiyamumāṉa piṟamoḻikaḷilel lām coṟkaḷākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻilō avai coṟ kaḷāka maṭṭumiṉṟic coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. avveḻuttukkaḷ uṇmaiyil ōreḻuttuc coṟkaḷē.
neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi.
kuṟṟeḻut taintum moḻiniṟai pilavē.
āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
ai uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu.
āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉāa.
evvayiṟ peyarum veḷippaṭat tōṉṟi.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (43, 44, 301, 31, 32, 552) kūṟuvatālum, cuṭṭum viṉāvum iṉṟum taṉi neṭilākat tiraviṭattilum vaṭatiraviṭak kāṉ muḷaiyākiya intiyilum vaḻaṅkuvatālum, kuṟil neṭil palukka veḷitā yiruppataṉālum, āṇṭu īṇṭu yāṇṭu (ēṇṭu) eṉṉuñ coṟkaṭku mutal kuṟukiya vaṭiviṉ maiyālum, cuṭṭu viṉāveḻuttukkaḷ mutaṟkālattil nīṇṭē yiruntu piṉṉark kuṟukiyamai aṟiyappaṭum.
cuṭṭaṭikaḷum viṉāvaṭikaḷum taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷā yiruppata ṉāl, avaṟṟai iṭaic colleṉṟu koṇṭaṉar muṉṉōr. avai taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷāyiṉum, poruḷuṇarttuva tāṟ col lāyum palaveḻuttuc coṟpōṉṟē  poruḷ  nirampiyum, iruppatai aṟika.
āvūr, avvūr, antavūr eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṉṟē. a = anta 
(that) That eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟpōṉṟē, a, anta eṉpaṉavum cuṭṭup peyareccam (Demonstrative Adjective) eṉa aṟika. peyarait taḻuvuvatellām peyareccamē. atu terinilai viṉaiyākavu mirukkalām; kuṟippu viṉaiyākavu mirukkalām; piṟavāṟum irukkalām.
iyalpāṉa cuṭṭukkaḷ cēymai, aṇmai, muṉmai eṉa mūṉṟē. ākavē, avaṟṟaik kuṟikkum olikaḷ allatu eḻuttukkaḷum ā (a). ī. (i), ū (u) eṉṉum mūṉṟē.
ivai kumarikkaṇṭattil tamiḻt tōṟṟak kālattil kaic cuṭṭukkaḷukkup pakaramāka (patilāka) vāyccuṭṭukkaḷākat tōṉṟiya  olikaḷ, immūvolikaḷaṉṟi vēṟevvolikaḷum ammūviṭattaiyum cuṭṭāmaiyai olittuk kāṇka.
muccuṭṭum mutaṉmutal tamiḻil tōṉṟiyataṉālēyē, avai tamiḻilum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa tiraviṭattilum iṉṟum taṉiyeḻuttu vaṭiviliruppatuṭaṉ, mūviṭattaiyum oḻuṅkākac cuṭṭavum ceykiṉṟaṉa. iḵtoṉṟē tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlam eṉṉum uṇmaiyaik kāṭṭavum nāṭṭavum pōtiya cāṉṟām.
vaṭamoḻi tirimoḻi yātaliṉ, atiṟcuṭṭeḻuttillā tiruppatoṭu, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ iṭamāṟiyuñ cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu : atra=iṅkē. atya=iṉṟaikku. atunā=ippōtu. cila akarac cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ cēymai yaṇmaiyākiya īriṭattaiyuñ cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu : ata=atu, itu.
cila ikarac cuṭṭukkaḷ ēkāramākat tirintuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu : ētat=itu, ēvam=ippaṭi.
cila cuṭṭukkaḷiṉ cārpu meyyāṉa takaram sakaramākat tirintuḷḷatu.

3. vaṭamoḻi: toṭariyal
(1) tokaic col (samāsa)
vētamoḻiyiṉ mūlamākiya mēlaiyāriya muṟaippaṭi camaṟkirutac coṟṟoṭaramaippu oru maruṅku vēṟupaṭṭi ruppiṉum, vēta moḻiyiṉ vaṭatiraviṭa allatu pirākirutak kalappiṉālum, camaṟ kirutattiṉ tamiḻt toṭarpiṉālum, atu oru maruṅku tamiḻaiyum taḻuviyuḷḷatu.
camācam eṉṉum camaṟkirutak kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ allatu tokaic coṟkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu aivakaippaṭum.
(1) tuvantuvam (tvantva)  ummait tokai
(2) taṟpuruṭaṉ (tatpuruṣa)  vēṟṟumait tokai
(3)karumatārayaṉ (karumatāraya) paṇputtokai (irupeyaroṭṭu)
(4) pakuvirīki (pakuvrīhi) aṉmoḻit tokai.
(5) avviyayīpavām (avyīpāva) iṭaic colliyal tokai allatu viṉaiyeccaviyal tokai.
samāsa eṉṉum collai, sam + a2 eṉṟu pakuppar. sam=kūṭa. a=eṟi. samāsa=(irucollai) uṭaṉiṭṭatu.
karumatārayaṉaip pala ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ taṟpuruṭaṉil aṭakkik  kūṟum. atu poruntātu. eṇṇup paṇput tokaiyait taṉiyāyp pirittut tviku eṉṟu peyar koṭuppar.
potuvāka nōkkiṉ, vaṭamoḻic camācap pakuppiṉum tamiḻt tokai toṭarp pakuppu mikac ciṟantatu.
aṭukkut toṭar (vīpsā)
tamiḻiṟ  pōṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyilum coṟkaḷ aṭukkivaralām.
e-ṭu : vrukṣm vrukṣm siñcati=maram maramāyt taṇṇīr ūṟṟukiṟāṉ.
(2) coṟṟoṭar (vākya)
vaṭamoḻic coṟṟoṭarc colvaricai perumpālum tamiḻ muṟaiyai ottatē.
eḻuvāy muṉṉum ceyappaṭuporuḷ iṭaiyum payaṉilai. piṉṉum varuvatē iyalpāṉa muṟai.
pālaka : krantam paṭati=paiyaṉ pottakattaip paṭikkiṟāṉ. [ th ïnk ghyfh th M«u« ¡UàzªJ.=mtdhtJ intap paiyaṉkaḷāvatu māmpaḻattai eṭuttuk koḷḷaṭṭum.
coṟṟoṭariṉ mūvuṟuppum aṭaimoḻiyoṭum varum. aṭaimoḻi potuvāka aṭaikoḷikku muṉniṟkum.
e-ṭu : rūpavati trī = aḻakāṉa peṇ.
apyakāryasatam krutvā parttavyā=nūṟu tīviṉaikaḷaic ceytum kākkappaṭavēṇṭum.
iru, uṇṭu eṉṉum iṇaippu viṉaiyiṉṟip peyarum payaṉilaiyāka varuvatuṇṭu.
e-ṭu : simha : cvāpatarāja : = arimā vilaṅkaracu.
ēṣa mē niccaya : = itu eṉ tīrmāṉam.
iṉi, coṟṟoṭariṉ mūvuṟuppukkaḷum, ētēṉum oru payaṉ nōkki, poruḷ māṟā vakaiyil mēṟkuṟitta muṟai māṟiyum varum.
maṇīm cōrayati tēna : = maṇiyait tiruṭukiṟāṉ tiruṭaṉ. iṉi, kūṭṭuk kiḷaviyap payilvu, neṭuntoṭar yāppu, cārpuk kiḷaviyaṅkaṭkup pakaramāka eccaviṉai varal, muṟṟuviṉaik kīṭāka iṟantakāla viṉaiyecca āṭci, nēral kūṟṟiṉmai mutaliya kūṟukaḷilum, vaṭamoḻic coṟṟoṭaramaippu tamiḻait taḻuviyatē.
vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇam eḻuttu, col, coṟṟoṭar eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu muṭikiṟatu. ceyyuḷilakkaṇam canta eṉṟum, aṇi yilakkaṇam alaṅkāra cātiram eṉṟum vēṟu nūlkaḷpōl vevvēṟākak kūṟappaṭum.
tamiḻil vaṭamoḻiyillāp poruḷilakkaṇa muṇmaiyālum, paṇṭai yilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivi liruntamaiyā lum, collaiyaṭutta moḻiyaṟuppu poruḷeṉṟu koṇṭu, ataṉuḷḷē ceyyuḷaiyum uṭampiṉ iyaṟkaiyaḻaku pōṟ ceyyuḷilamain tirukkum aṇiyaiyum aṭakki viṭṭaṉar, meyp poruḷiyal muṟṟat tuṟaipōya mētaku paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlōr.
naṉṉūl, ciṉṉūl mutaliya teṉṉūlum eḻuttuñ collum coṟṟoṭarumē kūṟukiṉṟaṉavēyeṉiṉ, avai vaṭanūṉ muṟai taḻuviya piṉṉūleṉṟum, tolkāppiyampōl niṟai vāṉa piṇṭa nūlākātu kuṟaivāṉa tuṇṭa nūlē yeṉṟum, kūṟi viṭukka.
vaṭamoḻi eṉpatu orukālum pēcappaṭāta nūṉmoḻiyē yātaliṉ,  ataṉ varalāṟeṉpatu uṇmaiyil ilakkiya varalāṟē.  (va.va.)
vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum vēṟupāṭu
1.	eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tokai (uṇmaiyāṉapaṭi) vaṭa moḻiyil 42 : teṉmoḻiyil 30.
2.	vaṭamoḻiyolikaḷ valiyaṉa; teṉmoḻiyolikaḷ meliyaṉa.
3.	mūccolikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil maṭṭumuṇṭu.
4.	kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ teṉmoḻiyil illai.
5.	vaṭamoḻiyil uyirkaḷ (tīrkkam, kuṇam, viruttiyeṉa mūvakaiyil) neṭilāka māṟip puṇarum; teṉmoḻiyil uṭaṉpaṭumey peṟṟup puṇarum.
6.	tamiḻil moḻi mutal iṭai kaṭai varāta eḻuttukaḷellām, vaṭamoḻiyil moḻi mutaliṭai kaṭai varum.
7.	iṭukuṟippeyar tamiḻukkillai.
8.	muṉṉilaiyai uḷappaṭuttum taṉmaip paṉmaip peyar vaṭamoḻiyilillai.
9.	uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai eṉṟa pākupāṭu vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
10.	pālkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil āṇpāl, peṇpāl, alippāl eṉa mūṉṟu; avai īṟu paṟṟiyaṉa; tamiḻil āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉap pāl aintu; avai poruḷum eṇṇum paṟṟiyaṉa.
11.	irumai eṉṉum eṇ tamiḻil illai.
12.	mutal vēṟṟumaikku urupu vaṭamoḻiyi luṇṭu; teṉmoḻiyil illai.
13.	kuṟippuviṉai vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
14.	vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peyareccamum peyar pōla vēṟṟumaiyēṟkum.
15.	vaṭamoḻiyil varum muṉṉoṭṭuc (Prefix) coṟkaḷ tamiḻil piṉṉoṭṭuc (Suffix) coṟkaḷāyirukkum.
16.	taḻuvuñ collum nilaimoḻiyum  vaṭamoḻiyil varu moḻiyā yiruppatuṇṭu. tamiḻil vaḻuvamaitiyāyum aruki yumē aṅṅaṉam varum.
17.	viṉaittokai vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
18.	tamiḻil mika mukkiyamākak karutappaṭum poruḷilakkaṇam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
19.	veṇpā, āciriyappā mutaliya pākkaḷum ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu kaḷum iṉaṅkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
20.	iyal icai nāṭakameṉat tamiḻai mūṉṟākap pakuppatu pōla vaṭamoḻiyaip pakuppatillai.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻi mēṉāṭṭariya moḻikaḷaic cērntateṉ pataik kīḻvarum coṟkaḷāl uṇarka.

6. `iru' eṉṉum viṉaiccol

mēṉāṭṭāriya moḻikaṭkum vaṭamoḻikkum coṟkaḷil oppumai yiruppatupōlavē, ilakkaṇa neṟimuṟaikaḷilum oppumai yirukkiṉṟatu.
pārppaṉar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vanta putitil, tamiḻaic cariyāyp pēcamuṭiyavillai. ippōtuḷḷa mēṉāṭṭāriṉ tamiḻp pēccup pōṉṟē, akkālap pārppaṉar pēccum nakaippai viḷaivittatu. ataṉāl, tolkāppiya meyppāṭṭiyal 
(1-ām cū.) uraiyil, āriyar kūṟum tamiḻai nakaic cuvaikkuk kāṭṭāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. kapilar, nacci ṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya tamiḻaṟiñar palar āriyap pārppaṉa rēṉum, avar oru ciṟu tokaiyiṉarē yāvar. perumpāṟpaṭṭa pārppaṉar putitāy vantavarāyum tamiḻaic cariyākak kallātavarāyum iruntamaiyiṉ, tamiḻaic cevvaiyāyap pēcamuṭivillai. mēṉāṭṭāruḷ peki, kālṭuvel, pōp, uviṉlō mutaliya pala ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñariruntataiyum, piṟarukku avaraippōl tamiḻaic cevvaiyāyp pēca muṭiyāmaiyaiyum mēṟkūṟiyatuṭaṉ oppiṭṭuk kāṇka.
tamiḻarkkum, vaṭamoḻi ataṉ ceyaṟkaiyoli mikutipaṟṟi naṉṟāyk kaṟka varāmaiyiṉ, "pārppāṉ tamiḻum vēḷāḷaṉ kirantamum vaḻavaḻa" eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu. itaṉālum, pārppaṉarum tamiḻarum vēṟāṉavar eṉpatai yaṟiyalām.
āriyaṉ kaṇṭāy tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭāy.  (tēvā. 844:5)
eṉṟu kūṟiyatum ik karuttuppaṟṟiyē.
kulavaḻakku : pārppaṉar ippōtu naṉṟāyt tamiḻaṟintavarākak, karutappaṭiṉum, vaṭamoḻippaṟṟuk kāraṇamāka, iyaṉṟavarai vaṭacoṟkaḷaik kalantu maṇippavaḷa naṭaiyiṟ pēcuvatē vaḻakkam, cāppāṭu, kāraṇamāka, aṭaimāṉam, taṇṇīr, tiru viḻā, kuṭiyiruppu, kaliyāṇam mutaliya coṟkaḷukkup patilāka, muṟaiyē pōjaṉam, viyājamāka, pōkkiyam, tīrttam, utcavam, vācam, vivākam mutaliya coṟkaḷaiyē vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarka ḷiṭattiṉiṉṟē tamiḻarum pala vaṭacoṟkaḷaik kaṟṟut tam pēccil vēṇṭātu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilac coṟ kaḷait tamiḻiṟ kalantu pēcuvatu uyarveṉṟu tavaṟāy ippōtu eṇṇappaṭuvatu pōlavē, vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷaik kalantu pēcuvatum muṟkālattil uyarvā yeṇṇappaṭṭatu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam pārppaṉat talaimaiyē.
ammāmi, ammāñci (ammāṉcēy), attimpēr (attiyaṉpar) mutaliya muṟaippeyar vaṭivaṅkaḷum, cāsaṉam (paṭṭayam) tīrttam mutaliya poruṭpeyarkaḷum, avā pōyppaṭṭā, nēkku, pōṭṭuṇṭu, colṟēḷ (avarkaḷ pōyviṭṭārkaḷ, eṉakku, pōṭṭukkoṇṭu, colkiṟīrkaḷ) eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa koccai vaḻakkum, āṭavar peṇṭiruḷ mūttāraiyum aṭī eṉṟa viḷippatum, pārppaṉat tamiḻp pēcciṟku oru taṉittaṉmai yūṭṭuvaṉavākum.
pārppaṉar cila taṉiccoṟkaḷāl taṅkaḷaiyum taṅkaḷ poruḷkaḷaiyum pirittuk kāṭṭuvatumuṇṭu.

pārppaṉar tamiḻppaṟṟiṉmai, taṉittamiḻai virumpātati ṉālum, yūṉivarciṭṭi, kūl paiṉal eṉṟu āṅkilac coṟ kaḷai moḻipeyarkkātu vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālum aṟiyappaṭum.
(5) uṟaiyuḷ : 
pārppaṉar eṅkiruntālum tamiḻaroṭu kalavātu taṉittē uṟaikiṉṟaṉar. akkirakārattil iṭam allatu vacatiyillāta poḻutē tamiḻar naṭuvil vantu kuṭiyiruppar. aṅṅaṉamiruppa varum perumpālum āṅkilam kaṟṟavarāyum aluvalāḷa rāyumē yiruppar. akkirakārattil ekkāraṇattaiyiṭṭum tamiḻarukkuk kuṭiyirukka iṭantaruvatillai. palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ cuṭukāṭu kūṭap pārppaṉarukkut taṉiyāka vuḷatu.
(6) oḻukkam :
pārppaṉar tamiḻa muṟaippaṭi, viruntōmpal, naṭuvunilaimai mutaliya aṟaṅkaḷ iṉmaiyāl illaṟattārumallar; kol lāmai, tuṟavu mutaliya aṟaṅkaḷiṉmaiyil tuṟavaṟat tārumallar.
iruntōmpi yilvāḻva tellām viruntōmpi
vēḷāṇmai ceytaṟ poruṭṭu.  (kuṟaḷ. 81)
oruvar tam kulattārukku maṭṭuma viruntu ceyvatu virun tōmpalākātu.
vēḷviyil uyiraik kolvatum kolaiyē.
ellārkkum potuvāṉa aṟaccālaikaḷiṟkūṭap pārppaṉar uṇṭapiṟakē tamiḻar uṇṇavēṇṭu meṉṟa ēṟpāṭu iṉṟum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām Turanian, Semitic, Aryan eṉa mup perum pirivukaḷā yaṭaṅkum. avaṟṟuḷ āriyak kavaiyil Teutonic kiḷaikkut talaimaiyāṉatu vaṭamoḻi yeṉṉum camaṟkirutam; ēṉaiya German, Dutch, English mutaliyaṉa.
mēṉāṭṭu moḻiyārāyccip pulavar, ilakkaṇa oṟṟumai yālum ilakkiyac coṟkaḷiṉ  oṟṟumaiyālum vaṭamoḻiyaic cerumaṉ moḻikku iṉamākak kaṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, intiya āriyarum ceru māṉiyarum oru kālattil orē vakup piṉarāy orēyiṭattil (mēlāciyā) vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum. carittira nūlkaḷellām perumpālum kiṟittu vukkup piṟpaṭṭaṉavātaliṉ, avarkku muṟpaṭṭa varalāṟukaḷaiyel lām uṇartaṟku moḻinūlē ciṟanta karuviyākkiṉṟatu. oru vakuppārai iṉṉāreṉa uṇartaṟku avaratu pēcciṉum ciṟanta karuvi piṟitoṉṟillai.
nilattiṟ kiṭantamai kālkāṭṭuṅ kāṭṭuṅ
kulattiṟ piṟantārvāyc col.  (kuṟaḷ. 959)
ākavē, intiya āriyar cerumāṉiyarkku iṉameṉṟum avar pēciya moḻiyum cerumaṉukku iṉameṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum. atai avviru moḻikaṭkum iṉṟiyamaiyāta cila potuc coṟkaḷāṟ kāṭṭutum. iṅkilīṣ cerumaṉ moḻiyiṉiṉṟum piṟantatātaliṉ, kīḻvarum iṅkilīṣ coṟkaḷellām cerumaṉeṉṟē teḷika. (Jutes, Saxons, Angles eṉṟa mūvakaic cerumāṉiya vakuppiṉarē ki.mu. 449-l cerumaṉiyiṉiṉṟum pirittāṉiyāvukkup pōy iṅkilīṣ moḻiyai vaḻaṅkiṉavar.)

iṉi ilakkaṇa oṟṟumaikaḷāvaṉa :
1. mey moḻi mutalātal
2. orumai irumai paṉmai eṉa eṇ mūṉṟātal
3. peyareccaṅkaḷ pāl eṇ vēṟṟumaikaḷaik kāṭṭal
4. ilakkaṇap pāl (grammatical gender)
5. viṉaimuṟṟukaḷ pāl kāṭṭāmai
6. payaṉilai viṉaiccollāyē yiruttal
ivai pōṉṟavai iṉṉum ettuṇaiyō uḷa. mukkiya māṉavai maṭṭum iṅku utāraṇamākak kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āriyam eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu mutaṉmutal āriya moḻikaḷ ellā vaṟṟiṟkum potuvāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. piṉpu Teutonic vakuppukku maṭṭuma vaḻaṅkat talaippaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉpu intiya āriya moḻiyākiya camaṟkirutattiṟkē ciṟappāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. intiya āriyar nāl vakuppārāyiṉum, avarkaḷukkuḷ pirāmaṇar maṭṭum ik kuṟiyīṭṭait tamakkum tam moḻikkum varai yaṟuttukkoṇṭaṉar.
āriya vakuppu moḻikaḷuḷ Latin, Greek eṉṟa iraṇṭum muṟpaṭṭavai. Teutonic moḻikaḷ avaṟṟiṟku piṟpaṭṭavai. airōppiya aracukaṭkellām muntiya kirēkka carittiram ki.mu. 3000ām āṇṭiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ākavē, camaṟkiruta āriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉatum atē kālamāyttā ṉirukkavēṇṭum.
ulakil mutaṉmutal uṇṭāṉatu tamiḻ. tamiḻar (ki.mu. 8000) kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟum vaṭakkē pōyc ciṉṉa āciyāvil kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpatu pāpilōṉiyak kukai veṭṭālum kuḻi veṭṭālum teriya varukiṉṟatu. aṅkētāṉ tamiḻ cēmiyak (Semitic) kiḷaiyākat tirintatu. epirēyam (Hebrew), arapi (Arabic) mutaliyaṉa cēmiyak kiḷai moḻikaḷ. avaṟṟaiyaṭuttu Latin, Greek mutaliya āriyak kiḷai moḻikaḷ piṟantaṉa.  oru tāy moḻiyiṉiṉṟum oru kiḷaimoḻi uṇṭāyiruppiṉ ataṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta mutaṟ coṟkaḷellām tāymoḻiyā yirukkum. ēṉaiyavellām ataṟkac contamāṉa ākkac coṟkaḷē. avaṟṟuḷ oru pakuti piṟamoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉ koṇṭa ticaiccoṟkaḷāyirukkum. oru moḻiyiṉ ākkac coṟ perukkattiṟkum curukkattiṟkum kāraṇam am moḻiyai vaḻaṅkum makkaḷaṟivē yākum.
āriyak kiḷaikaḷil oṉṟākiya Teutonic eṉpatu Greko-Latin eṉṟa peruṅkiḷaiyiṉiṉṟum kiḷaittapoḻutē (atāvatu camaṟ kiruta āriyar intiyāvukku varumuṉṉamē) atil nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kalantiruntaṉa. āriyarkaḷ intiyāvukku vantu tamiḻarōṭu (ki.mu.2500) kalantapiṉ eṇṇiṟanta teṉ coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalantaṉa. āriyar varumuṉṉum vanta poḻutum vaṭaintiyāvil vaḻaṅkivantavai yellām koṭun tamiḻ moḻikaḷē.
āriya varukaikku muṉ camaṟkirutattil allatu Teutonic moḻi kaḷiṟ kalantirunta teṉcoṟkaḷiṟ cila:

camaṟkirutattiṟku mutaṟkāviyam irāmāyaṇam. irāmar  iruntatu  iṭaiccaṅka kālam. ākavē, tolkāppiyamum akattiyamum irāmāyaṇattiṟku muṟpaṭṭaṉavām. vaṭa moḻikku mutal ilakkaṇam pāṇiṉīyam. pāṇiṉi pāratattiṟ kum piṟpaṭṭavar. pāratakālam 1000 B.C.; irāmāyaṇa kālamō 2000 B.C.; ākavē, pāṇiṉīyam tolkāppi yattiṟkuk kuṟaintatu āyiram āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. āriya varukaikku muṉṉamē tamiḻar muttamiḻilum palkalai kaḷilum tērntiruntaṉar. akattiyam vaḻinūlē yaṉṟi mutaṉulākātu. 
iṉi, vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷāvaṉa:
matam
mati + am = matam.
tēy + u = tēyvu - tēvu - tē - tī.
tēyvu + am - tēyvam - teyvam Gk. Theos.
tēyvam - tēvam - tēvaṉ.
teyvam eṉpatu mutalāvatu neruppaiyum piṉpu civattaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. (tiruvācaka virivurai kāṇka)
civa + m = civam - civaṉ - civai.
uru + tiram = uruttiram - uruttiram - rutraṉ = ciṉaṉ. 
uruttal = ciṉattal, tiram toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
ari + am = aram - araṉ.
ō - ōm - ōṅkāram or ōkāram.
ōveṉpatu mūla volikkuṟippu. ol, oli, ōlam, ocai, ōtai mutaliya ōcaipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷellām ōkara ōkāra mutaṉ moḻikaḷātal kāṇka. rī eṉpatu rīṅkāramāṉāṟ pōla ō eṉpatu ōṅkāramāyiṟṟu.
uru + tiram + ākkam = uruttirākkam - ruttirākṣam (urutti rākka viḷakkaṅ kāṇka.)
vi + pūti = vipūti. puḻuti - pūti; vi, upacarkkam.
āṇavam : āḷ - āṇ - āṇmai - āṇavam.
māy + ai = māyai : ai. - to. pe.vi.
kāmi + am = kāmiyam, kām + iyam eṉṟum pirikkalām.
ṇaiyam oru toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
kām + am = kāmam.
kām + ar = kāmar.
kumaraṉ, kumari - kommai (tiraṭci) eṉṉum paṇpiṉaṭiyākap piṟantavai. cf. virgin Gk. orago - to swell. cēy (zeus), murukaṉ, vēlaṉ eṉpaṉa kumaraṟku maṟupeyarkaḷ.
caṇṭikai - caḷ- caḷḷai - caṇṭai - caṇṭaṉ - caṇṭi - caṇṭikai.
kāḷi : kaḷ - kaḷḷam - kāḷam - kāḷi. - kaḷam - kaḷaṅkam = karumpu.
ciṉṉa : ciṉa + am = ciṉam - ciṉaṉ.
viṣṇu : viṇ - viṇṭu. viṇṇaippōl nīlamāyum eṅkum niṟaintumiruppavaṉ.
māyōṉ : mā - māy - māyōṉ. mā = karuppu.
kiruṣṇaṉ : kaḷ - kaḷḷaṉ - kaṇṇaṉ.
māyōṉ, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ kiruṣṇāvatārattiṟ putitāy uṇṭāṉavai yalla. ataṟku muntiyē vaḻaṅkiṉavai. "māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟai yulakamum" eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu kāṇka.
araṅkaṉ :- araṅkam - araṅkaṉ. araṅkam = āṟṟiṭaikkuṟai.
irāmaṉ :- irāmam - irāmaṉ.
irāmam eṉpatu tirumaṇkāppu. irāmam = irēkai vari. irāmak karumpu eṉṉum vaḻakku nōkkuka. vaṭanāṭṭil itait tavaṟāka nāmam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. irāmar oru vaiṇava paktar. irāmarukku muṉpē māl vaḻipāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭi liruntatu.
iraṇṭu, alampu, ilakku, ulāttu mutaliya coṟkaḷ uyirmutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkuvatupōla araṅkaṉ irāmaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷum uyir mutal nīṅki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nārāyaṇaṉ: nāram + ayaṉaṉ.
varuṇaṉ : vār +  aṇaṉ - vāraṇam - vāraṇaṉ - varuṇaṉ.
vār + i = vāri. vār + iti = vāriti.
ātmā : āṉ + mā - āṉmā. āṉ - pacu. mā perumaip poruḷ vikuti. māṉ eṉpatu īṟu kuṟaintu niṉṟateṉiṉum okkum. Gk. animos. vaṭamoḻiyil ātamā carīrattaik kuṟikkum.
naraṉ : narai + aṉ = naraṉ = naraippavaṉ.
pati : patikkut talaivaṉ : iṭavāku peyar. pati = nakar. vīṭu.
pacu : pācattāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu pacu.
pācam : pacumai - paccai - pāci - pācam, pācam. pācam pōṟ piṇikkuṅ  kayiṟu: kayiṟupōṟ piṇikkum malam.
pācam eṉpataṟku ācai yeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉum poruntum.
narakam : narakal - narakam.
cf. dungeon, from dung 
kāyam - ākāyam. kāyam = karuppu. `kāyap peyarvayiṉ' eṉṟu tolkāppiyattuḷ varutal kāṇka.
pūtam : potu - potukku - pontu - pūtam.
ulakam : ula + ku = ulaku. ulaku + am = ulakam.
ulattal = aḻital.
nīr + am = nīram - nāram - nār + am.
cali + am = calam. cali + aṉam = calaṉam. cali + ati = calati. calittal = acaital. calippu eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkaiyum acaivu eṉṉum meyppāṭṭaiyum oppu nōkkuka.
kālam - kāl + am = kālam. camai + am = camaiyam. (viti mutaṉilait toḻilākupeyar.)
vāṉanūl
cūriyaṉ - cur - curaṉ - cūraṉ - cūriyaṉ. Gk. sol. cur eṉpatu oru vemmaik kuṟippu.
cur + am = curam = kāyccal. pālainilam.
mati - kālattai matittaṟ kiṭamāṉatu.
mati + am = mātam cf. Moon. māṉi + am = māṉam.
matiyāl uṇṭāvatu mattam. cf. L. lunacy, from luna. 
tārakai - tār pōlvaṉa.
putaṉ : puttaṉ - putaṉ. putti - utti.
caṉi : cal  - calliyam - caṉi.
kōḷkaḷ ēḻēyaṉṟi oṉpatākā. āyiṉ nāḷkaḷum ēḻaṉṟi oṉpatāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟika.
irāci = (race) kūṭṭam.
mēṣam : mēḻakam - mēḻam - mēṣam.
riṣapam : viṭai - viṭalai - viṭapam - iṭapam.
ā + viṭai = āviṭai. viṭaittiruppatu viṭai.
cf. kuṇṭāyiruppatu kuṇṭai.
kōḻiviṭai eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkaik kāṇka.
kumpam : kum - kumi - kumpam. kumpal = kuvital.
kumpu + iṭu = kumpiṭu. kumpu + al = kumpal.
kumpu + am = kumpam.
kumpu + aṉai = kumpaṉai (company).
kumpu - kūmpu. kumi - kuvi. kumpu - kuppu. kumi + ḻ = kumiḻ.
kuppu + ai = kuppai. kuppu + al = kuppal.
mīṉam : miṉ - mīṉ - mīṉam.
kaṉṉi: kaṉ + i = kaṉṉi. kaṉṉutal = paḻuttal.
cf. maturity, from mature - to ripen.
tulām: tāl - tālam - tulai - tulām.
ciṅkam: ciṅku + am = ciṅkam, aḻippatu.
ciṅkal = kuṟaital, aḻital.
kaṟkaṭakam:- kal + kaṭakam = kaṟkaṭakam, kalnaṇṭu
vaṭavai: vaṭam + ai = vaṭavai, vaṭakkuḷḷa neruppu
c.f. Aurora Borealis, the northern light.
aurora - fire; borealis - northern.
vaṭavai + mukam = vaṭavaimukam. itaip peṭavā mukam eṉat tirittup peṭṭaik kutirai eṉṟu poruḷuraippar.
vaṭavaik kaṉalaip piḻinteṭuttu  -vīrarākavar
akkaṭaliṉmītu vaṭavaṉal niṟkavilaiyō  -tāyumāṉavar
pūkōḷam
kōḷam: kuḻu - kōḷam - kōli.
maṇṭalam: maṇ + talam = maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam.
pūmi vaṭṭamāyiruppatu tamiḻarkkut terintiruntatu.
iṭai yeṉṉum uṟuppuppeyar amcāriyai peṟṟu iṭamā ṉāṟ pōla, talai yeṉṉum uṟuppuppeyar amcāriyai peṟṟut talamāyiṟṟu. iṭappeyarkaḷ perumpālum ciṉaippeyarkaḷākavē yirukkum.
kaṇkāl puṟamakam uḷḷuḻai kīḻmēl
piṉcār ayalpuṭai tēvakai eṉāa
muṉṉiṭai kaṭaitalai valamiṭam eṉāa
aṉṉa piṟavum ataṉpāla eṉmaṉār.  (tol.col.vēṟ.20)
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam. maṇṭalittal - vaṭṭamiṭutal.
tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilam = tiṅkaḷ vaṭṭam.
kaṇṭam : kaṇ - kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam.
tīvu: tīr - u = tīrvu - tīvu. taraip pākattiṉiṉṟum tīrttiruppatu. itait tvīyam eṉṟu tirittu iru pakkattil nīrāl cūḻap paṭṭiruppateṉṟu poruḷuraippar.
ilakkaṇam :
ilakku + aṇam = ilakkaṇam - lakṣaṇam.
ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam - lakṣiyam.
ilakkittal = eḻututal (cīvaka. 180)
cf. grammer from Gk. gramma, a letter, Gk. grapho, to write literature from letter.
karam, kāram, kāṉ, ēṉam - tamiḻ eḻuttuc cāriyaikaḷ.
āytam : āy + tam = āytam. āytal = ārital.
māttirai: mā + tirai = māttirai. māttal = aḷattal. mā + tiram = māttiram.
mā eṉpatu ōr eṇṇaḷavukkum oru parappaḷavukkum peyar.
pakuti : paku + ti = pakuti - pāti. paku - vaku.
paku + am = pakkam = pakṣam  - pākam. pakkam - pāṅku - pāṅkaṉ.
paku + al = pakkal - pakal - pāl.
paku + ai = pakai; paku - pā; pāttīṭu - pātīṭu.
pā + ti = pātti. pakuti - pirakruti.
mūlam: muḷ - muḷai - mūlam.
vikuti : viku + ti = vikuti. viku - vī. vītal = muṭital.
vikuti - vikruti.
cūttiram: cū + tiram = cūttiram, carukkam.
vaṇṇam: paṇ - paṇṇam - vaṇṇam - vaṇṇakam.
cantam: - olikkuṟippup paṟṟiyatu. cantaṭi eṉpatu vaḻakku.
paṇṇatti: paṇ + atti = paṇṇatti.
urupu:  uru + pu = urupu; uru + u = uruvu. uruvu + am = uruvam. uruttal, tōṉṟutal.
uru x aru. cf. uṟu x aṟu. uruvam from rūpam.
kāraṇam: karu - karuvi - karaṇam kāraṇam - kāriyam.
karu - karuppu - karuppam. mēkam cūlkoṇṭa piṉ karuttiruttal kāṇka.
ētu: ētu eṉṟu viṉavuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruppatu.
vf. the why; the how.
ampōtaraṅkam: ampu + taraṅkam.
am - ampu; taraṅku + am = taraṅkam, karaiyaik kuttuvatu.
uvamai: uva + mai - uvamai. uvattal = virupputal. uvama vurupukaḷ perumpālum viruppu veṟuppup poruḷkaḷilēyē varum. uvamai - upamāṉam.
e-ṭu: nāṭa = virumpa. pukala - pōla. pukalal - virumpal.
vf. like, similar, to be pleased with.
uruvakam: uru + akam = uruvakam. oṉṟai maṟṟōr uruvākac collutal. uruvakam - rūpakam.
icai, nāṭakam
curam: curai + am = curam. curai = tuḷai. nākattiṟkup piriyamāṉatu nākacuram.
toṉi: toḷ - toḷai - toṇṭai - toṉi.
irākam: arākam - irākam. kalippā vuṟuppu.
tāṇṭavam: tāḷ - tāṇṭu - tāṇṭavam.
naṭam - naṭa - naṭi - naṭam - naṭṭam.
naṭi + am - naṭam.
naṭi + akam = nāṭakam.
maṇṭalam = mandoline. curavīṇai - saraphina.
kuṭam - kuṭai + am = kuṭam, vaḷaintiruppatu. kuṭam - kaṭam.
aracu
ari - aram - aravu - aracu - aracaṉ, Gk. archon.
araicaṉ - araiyaṉ -  Roy, Royal.
maraṅkaṭkuḷ aracu pōṉṟiruppatu aracamaram.
cēraṉ: cāral - cēral - cēraṉ - cēralaṉ - kēraḷaṉ.
cōḻaṉ: cōḷam - cōḻam - cōḻaṉ.
kaḷḷarveṭṭi (makkā)c cōḷam cōḻanāṭṭiltāṉ ciṟappāy viḷaikiṟatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ: pāṇṭi + aṉ = pāṇṭiyaṉ, vīraṉ.
pāṇṭi = vaṭṭam. pāṇṭil = erutu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ kāḷai pōṉṟavaṉ.
pāṇṭavar campantappaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟu Caldwell  kūṟiyiruppatu tavaṟu. pāṇṭiyaṉ peyar pāratattiṟku 1000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa vālmīki irāmāyaṇattiṟ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
cāmam: camam - cāmam.
pētam: pēti + am = pētam.
tāṉam: tā + aṉam = tāṉam.
taṇṭam: taṇṭi - taṇṭu - taṇṭam.
taṇṭi + aṉai = taṇṭaṉai. taṇṭi - taṭi.
taṭittal - paruttal. taṭital = veṭṭutal.
mutal taṇṭaṉai taṭiyālaṭippatā yiruntatu.
ciṉaippeyarkaḷ
mukam: mukar - muka + m = mukam. mukattal. muka - mō.
mukar - nukar. mukam - nukam. mukam - mūkku - mūñci - mukaṭu.
muka + pu = mukappu. muka + vai. mukavai.
mukam mutalāvatu mūkkiṉ peyarāyiruntu piṉpu tāṉiyāku peyarāy mukattiṟkāyiṟṟu. vaṭamoḻiyil vāyai maṭṭum kuṟikkum.
irattam: arattam - irattam. arakku, alattakam iratti eṉpavai cenniṟattaik kuṟikkum.
pittu + am = pittam - paittiyam.
karu + pu = karuppu. karuppu + am - karuppam.
moḻi - muḻam - muṭṭu - muṭṭi - muṣṭi pati + am = pātam.
ciṉaip peyarkaḷ
vaṭivam = paṭi - paṭivu - vaṭivam, body, piratimā.
paṭi - vaṭi - vaṭivu - vaṭivam.
vaṭṭam: vaḷ + tam = vaṭṭam. vaḷ + i = vaḷi.
vaḷ + ti = vaṭṭi - vaṇṭi vaḷ + am = vaḷḷam.
vaḷ + tu = vaṭṭu - vaṇṭu = vaḷaiyal.
vaḷ + tai = vaṭṭai - vaṭai. vaḷai +am = vaḷaiyam.
vaḷ + ai = vaḷai. vaḷai + al - vaḷaiyal.
vaḷaiyam vaṭamoḻiyil valayam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. vaṭṭam viruttattiṉ tiripaṉṟu.
ticaip peyarkaḷ
tikku + ai = tikai. tikaittaṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruppatu.
tikai - ticai.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭam - kuṇam - kuṇakku.
kuṭam: kuṭai - kuṭam. katiravaṉ vaḷaiyumiṭam - kuṭakku.
uttaram: u + taram = uttaram. u = mēl, uyarvu.
teṟku + aṇam - teṟkaṇam - tekkaṇam - takkaṇam. tek kaṇam - Deccan. cf. koṅku + aṇam = koṅkaṇam.
eṇṇup peyarkaḷ
ilakkam = eṇ. ip potup peyarē nūṟāyirattukkuc ciṟappup peyarāyiṟṟu. lakh - lakṣam.
eṭṭu - aṣṭam.
kōṭi - kōṭṭi. kōṭi = kaṭaici yeṇ.
nāṉā - nāl - nālā - nāṉā.
palvakaic coṟkaḷ
tamiḻam - trāviṭam.
vēṭṭi: veṭṭuvatu vēṭṭi. vēṭṭi - vēṣṭi.
cf.  tuṇippatu tuṇi; aṟuppatu aṟuvai.
muṭṭi - muṭṭiyāṟ ceyyum pōr. muṭṭi - muṣṭi.
vf. wrestle from wrist.
kākam - kākkā - kākkai kākam.
mayil - mayūram. kūkai - kūkam.
kalai - kal + ai. paṭi + am - pāṭam.
cuṟṟuru - cūtram. kali + yāṇam = kaliyāṇam.
kalittal = taḻaittal. yāṇam = putumai. putuvaruvāy.
paḻam - palam. paḻu + am = paḻam. aṭavi - aṭarntiruppatu.
pari + am = pāram - pāri. tari + am = tāram.
tarukku + am = tarukkam - mikuttuk kāṭṭutal.
kuṭi - kuṭil - kuṭilam - kuṭīram. cāy + ai = cāyai.
i - itam = ippaṭi. pavaḷam - pravāḷam. nīl + am = nīlam.
iṉi, akkā, attā, āṇi, ammā, ampā, aṭavi, āḷi, kaṭu, kāvēri, kucam, kūccam, kuṭi, kūṉ, kuḷam, kōṭṭai, kaṭṭil, cām, cā, paṭṭaṇam, poṉ, paḷḷi, veḷ mutaliyaṉa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya teṉcoṟ kaḷām. iṉṉum ivai pōṉṟa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻivāyp paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷatellām vaṭacoṟkaḷ eṉṟu moḻi nūluṇarātār cilar koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. mūla mutaṉmoḻi yākiya tamiḻ tavira vēṟu enta moḻiyāvatu piṟacoṟka ḷillātatillai. oru collai iṉṉa moḻiyeṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakutip poruḷaiyum ataṉiṉṟum tirinta palvēṟu coṟkaḷaiyum pārkkavēṇṭum. avai enta moḻi yiluḷḷaṉavō anta moḻikkē atu uriyatām. viṉaic collāyiṉ puṭaipeyarcci (Conjugation) vēṇṭum. peyarc collāyiṉ peyarilakkaṇam (Declension) vēṇṭum kal eṉṉum viṉai kaṟṟāṉ, kaṟṟāḷ, kaṟṟār, kaṟṟatu, kaṟṟa, kaṟka, kaṟpittāṉ, kaṟkappaṭṭatu, kaṟṟilaṉ, kalai eṉap pala vaṭivukaḷilum puṭaipeyara vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyilō atu kalā (kalai) eṉat toḻilākupeyarāy maṭṭum niṟkum. iḵtōr utāraṇam.
cila vaṭamoḻip paṟṟuṭaiyār muṉivar eṉṉum collukku muṉi eṉpatu pakutiyeṉ ṟaṟiyāmal, mavuṉam ceypavar muṉivar eṉṟu vaṭamoḻivaḻi yuraippar. `aiyaṉ' eṉṉum tamiḻ muṟaip peyarai āriyaṉ eṉpatiṉ citaivākak kūṟuvar. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉaic coṟkaḷukkum avar vaṭamoḻiyaik kaṟpippatuṇṭu.
oru vakuppārāvatu piṟa vakuppāruṭaṉ iyaipiṉṟit tāmāy ulakattil vantavarallar. aṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ tavira oru moḻiyā vatu piṟamoḻiyiṉ kalappiṉṟit tāṉāy vantataṉṟu eṉṟu teḷika.  ("centamiḻc celvi" kaṭakam 1931)
vaṭamoḻit teṉcoṟkaḷ
ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷellām oru perun tāymoḻiyi ṉiṉṟum utittavai yeṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūl (History), kulanūl (Ethnology), moḻinūl (Philology) mutaliya pala nūlkaḷālum valiyuṟuttappaṭum. āyiṉum, ticaiccol vakaiyil oru vakuppār putiyaṉa vāy ākkik koṇṭaṉavum tāymoḻiyi ṉiṉṟum putiyaṉavāyt tirittukkoṇṭaṉavu māṉa iruvakaic coṟkaḷ ovvoru moḻikkum ciṟappākum. iv viruvakaic coṟkaḷum oru kiḷaimoḻikkuriya ataṉ tāymoḻiyaic cārum; oru tāymoḻik kuriyavai oru perun tāy moḻiyaic cārum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai nōkkat tamiḻ oru tāymoḻi yum, āriya moḻikaḷai nōkkat tamiḻ oru perun tāymoḻiyu mākum.
eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭamaiyālum, āriyac coṟkaḷil pala urut teriyātavāṟu attuṇait tirintuḷḷati ṉālum, pala vaṭacoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ciṟappuc coṟkaḷākavē tōṉṟāniṟkum. ēṉaic coṟkaḷai itu pōtuḷḷa teṉ coṟ ṟuṇaikoṇṭē teṉcolleṉak kāṭṭal moḻi nūlāṉ muṭṭiṉṟi muṭivatākum. iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷāyiṉ iv varampiṟ kuṭpaṭā.
oru collai oru moḻik kuriyateṉṟu aṟitaṟku ataṉ pakuti (stem) allatu mūlam (root) emmoḻiyiluḷḷa teṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum.
oru colliṉ pakuti allatu mūlam palamoḻiyi liruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil, pakuti iyaṟkaiyāṉateṉṟum, poruḷ ciṟanta teṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
pala moḻiyilum, pakuti vaṭivilum poruḷilum ottiruppiṉ, emmoḻiyil ataṉ tiricoṟkaḷuḷa veṉṟum, avaiyum pala moḻiyiliruppiṉ, etil avai kaḻipalaveṉṟum kāṇṭal vēṇṭum.
ettuṇaiyō teṉcoṟkaḷai vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉṟu paṉ moḻip pēraṟiñaruṅkūṭap pakarntu varukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu:
akaṅkāram. akam + kāram. akam - avvulaka, vāṉulaku. mōkṣam. akaraccuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟantatu: cēymaiyiluḷḷa vāṉulakattaik kuṟippatu; ampar eṉṉum coṟpōla. a - ampar - amparam. ikarac cuṭṭaṭiyāp piṟanta ikam, impar eṉṉum  coṟkaḷ aṇmaic cuṭṭāy ivvulakattaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
viṇṇulakaiyum maṇṇulakaiyum cēymai aṇmaipaṟṟi avvulakam ivvulakam eṉpatu marapu.
aruḷillārk kavvulaka millai poruḷillārk
kivvulaka millāki yāṅku  (kuṟaḷ. 247)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum.
vīṭu eṉṉum muttip peyar illattai uṇarttiṉāṟpōla. akam eṉṉum peyarum illattai uṇarttum vīṭu = viṭutalai: viṭuvatu vīṭu. piṟavit tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai ceyvatu pēriṉpa vīṭu. veyiṉ maḻait tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai ceyvatu ivvulaka vīṭu.
il eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyar  ēḻām vēṟṟumai urupāy uṭpākattaik kuṟittāṟ pōla, akam eṉṉum vīṭṭup peyarum uṭpākattaiyum uḷḷattaiyum kuṟikkum `akameṉ kiḷavi' eṉat taṉittamiḻ ilakkaṇamākiya tolkāppiyat tuḷḷum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
kāram: kari eṉṉum viṉaiyaṭiyāp piṟanta toḻiṟpeyar. kari + am = kāram: mutaṉilai tirintu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
cf: paṭi + am = pāṭam. tavi + am = tāvam.
karittal mikutal. kāram mikuti.
kari eṉpatu kaṭi eṉṉum uriccolliṉ tiripu.
ṭa-ra, pōli. o.nō: paṭavar  - varavar. mukaṭi - mukari.
uppukkarikkum, uppuk kaṭukkum eṉpaṉa oruporuḷpaṟṟiya ulaka vaḻakku. kaṭi eṉṉum uriccoṟ poruḷkaḷuḷ mikutiyum oṉṟu.
kāram eṉṉum col mutalāvatu mikutiyai uṇarttip piṉpu uṟaippu mikutiyai uṇarttum.
kari eṉṉum collē ati eṉṉum upacarkkam (prefix) peṟṟu atikari eṉa niṟkum. atikarippatu atikāram.
akam karippatu akaṅkāram. akaṅkāram - āṅkāram.
ati eṉpatu oru mikutip poruḷupacarkkam (intensive prefix).
atikārattai (āṇai mikutiyai or āṟṟaṉ mikutiyai) uṭaiyavaṉ atikāri.
oru nūliṉ perumpākam atikāram.
akam karippatu akaṅkāram. akaṅkāram - āṅkāram.
akaṇṭam: a + kaṇṭam.  a. etirmaṟai upacarkkam: al eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. al eṉṉum etirmaṟaic collē un eṉṟu āriyamoḻikaḷil tiriyum. il eṉpatu il, in, im, eṉṟu tiriyum.
kaṇṭam: kaṇ - kaṇu - kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam.
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭi. cf: tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi. taṇṭu - taṇṭi - taṭi.
kaṇpōṉṟu iruppatu kaṇu.
kaṇukkaṇuvāy naṟukkuvatu kaṇṭu.
kaṇṭu eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu kaṇṭi eṉṉum viṉaiyuṇ ṭākum. kaṇṭittal kaṇukkaṇuvāy allatu tuṇṭutuṇṭāy veṭṭutalum, aṅṅaṉam veṭṭutalpōṟ kaṭumoḻi kūṟalum.
kaṇṭippatu kaṇṭam. kaṇṭi + am = kaṇṭam. cf. tuṇṭi + am = tuṇṭam. taṇṭi + am = taṇṭam.
kaṇṭi eṉṉum col iṭaik kuṟaintu kaṭi eṉṟum niṟkum.  cf. taṇṭi - taṭi
kaṇṭam eṉpataṉ nīṭṭal kāṇṭam. cf. karaṇam - kāraṇam. kaṇṭam = tuṇṭu, pākam, pirivu.
kaṇṭam eṉpatu cataippākam nilappākaṅkaḷaiyum, kāṇṭam (canto) eṉpatu nūṟpākaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟikkum.
Canto eṉṉum āṅkilac collai cano (to sing) eṉṉum ilattīṉ   viṉaiyaṭip piṟappākak koḷvatu ciṟantataṉṟu.

katittal - cellutal. kati + am = katam.
irākkatam - irāc celavu.

 nici = iravu: caram - acaivu, celavu.
