aparam: a + param. a, al eṉpataṉ kuṟai.
param = para + am. parattal = virital, peruttal, mēṟpaṭutal.
param eṉpatu mikutip poruḷil mēliṭattai yuṇarttum. itaṉ pariyāyac collākiya mikal eṉṉuñ collum aṅṅaṉamē.
miku - mikal - mēl. mēliṭam uyarattiṉ mikutiyākum naṭṭukkut talāy (vertical) oru poruḷ mikkirukkumpōtu uyaramā yiruttal kāṇka.
param = mēl. aparam = kīḻ.
paraṉ = mēlāṉavaṉ - kaṭavuḷ.
parāparaṉ = mēlum kīḻumuḷḷa poruḷkaḷā yiruppavaṉ.
paraṉ - civaṉ, āṇpāl.
parai - civai (pārvati), peṇpāl.
param + aṉ  = paramaṉ.
paratēcam = param + tēcam.
param - Foreign. L. foras
paralōkam =  param + lōkam.
paratēcam - pakkamākap (sidewise) parantatu.
paralōkam  - mēṉōkkip parantatu - oru poruḷ parakkumpōtu virintu tūramākum.
mēṉōkkiya parappu uyaram.
pakkamāṉa parapput tūram.
paranta (tūramāṉa) tēcam = paratēcam.
paratēcattāṉ - paratēci.
param + kālaṉ = parakālaṉ.
param + kēcari = parakēcari. param = tūram, ayal piṟitu.
parakālaṉ - piṟarkkuk kālaṉ pōṉṟavaṉ.
aparūpam: apa + rūpayam.
ava - apa.
uruvam - rūpam.
uru - uruvu - uruvam - rūpam. uruvu - urupam - rūpam.
uruttal = erital. ciṉattal, tōṉṟutal.
urum = (warm) veppam = (iṭi), (neruppāṉatu).
uruttal - ciṉattal. ciṉam - neruppuppōlvatu.
cf. kaṉal = nerupapu. kaṉalutal = ciṉattal.
aḻal = neruppu. aḻalutal = ciṉattal.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal. vaṭivam neruppiṉ taṉmai.
neruppāl oḷiyum oḷiyāl uruvamum tōṉṟum.
urupu = uruvam. vaṭivam.
vēṟṟumaiyiṉ vaṭivaik kāṭṭum acai urupu eṉappaṭṭatu. "eḻuvā yurupu tiripil peyarē" eṉṉum cūttirattil, urupu eṉpatu vaṭiveṉṟē poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
urupam eṉṉum collē raṇṭu. rampa eṉṉum coṟkaḷaip pōla mutal keṭṭup piṉpu ayal nīṇṭu rūpam eṉṟākum.
uru eṉpatu mutaṉilait toḻilāku peyar.
uru x aru. cf. uṟu x aṟu. urupam x arupam.
arupamāṉavaṉ arupi.  urupi. urupi - urūpi.
arupamāṉavaṉ arupi.  arupi - arūpi.
uru = vaṭivam. mutaṉilait toḻilākupeyar.
uru eṉṉum col vaṭivup poruḷil ulakavaḻakkil mikutiyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
uru = vaṭivuṭaip poruḷ.
cuṟṟuru = tālikkoṭiyil tāliyōṭu cērnta pala vaṭivāṉa ciṟunakaikaḷ. kaḻuttaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa urukkaḷ eṉpatu poruḷ.
uruppaṭi = oru poruḷ.
uruvāku = uṇṭāku.
uruppaṭu = vaḷar, tēṟu, periyavaṉāku.
urukkulai. urumāṟu = vaṭivumāṟu, meli.
uruccēvi = vikkirakattai vaṇaṅku.
uruppōṭu = vikkirakattiṟkuriya mantiram jepi.
uruvēṟṟu = mantiram jepi, upatēci.
urupi = uruvattaiyuṭaiyatu. ruppi, Hindi, Skt. and English.
amaṅkalam: a + maṅkalam.
naṉkalam - (naṅkalam) - maṅkalam.
naṉkalam - tāli, ciṟanta aṇi.
naṉkalam aṇiyattakka nilai maṅkalam.
naṉkalam - maṅkalam. na - ma, pōli.
cf. nām - mēmu (teluṅku).
māṭṭuppeṇ - nāṭṭuppeṇ
muṉi-nuṉi		    ma-na. pōli.
maṅkalattai yuṭaivaḷ maṅkali - maṅkalai.
maṅkalam - maṅkaliyam - maṅkiliyam - māṅkaliyam - māṅkiliyam.
maṅkalam - maṅkaḷam.
maṅkalam = maṅkaliyam aṇikiṉṟa tirumaṇam.
tirumaṇam pōṉṟa nalviṉai.
e-ṭu: maṇṇumaṅkalam = naṉmai.
amarāpati: amarar + pati.
pati = patintirukkum iṭam.
patital = taṅkiyiruttal. poruntiyiruttal. nilaiyāyiruttal. vacittal.
pati - vati. pa - va, pōli. cf. paku-vaku.
pati - ūr, nakar (mutaṉilait toḻilākupeyar).
amarāpati - amarāvati.
pati + am = patam.
amarar + patam = amarāpatam.
gâ-bad- (Hindi) - pāttu.
aitarāpati - Hyderabad - haitarāpāttu.
amāttiyar: aṇmai + c + ar = aṇmaiccar - amaiccar.
cf. araṇmaṉai - aramaṉai (ulakavaḻakku).
amaiccar aracaṉukku aṇmaiyi liruppavar.
amaiccar - amāttiyar.
aviḻutu: aviḻ-aviḻtu-amutu.
aviḻtal - virital.
aviḻ = cōṟṟupparukkai, ventu virinta arici.
aviḻtu = cōṟu.
aviḻtu = amutu. va-ma, pōli.
aviḻtu + am = aviḻtam - amutam.
amutu paṭaittal = cōṟiṭutal.
ampikai: ammai-ammāḷ-ammā - ampā - ampikā - ampikai.
ampu: kam - ōr olikkuṟippu.
kam = nīr, olippatu.
kam-am. cf. kaṉal - aṉal, taḻal - aḻal.
am - ampu (filÉÇ-Epenthesis).
cf. uṭal - (uṭam)-uṭampu.
ampōtaraṅkavottāḻicaik kalippā, centamiḻc ceyyuḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.
ampu (arrow) pōlap peyyum maḻainīr ampu eṉiṉum okkum.
araṇiyam: araṇ + iyam.  iyam oru vikuti (suffix).
araṇiyam = kāṭu. araṇāyiruppatu.
nālvakai araṇkaḷiṟ kāṭṭaraṇ oṉṟu.
araṇiyam - āraṇiyam.
araṉ: ar - ōr olikkuṟippu.
ar + am = aram. oru karuvi, ar eṉṟu olippatu.
aram - arampōl tēyttal, aḻittal.
aram - tuṉpam. aḻivu ceyvatu.
aram (harm) - arantai (marūu).
aram = accam, tuṉpattiṉālāvatu.
araḷ = añcu. araḷ - horro.
araṟṟu = añcik kataṟu.
araṉ = tēvaṉ. añcat takkavaṉ, varuttupavaṉ. malaiyilum kāṭṭilumuḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ tamiyarai varuttumeṉpatu tamiḻnāṭṭuk koḷkai. kuṟiñcittiṇaic ceyyuḷkaḷil cūrara makaḷir eṉpatu peruvaḻakkāy varuvataic caṅka nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. cūr = accam.
aramakaḷir = teyvap peṇṭir.
araṉ = civaṉ, malaitteyvam.
malaināṭṭilēyē caivam mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiṟṟu. kuṟiñcit teyvamākiya murukaṉ civapirāṉatu makaṉ. akattiyar mutaliya paṇṭai muṉivarum malaiyilēyē vatintaṉar.
araṉ = uruttiraṉ:  ūḻiyiṟutiyil ulakaiyaḻippavaṉ.
araṉ - haraṉ. cf. ālī - hail.
ar + i = ari - aḻippatu.
arittal - pūccikaḷ poruḷkaḷai aḻittal.
ari = ciṅkam, vilaṅkukaḷai aḻippatu.
curaari - (jvarahari) - kāyccalai aḻikkum maruntu.
ari = viṣṇu, ciṅkavaṭivu koṇṭavaṉ. civaṉukku maṉaiviyā yiruntavaṉ eṉiṉum okkum.
araṉ āṇpāl. ari peṇpāl. ari - hari.
arākam: ar - ōr olikkuṟippu.
arākam = paṇ, cantam, ōcai.
arākam = irākam. vf. aṟu - iṟu.
irākam = paṇ, viruppam.
paṇ iṉimaiyā yiruttaliṉ evarukkum viruppām cf. Music- that which amuses. aṉurākam = kātal. arākam muṭukiya ōcaiyuḷḷa oru kaliyuṟuppu. centamiḻ ilakkaṇamākiya tolkāppiyattuṭ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
arākam eṉiṉum vaṇṇakam eṉiṉum okkum.
vaṇṇakam = paṇ, ocai.
arākam eṉpatu iyaṟṟamiḻkkaṉṟi icaittamiḻkkum iṉṟi yamaiyātatōr collām. icaittamiḻum poruḷilak kaṇamum āriya varukaikku muṉpē tamiḻrkkiruntavai.
alaṭciyam: a + laṭciyam.
ilakkiyam - lakṣiyam.
ilakkiyam - kuṟi, utāraṇam (Literature) ilakku pakuti.
avamati: avam + mati
matittal = aḷaviṭutal, uyarvā yeṇṇutal.
mati = aḷantaṟiyum carakku. ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati.
matittal=nitāṉittal, maṉattālaḷattal.
mati=cantiraṉ, orumāta kālattai aḷaviṭuvatu.
mati + am = mātam.
mati + u = mātu
mati + ar = mātar    uṇavai aḷavāyp pakirpavaḷ, peṇ tāy.
mātu - mātā. Skt.
mati = aṟivu. Knowledge.
cammati = icaivu. aṟintu icaivatu.
aṉumati = uttaravu. aṟintu viṭuppatu.
mati = matittaḷikkum paricu, vekumati.
mati + am = matam, kaṭavuḷai allatu vīṭṭaippaṟṟiya matippu allatu koḷkai.
avalaṭcaṇam: avam + lakṣaṇam.
ilakkaṇam = lakṣaṇam. ilakku - pakuti
ilakkaṇam = ilakkiyattiṉ oḻuṅku allatu aḻaku.
avi: avi = uṇavu. ney (avikkappaṭuvatu).
avinayacālai: api + nayam + cālai.
api mikutip poruḷuṇarttum oru vaṭamoḻi upacarkkam.
nayam = nērtti. (teṉcol) nayam nicety: ya-ca, pōli.
apinayam = mika nērttiyāṉa āṭṭam allatu āṭṭattiṉ uṟuppu.
apinayam ōr orumoḻit toṭar (Hybrid).
nai + am = (naiyam) - nayam, toḻiṟpeyar.
cf. vai + u = vayavu.
naital = nuṭpamāka noṟukkappaṭutal.
nayam = nuṭpam. apinayam = nuṭpamāṉa āṭṭam.
cālai: cāl + ai = cālai.
cālutal = niṟaital. paruttal. vicālittal.
cāla (nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccam) mikutip poruḷuṇarttum uriccol.
cālai = akaṉṟa pātai. akaṉṟa kūṭam.
pāṭacālai = pāṭam paṭikkappaṭum kūṭam.
cālai = saloon, hall 
cāl + pu = cālpu = niṟaivu. peruntaṉmai.
cāl + vai = cālvai (shawl), uṭampu niṟaiyap pōrppatu.
cāṉṟōr = aṟivu niṟaintōr, vīram niṟaintōr.
cālu = niṟaiyum, pōtum (teluṅku).
avivēkam: a + vi + vēkam.
a - etirmaṟai upacarkkam. vi - mikutipporuḷ upacarkkam.
vēku + am = vēkam = neruppu. erital.
vēkam = aṟivu, neruppiṉ taṉmaiyuḷḷatu.
neruppu eṅṅaṉam oru poruḷai viraintu paṟṟumō aṅṅaṉamē aṟivum oru poruḷai viraintu paṟṟum. aṟivum viraivumuḷḷavaṉaic cūṭṭikkaiyuḷḷavaṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkam.
ākāyam: ā + kāyam. ā, mutalviri (prothesis)
"kāyap peyarvayiṉ" eṉṟār kolkāppiyar.
kayam = paḷḷam, kuḷam, kaṟuppu. āḻamāṉa nīr kaṟuppatiyalpu.
kayam - kāyam (nīṭṭal). kayam - kacam, pōli. iruṇṭa iruṭṭai iruṭṭuk kacameṉpar teṉṉāṭṭār. kāyā - karuniṟamāṉa pū.
kāyam = vāṉam (kariyatu).
ā + kāyam = ākāyam - ākācam. yā-ca, pōli.
ācayam: akam (Tamil) - eikos (Gk.) - ācayam (Skt.) - house (E). 
akam = vīṭu. ācayam = iruppiṭam: e-ṭu: irattācayam.
āññai: āḷ-āṇ-āṇai-ājñā.
`āvīṟaiyum' eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār tamiḻukkuk kūṟiya muṟaiyē aiyīṟākavum eṉṟu vaṭamoḻikku māṟṟik kūṟuka.
āttumam: āṉ + mā = āṉmā.
ā = pacu, ṉ - cāriyai.
āṉmā-āmā=pacu. kāṭṭuppacu.
mā eṉpatu vilaṅkup potuppeyar.
āmā oru col.
uyirkaḷaip pacukkaḷ eṉpatu caivacittānta vaḻakku.
āṉmā = uyir, animos (L.) = life. animal = pirāṇi.
ātmā - Skt. ātmi (Hindi).
ātām = mutal maṉitaṉ (Hebrew). uyir eṉpatu tamiḻil oru pirāṇiyaik kuṟittāṟpōla ātmā eṉpatu vaṭamoḻi. urutu mutaliya moḻikaḷil oru maṉitaṉaik kuṟikkum.
ātmā - āttumam.
ālāpaṉam: ālāpi + aṉam = ālāpaṉam.
āl - ālāpi.
ālutal = vaṭṭamāka āṭutal. `mayilāla' - kalittokai.
ālāpittal = ōr irākattai vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟiccuṟṟip pāṭutal.
ālāpi + aṉai = ālāpaṉai.
ālatti - āḷatti = viḷakkai allatu irākattai vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟuvittal.
riṣapam - iṭapam.
viṭai - viṭapam-iṭapam.  viṭai - viṭalai.
vilaṅkukaḷilum, ciṟappāyp paṟavaikaḷilum iḷamaiyāyp paruttiruppavaṟṟai viṭai eṉpatu vaḻakku. viṭai - viṭait tiruppatu mutaṉilait toḻilākupeyar.
viṭaittal-paruttal.
viṭai eṉpatu viṭapam eṉṟu tiriyum. pam oru vikuti.
cf. vālam-vālipam.
viṭapam eṉpatu mutaṉmey nīṅki iṭapam eṉṟu niṟkum.
cf. camai - amai. vaḷai-aḷai.
viṭai eṉpatu viṭalai eṉṟum tiriyum. viṭai eṉṉum peyarum ataṉ tiripukaḷum pala vilaṅkiṟkum potuvēṉum, ciṟappāka māṭṭiṟkāḷaṅkaṉṟaiyē kuṟippatu nūṉmarapu. tōṭuṭaiyaceviyaṉ viṭaiyēṟi. kāḷai eṉṉum peyar uvamaiyāku peyarāy ōr iḷaiñaṉai allatu vīraṉai uṇarttiṉāṟpōla. ataṉ pariyāyap peyarākiya viṭai eṉpa tum ataṉ tiripukaḷum iḷaiñarai allatu vīrarai uṇarttum.
pālaimakkaḷāṉa maṟavariṉ talaivaṉ viṭalai eṉappaṭuvatu kāṇka.
ōr iḷaiñaṉai viṭalaippaiyaṉ eṉpatum, iḷaṅkōḻi kaḷaic cēvalviṭai kōḻiviṭai eṉpatum iṉṟum teṉ ṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
viṭalai eṉṉum kaṉṟuppeyar ilattīṉ moḻiyil vitula eṉṟu tiriyum.
iti, ittiyāti: ikaraccuṭṭu pakuti. vaṭamoḻikkuc cuṭṭuc collēyaṉṟic  cuṭṭeḻuttillai.
irattam: arattam = civappu.
arattam-arattakam-alattakam = cempañcukkuḻampu.
arattam eṉṉum collukkup pakuti vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
itaṟkum itaṟkiṉamāṉa piṟa coṟkaṭkum ar allatu aram eṉpatu mūlamā yirukkiṉṟatu.
arakku = civantiruppatu.
arattamma - irattam.
a - i. pōli.
iratti = ilantai, civanta kaṉiyai yuṭaiya ceṭi. alattakam, ilantai eṉṉum coṟkaḷil lakaram pōli.
iratti - ilatti - ilantai.  arattam - rattam. Skt. red, E.
irampam: ar ōr olikkuṟippu.
ar ar eṉṉum olipaṭat tēyttal. arāvutal. arāvuvatu aram. arampōl aṟukkum āyutam arampam.
araṅkutal = tēytal, aḻital (taṉviṉai).
arakkutal = tēyttal, aḻittal (piṟaviṉai).
arantai = tuṉpam, aṟuppatupōl tuṉpam ceyvatu.
arittal = tēyttal, aṟuttal, aḻittal.
arampam - irampam. a - i, pōli.
irākkataṉ: irā + katam + aṉ = irākkataṉ.
irā = iravu. katam = celavu, kati - pakati. katittal = cellutal. aṉ-vikuti. iravil ceṉṟu uṇavu tēṭupavaṉ irākkataṉ. irākkataṉ rākṣasaṉ. Skt. kka-kṣa.
irākkataṉ eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppē nicācaraṉ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟpeyar. nici = iravu, caraṉ - celpavaṉ.
irākkataṉ eṉpatu muṟaiyē irākkaṉ, arakkaṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
arakkaṉ eṉpataṟku aḻippavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉum amaiyum.
irākkaṉ, irākki eṉṉum peyarkaḷ muṟaiyē āṇpāṟkum peṇpāṟkum, iṉṉum teṉṉāṭṭil cila vakuppārkkuḷ iṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
irākkatar paṇṭaikkālattilēyē tamiḻnāṭṭi liruntatāl, avaraik kuṟikkum peyarum tamiḻiliruttal vēṇṭum.
irācaṉ :  aracu-aracaṉ-rājaṉ.
aram = aḻittal. See araṉ, aram-aracu.
araṉ = teyvam, civaṉ.
aracu + aṉ = aracaṉ = tēvaṉ pōṉṟavaṉ.
aracu = talaimai. maraṅkaṭkuḷ talaimaiyāṉatu aracamaram.
tēkku, karuṅkāli mutaliyavai uṟutiyāyiruppiṉum aracupōl ōṅki vaḷarvatillai.
"ālpōṟ paṭarntu aracupō lōṅki" eṉṉum ulakiyal tirumaṇa vāḻttai nōkkuka.
ālamaram atikamāyp paṭariṉum, viḻutūṉṟiyapiṉ aṭi taḷarntupōm. aracu ōṅkivaḷarntu neṭunāḷirukkum. ataṉāl piḷḷaiyār kōyil, maṇavaṟai, paṭṭañcūṭṭu viḻāvakam mutaliyavaṟṟil aracaṅ kaṉṟaiyē naṭuvar.
aracu, pūvaracu eṉpaṉa ōriṉa maraṅkaḷ, mēṉāṭṭār ilai vaṭivoppumai paṟṟic cila maraṅkaḷai ōriṉamākkiṉāṟ pōlap paṇṭait tamiḻarum ilaivaṭi voppumaipaṟṟi aracaiyum pūvaracaiyum ōriṉa mākkiṉar. pūvaiyuṭaiya aracu pūvaracu, aracamarattiṟkup pūviṉmai yaṟika.
aracaṉ eṉṉum teṉcollē rājaṉ eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. aracaṉ eṉpatai rājaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟṟiripākat taṭumāṟak kūṟuvar moḻinūl aṟiyātār cilar. aracu, pūvaracu eṉṉum marappeyarkaḷ āriya varukaikku muṉpē tamiḻ nāṭṭil  vaḻaṅkiya paḻan tamiḻppeyarkaḷ eṉpataiyēṉum avar aṟivārāka.
aracaṉ - rājaṉ, Skt.  rāj (intutāṉi)
rājaṉ - irājaṉ - irācaṉ (taṟpavam)
rājyam - rācciyam - irācciyam.
rājaṉ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu rego, regent, region, regicide, regular mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
aracaṉ-araicaṉ, a-ai pōli
araicaṉ-araiyaṉ, ca-ya pōli.
arayaṉ-rāyaṉ Skt. roy; E.Vice-Roy.
rāyar eṉpatu aracaraiyum oru pārppaṉak kuṭiyaiyum uṇarttum. 
irācakāriyam, irāca ciṉṉam, irājasrī, irācanīti, irārācaṉ, irācavacīkaram, irācavīti, irācākkiṉai, irācātikāram, irācātipati, irācātti, irācāḷi mutaliya toṭarmoḻika ḷellām aracakāriyam, araca ciṉṉam mutaliya centamiḻt toṭarkaḷiṉ tiripukaḷē yeṉṟaṟintu koḷka.
irāttiri. irāttiram.
irā + tiri = irāttiri. irā+tiram=irāttiram. tiram eṉpatu oru vikutic (suffix) col.
cf. cāyuntiram - cāyntiram.
tiram - tiri
irā - iruṇṭiruppatu. irumai = karumai, iraṇṭu, iravu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ raṇṭu, rāvu eṉṟu tirintāṟpōla, irāttiri, irāttiram eṉṉum peyarkaḷum rātri, rātram eṉṟu tiriyum.
ucitam: ucitam = uyarvu. ukaraccuṭṭaṭiyāyp piṟantatu.
laṭcaṇam: lakṣaṇam, lakṣyam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ ilak kaṇam. ilakkiyam eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ tiripē.
laṭcam. ilakkam-lakṣam.
tamiḻiluḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷellām ukaravīṟṟaṉa eṉṟu koṇṭu, tamiḻarkku ātiyil 100 varaiyiltāṉ eṇṇat terintateṉṟum, 999 varaiyiltāṉ tamiḻilakkameṉṟum, āyiram, ilakkam, kōṭi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉ ṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vāṉa nūl kaṇita nūl mutaliya kalaikaḷil ettuṇaiyō vallunarum ciṟṟilakkam, poṉ ṉilakkam mutaliya aḷavaikaḷaiyum āytakkuṟukkam makarak kuṟukkam mutaliya māttirai nuṇukkaṅkaḷaiyum aṟivatil nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattiṉarum, veḷḷam, āmpal mutaliya pēreṇkaḷaik kuṟiyiṭṭāṇṭavarumāṉa muṉṉait tamiḻrkku mūṉṟu tāṉaṅkaṭkumēl eṇṇat teriyātu eṉpatu eṅṅaṉ poruntum?
"āyirak kiḷavi varūuṅ kālai" eṉat tolkāppiyattuṭ kūṟiyiruppatāl, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē āyiram eṉṉum eṇṇum ataṉ peyarum tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅki yiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
ilakkameṉpatu oru periya ilakkam (eṇ).
kōṭi eṉpatu kōṭi (kaṭaici) yeṇ.

kōṭi tamiḻ. kōṭṭi, Skt. kuṭōṭu Arabic, crore, E.
vaukīkam. ulakam-lōkam-laukīkam.
ulaku + am = ulakam.
ulaku = ulappatu:  ulattal = aḻital.
`kāla mulakam' eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl, ulakam eṉṉum col tolkāppiyarukku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya teṉṟum, centamiḻccol eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
ulakam eṉpataṉ tiripākiya lōkam eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻi yil laukīkam eṉat tattitāntamākum.
cāmam. camam-cāmam.
iravu mūṉṟu camapākamākap pakukkappaṭṭatiṉāl, ovvoru pākamum cāmameṉappaṭṭatu.
oru cāmam pattu nāḻikai.
cāmam-yāmam.  ca-ya pōli.
mūṉṟu cāmaṅkaḷum mutaṟcāmam, naṭuccāmam, kaṭaiccāmam eṉa ulakavaḻakkilum, mālaiyāmam, naḷḷiyāmam, vaikaṟaiyāmam eṉac ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum kūṟappaṭum.
yāmam eṉpatu ciṟappāka naṭuc cāmattaik kuṟikkum.
ciṉṉam. ciṉṉam=aṭaiyāḷam.
`ciṉṉam allāk kālai yāṉa'
`eṇṇiṭai yiṭṭuca ciṉṉaṅ kuṉṟiyum'
eṉac ciṉṉam eṉpatu kalippāviṉ ōruṟuppup peyarākat tolkāppiyattuṭ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl, tamiḻc collāta laṟiyappaṭum.
kuṭai, koṭi mutaliyavai araca aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷātaliṉ ciṉṉa meṉappaṭum.
araca ciṉṉaṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟum ceyyuḷ ciṉṉappū. ciṉṉam- sign (Eng) oruvariṉ aṭaiyāḷamākiya kaiyeḻuttu. sign-signal.

13. vaṭamoḻi palvakaic coṟperukkam
āriyar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantatiliruntu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ kālam varai, tamiḻ vaḷarcci yaṭaiyavillai. ēṟkeṉavē amaintirunta pala coṟkaḷum ovvoṉṟāka vaḻakku vīḻttap paṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyō tamiḻai iṉṟiyamaiyāta tuṇaiyākak koṇṭu, tamiḻukkē māṟāka iṭaiyaṟātu mēṉmēlum col vaḷarcci yaṭaintu vantiruk kiṉṟatu.

14. iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷ
samvartta, āvartta eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa mukiṟpeyarkaḷum, caṅkarāparaṇam, nātanāmakkiriyai eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paṇṇup peyarkaḷum, ivaipōṉṟa piṟavum, iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷām.
āṭpeyarkaḷ ikkālattiṟ perumpālum iṭukuṟikaḷākavē eṅkum iṭappeṟu kiṉṟaṉa. tārakākṣaṉ, kamalākṣaṉ eṉpaṉa pōla vaṭamoḻip purāṇaṅkaḷil varum paṭaippup peyarkaḷ iṭu kuṟiyuḷ iṭukuṟiyākum. pañcatantirak kataikaḷuḷ varum puṉai peyarkaḷum ittakaiyaṉavē.  (va.va.)
vaṭamoḻi vērccol tiraṭṭiṉ kuṟaipāṭukaḷ
(1) coṉṉilai
coṉmūlam vēr (Root), aṭi (Stem), mutaṉilai (Theme) eṉa mūvakaippaṭum. vērum mutalvēr, vaḻivēr, cārpuvēr eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. mutal vērukku mūlam muḷaiyākum.  muḷaikku mūlam vittē. aṭi eṉpatu kavaiyuṅ kompuṅ kiḷaiyum pōttuṅ kuccumākap piriyum. ivaiyellām tamiḻ pōṉṟa iyaṉmoḻiyilēyē teḷivāyk kāṇappeṟum.
vaṭavar tiripuṟṟa mutaṉilaikaḷaiyum coṟkaḷaiyumē vērccollākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.

(15) tamiḻ mūlam maṟaikkappaṭṭuḷḷamai
nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa vaṭacoṟkaṭku mūlam allatu mutaṉilai tamiḻilirukkavum, avaṟṟaik kuṟiyātu vaṭacolliṉ peyar vaṭiviṉiṉṟē viṉaiccollaic ceyaṟkaiyākat tirittiruk kiṉṟaṉar. camaṟkirutam ulaka vaḻakkilillāta ilakkiya naṭai moḻiyātaliṉ (Literary dialect), atiṟ peyaraṭi, viṉaikaḷai, viruppampōl mika eḷitākat tirittuk koḷḷalām. `tātupāṭa' eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi vērc coṟṟiraṭṭiṉ āciriyar, tantu puṉainturaittal eṉṉum uttiyai ivvakaiyil tārāḷa mākak kaiyāṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
e-ṭu: piṇṭam eṉṉum peyar piṭi eṉṉum tamiḻ viṉaiyiṉ muṉaṉai vaṭivāṉa piṇṭi eṉpatiṉṟu tirintirukkavum, tātu pāṭam ataṟkup piṇṭ eṉṟoru ceyaṟkai mūla viṉaiyaip paṭaittu, kaṭṭiyāka allatu uruṇṭaiyākat tiraḷ, oṉṟucēr, poruntu, kūṭu eṉṟu poruḷūraittuḷḷatu. itu peyaraṭi viṉai eṉpatai "prob. Nom. fr. next." eṉṟu mā. vi. a. kuṟittiruttal kāṇka. Nom. = Nominal verb. (va.va.)
viyākaraṇam eṉṉum col viḷakkam
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum teṉ coṟkaḷātalāl, toṉṟutoṭṭu nūlum vaṉappum eṉa iru pāṟpaṭṭa tamiḻc ceyyuṭṭokutikaṭkē muṟaiyē peya rāki vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku nēr vaṭa coṟkaḷ  viyā karaṇam (vyākaraṇa), cākittiyam (sāhitya) eṉpaṉavākum.
vyākaraṇa eṉṉum camaṟkirutac col vi+ā+kru eṉṉum mukkūṟṟuk kūṭṭuc col.
`vi'3 eṉpatu, vēṟu allatu tuṇṭāka eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭu;  `ā' eṉpatu colvēṟupaṭuttalaṉṟit taṉak keṉa oru poruḷillā iṭaiyoṭṭu, kru eṉpatu cey eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum viṉai mutaṉilai.
vyākru=tuṇṭu   cey,  vēṟu piri, paku, kūṟupaṭuttu, viḷakku.
vyākaraṇa=vēṟupaṭuttam, kūṟupaṭuppu (Analysis), viḷakkam, ilakkaṇam (Grammer).
`vi'3 eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu tvi (iraṇṭu eṉṉum col viṉiṉṟu tirintirukkalāmeṉṟu mā. vi. a. kūṟum. iraṇ ṭākkutal eṉṉum karuttiṉiṉṟu pakuttaṟ karuttu tōṉṟuvatu iyalpē. āyiṉ, viḷ eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ kaṭaik kuṟai yāka `vi' eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi muṉṉoṭṭaik koḷvatē mikap poruttamām.
o. nō:  al-a, kuḷ-ku, nal-na, poḷ-po.

16. vaṭavar kāṭṭum vērccoṟkaḷ vērccoṟkaḷaṉmai
vētamoḻi vaḻakkaṟṟa kalavait tirimoḻiyātalālum, camaṟkirutam araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭaimoḻiyāta lālum, vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu perumpālum vērccoṟ kaḷai aṟiya muṭiyātu.
iṭaikaḻi eṉpatu muṟaiyē ṭēkaḻi, ṭēḻi, rēḻi eṉak koccait  tiripukaḷil vērccollum poruḷum tōṉṟa villai. iṅṅaṉamē, tamiḻ pōṉṟa iyaṉmoḻiyil tōṉṟum vērccoṟkaḷ āriyam pōṉṟa tirimoḻikaḷil attuṇait teḷivāyt tōṉṟā; avaṟṟuḷḷum, tiripil tiripākiya camakirutattiṟ perumpālum tōṉṟavē tōṉṟā.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tam moḻiyait tēvamoḻi yeṉṟu kāṭṭi, tamiḻai eṉṟum ataṟku aṭippaṭuttal vēṇṭi, vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ mutaṉilaikaḷai allatu mutalaicaikaḷai yellām vērccolleṉṟu koṇṭu avaṟṟaip pirittuk kōvaip paṭutti mottam 1,750 vērccoṟkaḷeṉak kaṇakkiṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuṭ pala palporu ḷorucoṟka ḷātalāl, mēlaiyar avaṟṟai āyntu kaṇṭu vērccoṟṟokaiyai 2,490 ākap perukkiyuḷḷaṉar. āyiṉ, ciṟanta ārāycciyāḷar ellā vērccoṟkaḷaiyum 12-iṟkuḷ aṭakkiviṭalām eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar.
mēlaiyar vaṭamoḻi vērccoṟ ṟiraṭṭukaḷiṉ kuṟai pāṭṭai oruvāṟuṇarnta varāyiṉum, tamiḻaic cevvaiyāyk kallāmaiyāl avaṟṟait tiruttiyamaikkum āṟṟalaṟṟavarā yuḷḷaṉar.

paṇṭait tamiḻar cuṟṟuk kaṭalōṭikaḷā (Circumnavigators) yiruntamaiyāl, vaṭamuṉaiyil orōvorukāl tōṉṟum miṉṉoḷiyaik kaṇṭu ataṟku vaṭavai allatu vaṭavaṉal eṉap peyariṭṭiruntaṉar. kumarik kaṇṭattiṟ pala pēraḻivukaḷ nērntatiṉāl, ūḻiyiṟutiyaḻiviṟku avvaṭavaiyē kāraṇa meṉṟuṅ karutiṉar.
teṉmuṉaiyilum vaṭavai pōṉṟa oḷi tōṉṟumēṉum, paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻiyuṇṭu pōṉamaiyāl, ataippaṟṟiya ilakkiyak kuṟippum iṟantupaṭṭatu.
"Aurora, n Luninous atmospheric (prob. electrical) phenomenon radiating from earth's northern (borealis) or southern (australis) magnetic pole" eṉṟu COD kūṟutal kāṇka.  (va.va.)
vaṭavar vaṭavai eṉṉuñ collai vaṭacollākak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, vaṭapā eṉṉum ataṉ vaṭamoḻi vaṭiviṟkup peṇ kutirai eṉṟu poruḷiruttalāl, ataṟkēṟpa oru kataiyaik kaṭṭi vaṭapākṉi eṉṟum vaṭapā mukākṉi eṉṟum peyariṭṭu, peṇkutirai mukattil tōṉṟiya neruppeṉṟu poruḷum virittuviṭṭaṉar. akkatai varumāṟu:-
kirutavīriyaṉ makkaḷ, piruku muṉivar īṭṭivaitta peruñ celvattaik kavaravēṇṭi am muṉivariṉ makkaḷaiyum pērap piḷḷaikaḷaiyuṅ karu nilaiyiṅ koṉṟatiṉāl, akkuṭumpattaic cērnta orēyoru peṇ taṉ karuvait taṉ toṭaiyil maṟaittu vaittiruntu, atu piṟantapiṉ aurva eṉap peyariṭ ṭāṉ.  (ūru=toṭai.  aurva=toṭaiyiṟ piṟantavaṉ). koṉṟa variṭam paḻivāṅkum poruṭṭut tavañceyya, attavatti ṉiṉṟu ulakaiyē aḻittuviṭattakka oru perun tī kiḷarn teḻuntatu. ataik kaṇṭu avaṉ muṉṉōr añci ataik kaṭalil iṭṭuviṭumāṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭa, avaṉum ataṟkiṇaṅki aṅṅaṉamē iṭṭu viṭṭāṉ.  atu oru peṇkutirai muka vaṭivu koṇṭu aṅkut taṅkiṟṟu.  
mā. vi. a. ikkataiyaiyum varaintu, vaṭapākṉi eṉṉuñ coṟku, "`mare's fire,' submarine fire or the fire of the lower regions (fabled to emerge from a cavity called the 'mare's mouth' under the sea at the South pole") eṉṟu viḷakkamuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷatu.

vaṇṇam (1)
ceyyuḷ olippumuṟai ōcai, cantam, vaṇṇam eṉa mūvakaip paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ vaṇṇam eṉpatu, ellā aṭiyum māttirai yum eḻuttiṉamum cīrum ottuvarap pāṭuvatu. atai nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa vakaiyil pallāyiramākappāṭuvatu iṟaivaṉ tiruvaruḷ poṟṟavarkkē iyalum  (ta.i.va.48)
vaṇṇam (2)

uruvap palpū vorukoṭi vaḷaii  (neṭunal.kakaṅa)  (ti.ma:753)
vaḷ-vaṇ-vaṇṇam= (ka) vaḷaitteḻutum eḻuttu allatu ōviyam. eḻuttilum ōviyattilumuḷḷa kōṭukaḷellām perumpālum vaḷaintiruppataiyum, kōṭu, vari, varai eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ vaḷaitaṟ karuttaiyē aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭiruppataiyum, nōkkuka.

vaḷ-(var)-vari-varaṇam (vari+aṇam=niṟam, niṟam paṟṟiya kulavakai. varital=eḻututal. varittal=eḻututal. vaṇṇam-vaṇṇi-vaṇṇaṉai. varaṇam-- varaṇi -- varaṇai. o. nō: tiḷ=tiṇ--tiṇṇai; tiḷ--(tir)--tiraḷ-- tiraḷai-- tiraṇai. tiṇṇai tiraṇai eṉpaṉa pōṉṟē, vaṇṇam varaṇam allatu vaṇṇaṉai varaṇaṉai eṉpaṉavum, orē vēriṉa vāyiṉum vevvēṟayiṉiṉṟu kiḷaittiruttalai nōkkuka.
vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl
iṉṟu taṉik kalaiyāka vaḻaṅkiyum vaḷarntuvarum moḻinūl 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mēṉāṭukaḷil muḷait teḻuntatēṉum. ataṟku vittut tamiḻnāṭṭil ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē iṭappaṭṭateṉpatu
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉivē  (tol.640)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (tol.877)
iyaṟcol tiricol ticaiccol vaṭacoleṉ	
ṟaṉaittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē  (tol.880)
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē  (tol.884)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷāl uṇaralākum.
mēṉāṭukaḷil kaṭanta mun nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarkkappeṟṟu varum moḻinūl, toṭakkattil āṅkilattil, (Science of speech, Science of  Language, Linguistic Science, Linguistics, Glottology, Philology) eṉap pala peyarkaḷaip peṟṟiruntatu. ivaṟṟuḷ (Lin-guistics, Philology) eṉṉum iraṇṭē iṟutiyil nilaipeṟṟaṉa.
moḻinūl, orē camayattil, varalāṟu taḻuviyatākavum oppu nōkkiyatākavum pala kūṟukaḷākap pakukkappaṭātatākavum uḷḷatu. mākkacu mullar (Max Muller), viṟṟiṉi (Whitney), cecup perceṉ (Jespersen) mutaliya moḻi nūṟ pēraṟiñarellām mēṟkūṟiya muṟaiyilēyē moḻi nūlai vaḷarttu vantaṉar. āyiṉ, aṇmaiyil, cila mēlai moḻi nūlaṟiñar, ciṟappāka amerikkar, moḻinūlai,
1. vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl (Descriptive Linguistics) 
2. varalāṟṟu moḻinūl (Historical Linguistics)
3. oppiyal moḻinūl (Comparative Linguistics) 
eṉa mukkūṟākap pakuttu, avaṟṟuḷ vaṇṇaṉai moḻi nūlaiyē ciṟappāka vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ pala kuṟaikaḷ uṇmaikku māṟākavum tamiḻukkuk kēṭākavum uḷḷaṉa. avaiyāvaṉa.
1. varalāṟu taḻuvātatu
varalāṟu ekkalaikkum aṭippaṭaiyāy maṭṭumiṉṟi mutukan taṇṭāyumuḷḷateṉpatu, ulakaṟinta uṇmai. kalaikaḷuḷḷum, moḻinūl varalāṟṟiyalai iṉṟiyamaiyātu taḻuviyateṉpatu collāmalē peṟappaṭum. āyiṉum, varalāṟṟut toṭar piṉṟi uṇmai taḻuvāta uyiraṟṟa nilaiyil vaṇṇaṉai moḻi nūl vaḷarkkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. itu, an nūlāriṉ muyaṟci yiṉmai, kalaiyuṇarcciyiṉmai, naṭunilaiyiṉmai ākiyavaṟ ṟuḷ oṉṟaiyē kāṭṭum.
2. āriya aṭippaṭai koṇṭatu
muntiyal cemmoḻiyākiya tamiḻai ārāyāmaiyāl, moḻi nūl tiṟavukōl tamiḻilēyē potintu kiṭappatai aṟiyātu. tiripil tiripum ceyaṟkaiyil ceyaṟkaiyum āriyattiṉ muṭiyu mākiya camaṟkirutattai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, moḻinūṟ ceytikaḷaiyum moḻikaḷaiyum moḻiyuṟuppukaḷaiyum muṉ piṉṉākavum kīḻmēlākavum muṟaipiṟaḻak kūṟivaruvatu vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl.
tamiḻ āriyattiṉ aṭi eṉpatai iṉṉum mēlaiyar uṇarn tilar.
3. ellā moḻikaḷum iṭukuṟit tokutikaḷē eṉpatu
tamiḻpōlum iyaṉmoḻiyai ārāyātu āriyamākiya tirimoḻikaḷaiyē āyntatiṉāl, ellāmoḻic coṟkaḷum iṭukuṟikaḷē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa muṭiviṟku vantuḷḷaṉar iṟṟai amerikka moḻinūlāciriyar.
tamiḻil iṭukuṟic collē illaiyeṉpataṟku . "ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē."  eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā voṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām. "moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā" eṉpatu, col vērpporuḷ pārtta vuṭaṉ allatu mikat teḷivākat tōṉṟātu (cila coṟkaḷil) eṉap poruḷpaṭumēyaṉṟit tōṉṟavē tōṉṟātu eṉap poruḷpaṭuvataṉṟu.
iyaṟcoṟkaḷil teḷivākat tōṉṟum vērpporuḷ tiricoṟkaḷil maṟaintupōm eṉṉum uṇmaiyaik kīḻk kuṟitta coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu aṟika.
iyaṟcol	tiricol
iṭaikaḻi	rēḻi
veṇṇey	veṉṉa (teluṅku)
Episcope	Biscop
moḻi tōṉṟiya vakaiyai viḷakki niṟpatu ulaka muḻumai yiṉum tamiḻ oṉṟē.
4. coṟkaḷ tammaḷaviṟ poruḷuṇarttātu avaṟṟiṉ muṟaip pāṭṭiṉālēyē poruḷuṇartuvaṉa eṉpatu,
itu moḻi tōṉṟiya muṟaiyai aṟiyāmaiyāl nērnta tavaṟākum.
ikkālattum, kuḻantaikaḷum nōyāḷiyarum ayaṉmoḻit tuvakka māṇavarum cila vēḷaikaḷil oru collālēyē tam karuttait terivittal kāṇka.
5. āyiram āṇṭiṟkoru muṟai nūṟṟiṟkup pattoṉpatu viḻukkāṭu ellāc coṟkaḷum māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu.
ellā moḻikaḷilum, aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ uṭpaṭa, āyiram āṇṭiṟ korumuṟai nūṟṟukku 19 mēṉi ellāc coṟkaḷum vaḻakkaṟṟup putuccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu amerikka moḻinūlār koṇṭu, ivvaṭippaṭaiyil coṉmāṟṟak kālakkaṇippu (Glotto Ghronology) eṉa oru kaṇippu muṟaiyum vakuttirukkiṉṟaṉar. atu varumāṟu:
"oru moḻi irukiḷaikaḷākap pirintuviṭiṉ, āyiram āṇ ṭiṟkup piṉ ovvoṉṟum mūlac coṟṟokutiyil nūṟṟiṟkup pattoṉpatu iḻantuviṭṭu, eṇpattoṉṟē koṇṭu niṟkum. mūlaccoṟkaḷ 200 āyiṉ, ovvoṉṟilum 162 eñci niṟkum. āyiṉ, orē coṟṟokutiyai iraṇṭum iḻakkum eṉṟu karutak kāraṇamillai, oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟu koṇṭu niṟpatilum iḻantatilum taṉittaṉi nūṟṟukku 81 tāṅki niṟkum eṉpatu perumpāl nikaḻak kūṭiyatu. iṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ov voṉṟum mūlap potuc coṟ ṟokutiyil 132 allatu nūṟṟukku 66 koṇṭu niṟkum . ik kaṇippiṉpaṭi, iru moḻi kaḷil, aṭippaṭaic coṟ ṟokutiyil nūṟṟukku 66 iṉac collāyiruntāl avai pirintupōy āyiram āṇṭāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum, 44 iṉaccollā yiruppiṉ, perumpālum īrāyiram āṇṭā yirukku meṉṟum, aṟintukoḷḷalām."
ik kaṇippiṉpaṭi ovavoru moḻiyum aiyāyiram āṇṭiṟ kuḷ muṟṟum māṟiviṭa  vēṇṭum. ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya tolkāppiyam iṟṟaikku 2500 āṇṭukaṭku muntiyatu. atiluḷḷa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ attaṉaiyum iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅki varuvatōṭu.
ai uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu  (tol.31)
neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi  (tol.43)
paṉṉī ruyirum moḻimuta lākum.  (tol.59)
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ettuṇaiyō nūṟpākkaḷum, iṟṟai muṟai yil amaintu ellārum eḷituṇarak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, mēṟkāṭṭiya amerikkac coṉmāṟṟak kālakkaṇippu muṟṟum puraipaṭṭateṉka.
6. ellā moḻikaḷum tiruntiyavaiyē eṉpatu
"iyaṉmoḻi yeṉṟum tirimoḻi yeṉṟum, tiruntiyamoḻi yeṉṟum tiruntāmoḻi yeṉṟum, moḻikaṭkuḷ pākupā ṭillai. ellām tiruntiyavaiyē, curuṭṭu eṉpatu teluṅkil cuṭṭu eṉṟiruppiṉ, ataṟku atu cariyē. poṉ eṉpatu kaṉṉa ṭattil hoṉṉu eṉṟiruppiṉ, ataṟku atu tiruntiyatē". ivvāṟu koḷvatu vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl.
"uḷḷataic coṉṉāl noḷḷaik kaṇṇikkup pol lāppu."  teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavai tamiḻiṉ tiripu eṉṟāl, teluṅkarkkum kaṉṉaṭarukkum varuttam piṟakkum. ātalāl, irucārārkkum potuvākak kūṟuvatu ciṟantamuṟai eṉpatu, vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūlār karuttu. im muṟaiyoṭṭic cila tamiḻp pērāciriyarum, tiraviṭam eṉpatu, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya pala moḻikaḷiṉ tokutip peyar. avaiyellām ōriṉamoḻi kaḷ eṉṟu koḷvatallatu tamiḻ piṟa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ tāyeṉak kūṟuvatu poruntātu," eṉak kaṭṭuraippar.
taṉippaṭṭavar ceytikaḷil oruvar, oruvar kuṟṟaṅkuṟaikaḷai maṟaikkalāmēyaṉṟi, oru nāṭṭu makkaḷellārkkum potu vāṉa moḻit tuṟaiyilum kalaittuṟaiyilum kuṟṟaṅkuṟai kaḷai maṟaippatu paṇpāṭaṉṟu.
7. ilakkaṇattaip paḻippatu
ellā mutumoḻikaḷilum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ aḻakiya muṟai yil aṟiñarāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ arumaiyaṟiyātu, avaṟṟai varaituṟaiyiṉṟip paḻittu, māṇavarai mayakkuvaṉavum poruṭciṟappaṟṟaṉavum ōrīṟu koṇṭaṉavum oṉṟaiyoṉṟu paṟṟuvaṉavumāṉa pala kuṟiyīṭukaḷaip putitāyp paṭaittu avaṟṟaic cevvaiyāy viḷakkavum, iyalātu iṭarppaṭuvatu, vaṇṇaṉai moḻi nūlāciriyar vaḻakkamāka iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. itu mikap paranta ceytiyātaliṉ, itai virikkātu immaṭṭil niṟuttukiṉṟēṉ.
8. olikkuṟikaḷai (Phonetic Symbols) aḷavaippaṭuttātatu
vaṇṇaṉaimoḻi nūlārāl oppukkoḷḷappeṟum pala eḻuttoli nūlkaḷ, vevvēṟu kuṟikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai iṉṉum oru neṟippaṭuttiyilar.
oruvar ellā moḻikaḷaiyum tāymoḻiyākavuṭaiyār pōṉṟē pēcaviyalum eṉpatu, vaṇṇaṉai moḻi nūṟ koḷkaiyar tarukku. āyiṉ, itukāṟum oruvarum ataṉai meyppittuk kāṭṭiyilar.
9. kiḷai vaḻakkaip perukkuvatu
iṭavaḻakkum kulavaḻakkum paṟṟi ovvoru perumoḻiyilum cila kiḷaivaḻakkukaḷ (Dialects) ēṟpaṭuvatu iyalpē. āyiṉ, ñālaviyaṟ kiḷaivaḻakku (Geographical Dialect) eṉṟum, ūrk kiḷai vaḻakku (Dialect of a village town or city) eṉṟum, kūṭṭara viyaṟ kiḷaivaḻakku, (Social Dialect) eṉṟum, kuṭumpak kiḷai vaḻakku (Family Dialect) eṉṟum, taṉippaṭṭavar kiḷaivaḻakku (Individual Dialect) eṉṟum, vaṇṇaṉai toḻi nūlāṟ varam piṉṟi vakuttuk kāṭṭuvatu viyappiṉum viyappē.
10. pēcuvatē moḻi eṉpatu
pēccu vaḻakkiṉ uḷḷatē uṇmaiyāṉa moḻiyeṉṟum, eḻuttu vaḻakkiluḷḷatellām ēṭṭu moḻiyeṉṟum, tiruntiya vaḻakkeṉṟum koccai vaḻakkeṉṟum vēṟu pāṭillaiyeṉṟum, koccai vaḻakku evvaḷavu kēṭāyt tirintirippiṉum atellāṅ koḷḷat takkatēyeṉṟum, vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūlār ēṉait tiripuṭai moḻikaḷukkuk kūṟuvatu pōṉṟē iyaṉmoḻiyum ilakkaṇac cemmoḻiyu mākiya tamiḻiṟkum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
piṟa moḻikaḷiṭkillāta cemmaiyeṉṉum varampu tamiḻuk kuṇmaiyaiyum, iṭaikkālattil tamiḻ āriyattāl aḻip puṇṭataiyum, ikkālattum tamiḻ vaḷarcci pakaivarāl taṭup puṇṭu varuvataiyum, avar aṟiyār.
tamiḻ oruvaṉ eṅṅaṉam piḻaippaṭa pēciṉum, mēṭaiyēṟip pēcuṅkālum ēṭeṭutteḻutum pōtum ilakkaṇa naṭaiyaik kaiyāḷa vēṇṭumeṉpatu toṉṟutoṭṭu varum marapu.
eppaṭi eccoliṉ evvā ṟuyarntōr
ceppiṉar appaṭic cepputal marapē.
tamiḻnāṭṭil mikak koccaiyāyp pēcappaṭum māvaṭṭam vaṭārkkāṭu. aṅkum aṅkattu naṭaiyil mēṭaiyēṟip pēcuvārum nūleḻuvārum oruvarumilar. ittakaiya varam puṇmaiyālēyē tamiḻ iṉṟuvarai iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
iṭaikkālattil, tamiḻ citaikkappaṭṭamaiyālum, tamiḻk kāvalarāṉa pulavar piḻaippaṟṟup pōṉamaiyālum, nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa aruntamiḻc coṟkaḷ maṟaintu vēṟṟu moḻic coṟkaḷ vēṇṭātu pukuntuviṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, mukil, eḻili, mañcu, kāl, māl, vāṉ, eṉap pala coṟkaḷ iruppavum, mēkam eṉṉum vaṭacollē vaḻaṅkavum, mukil eṉṉum taṉ coṟku mēkam eṉṉum ayaṟ collāṟ poruḷ kūṟuvum nērntuviṭṭatu.  itu tamiḻar virumpi mēṟ koṇṭa nilaimaiyaṉṟu. āṇkaḷ peṇkaḷ eṉṉum vaḻaṅku tamiḻccoṟkaṭkup patilākap puruṣar tirīkaḷ eṉṉum vaḻaṅkā vaṭacoṟkaḷaip pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyaṅkaḷiṟ pukuttiyirup patum, vāṉoli eṉpatai ākācavāṇi eṉṟu māṟṟiyirup patum, tamiḻaik keṭuttaṟkum ataṉ vaḷarcciyait taṭuppataṟ kum ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷām.
tamiḻar takka vaḻikāṭṭiyiṉṟit tāymoḻi yuṇarcciyaṟṟup pōy, ēṟkaṉavē kāṟpaṅku vaṭacoṟkaḷaik kalantatoṭu iṉṟu araippaṅku āṅkilac coṟkaḷaiyum kalantu, iṭaiyiṭai pāracīkam arapi mutaliya piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷaiyum peytu, kalavai naṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkum kacaṭṭu moḻi. pallāyiram āṇṭu kaḷākap paṇpaṭuttic cīriyōr vaḷartta centamiḻākumō!
vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam mikka ik kālattil, iḷaiñar cilar tam takutiyaip perukki varuvāyai yuyartta vaṭa amerikkā ceṉṟu īrāṇṭiruntu mīṇṭu, ārāycciyeṉṉum peyarāl aruntamiḻaik kulaittuvaruvatu ettuṇai iraṅkat takka ceyti!
avarkaḷ eṉṉum colliṉ koccai vaṭivukaḷāṉa avaka, avuka, avika, aviya, aviyal avaṅka, avuṅka, aviṅka, avā eṉpavaṟṟaiyellām aḷavaiyākakkoṇṭu ārāyvatiṉum, nakarāṇmaik kuppaiyaik kiṇṭik kiḷaippatu naṉmai payakkumē!
tamiḻait tamiḻnāṭṭil takkārvāyk kallātu amerikkā ceṉṟu āyvataṟkut tamiḻolikaḷait tavaṟākap pativu ceyta olippeṭṭikaḷaṉṟi vēṟu aṅku eṉṉuḷḷatu? ulaka vaḻakkum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkum ārāyātu cila olikkuṟikaḷai maṭṭum amerikkāviṟ kaṟṟuvantatiṉāl eṉṉa payaṉ? ak kuṟikaḷai iṅkiruntē kaṟkalāmē!
tamiḻar pēcuvatellām tamiḻeṉiṉ, kūl, kālēj, kiḷā, ṭiviṣaṉ, pīriyaṭu, heṭmāṭṭar, cār, (Sir) puk, nōṭpuk, pēppar, peṉ, peṉcil, rappar, pēkku, (bag) kārṭiyaṉ, līvu, lēṭ (late) ṭirāyiṅ, cayaṉcu mutaliya colkaḷellām tamiḻātal vēṇṭumē! aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, tamiḻ āṅkilattiṉ kiḷaimoḻiyākavaṉṟō māṟiviṭum! aṅṅaṉam māṟāmai aṉaivarum aṟivar. ātalāl, iṭaikkālat tiruṇnilaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa kēṭṭaiyum ataṉvaḻi vanta tīṅkukaḷaiyum aṟavē nīkki, tamiḻaip paṇṭupōl tūymaippaṭuttuvatē, aṟivā rāycciyum urimaiyuṇarcciyum mikka iṟṟait tamiḻark kēṟṟatām.
iṉi, malaivāṇarāṉa tiraviṭarellām paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷeṉ ṟum, avarkaḷ pēcuvaṉa muntiya moḻikaḷeṉṟum, tamiḻ valliṉam vaṭamoḻi aivarukka mutalolikaḷai yottateṉ ṟum, tamiḻ arivari vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkaip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉṟum, cila tavaṟāṉa karuttukaḷ mēlai moḻi nūlaṟiña riṭait toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaiyel lām maṟuppiṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittalākum.
āyiṉum, kuṟiñci nilattiluḷḷa māntar tamiḻar nākarika vaḷarcci yiṉ mutal nilaiyait tāṅki niṉṟa kālam muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattilēyē kaṭantuviṭṭa teṉṟum, iṟṟai nāvalantēya malaivāṇarellām malaiyaṭi vārattil māṭu mēyttum uḻavuttoḻil ceytumvantu ak kālattu aṭik kaṭi nikaḻntuvanta pōrukkum koḷḷaikkum tappumāṟu malai mēṟceṉṟu vāḻntavar vaḻiyiṉareṉṟum; tamiḻ valliṉa volikaḷ vaṭamoḻi aivarukka mutalolikaḷiṉum melliya veṉṟum; tamiḻ neṭuṅ kaṇakkē vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟku mūlameṉṟum; īṇṭaikkaṟintu koḷka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, varalāṟṟaic ciṟitum nōkkātu, vaṭamoḻiyait talaimaiyākavum tiraviṭamoḻikaḷai yellām oṉṟōṭoṉṟu camamākavum koṇṭu, tamiḻukkuk kēṭu viḷaiyumāṟu koccaic coṟkaḷaiyum piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaiyum aḷavaippaṭuttuvatē vaṇṇaṉai moḻi nūliṉ iyalpeṉṟum, itu tamiḻaik kāṭṭik koṭuttut tannalam perukkum vāyp peṉṟum, kaṇṭukoḷka.
2. vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl vaḻukkaḷ
ka. aṭippaṭait tavaṟukaḷ
(1) tamiḻar kirēkka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu allatu naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarai yiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷ āvar eṉpatu.
(2) nīkarōvar āttirēliyar, muntu naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkaraiyar, naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkaraiyar, armīṉiyar, kāṇṭiṉēviyar ākiya aṟuvakaik kalavaiyiṉattār tamiḻar eṉpatu.
(3) vētamoḻiyum camaṟkirutamum oṉṟeṉpatu.
(4) camaṟkirutam paṇṭai ulakavaḻakku moḻi eṉpatu.
vētamoḻiyē vaḻakkaṟṟum pirākirutaṅ kalantum pōy viṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉ vētamoḻiyoṭu tamiḻaik kalantu ākkiya camaṟkirutam. ilakkiya naṭaimoḻiyē yaṉṟi ulaka vaḻakku moḻiyaṉṟu. ataṉ nilaimai paṭimaiyum pāvaiyum pōṉṟateṉa aṟika. atu piṟantatumillai; iṟantatumillai.
(5) camaṟkirutam āriyamūlam eṉpatu:
camaṟkirutam, kirēkkam, ilattīṉ ākiya ilakkiyac cem moḻikaḷai ciṟappākac camaṟkirutattai, āriya moḻikaṭku mūlamākak koṇṭē, kirim neṟiyīṭu eṉṉum āriya iṉamoḻi meymmāṟṟa vāypāṭu vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu kaṭai makaṉait talaimakaṉāka vaittu uṭaṉpiṟantār varicaiyait talai kīḻāka amaippatu pōṉṟatu.

val eṉṉum aṭi mal eṉpatiṉiṉṟu tirintiruppataiyum, val eṉpatiṉiṉṟu vāraṇam eṉpatuvaraiyum kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷellām nērē kōvaiyākat toṭarpu koṇṭirup pataiyum, vaṭamoḻiyil iṅṅaṉamiṉṟi iṭaiyiṭaiyuḷḷa orucila coṟkaḷē vaḻaṅkuvataiyum, perumpāṟ coṟkaḷ, tamiḻ ulaka vaḻakkil aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷāka viruppataiyum, `val varutta' eṉṉum iraṇṭaiyum vaṭamoḻiyār vevvēṟu mūlaṅkaḷākak kāṭṭuvataiyum,  nōkkuvārkku vaṭṭam eṉpatu tūya teṉcolleṉṟum, atuvē pirākirutattil vaṭṭa eṉṟum camaṟkirutattil varutta eṉṟum muṟaiyē tirintuḷḷa teṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat teriyalākum.

Valve (L.valva), vert (L.vertere) volve, (L.volvere), wallow (L.volvere), marina (L.mare, marinus) mutaliya paṟpala mēlaiyāriyac coṟ kaḷellām, vaḷ, vaḷavu, vaṭṭam, (varutta), vāri,  vāraṇam mutaliya teṉcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu tirintaṉavē.
orucila vaṭacoṟkaḷ pirākirutavāyilākat tamiḻil vantu tirintuḷḷatu uṇmaiyē-āyiṉ tamiḻukku ettuṇaiyum vēṇṭātaṉa eṉa aṟika.
(8) tamiḻ camaṟkirutattāl vaḷam peṟṟatu eṉpatu.
camaṟkirutac coṟkalappāl, tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṭ pala iṟantu paṭṭum pala vaḻakkiṟantum pala poruḷiḻantum allatu māṟi yumē uḷḷaṉa.
(9) intiya nākarikam āriyaratu eṉpatu.
āriya varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum aḻikkap paṭṭatiṉālēyē, camaṟkirutanūlkaḷ mutaṉūlkaḷum mūlanūl kaḷum pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
(10) āriyam taṉimoḻi eṉpatu.
āriyam tirimoḻi eṉpatai, eṉ The Primary Classical Language of the World eṉṉum nūlaik kaṇṭu teḷika.
(11) vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷaṉavellām āriyam eṉpatu.
āriyar varumuṉ vaṭaintiya vāṇaruṭ perumpālār tiraviṭarēyātaliṉ, vaṭaintiyak kalai nākarikak kūṟukaḷuṭ perumpakuti tiraviṭamē eṉa aṟika.
(12) āriyam ellāvakaiyilum aḷavaippaṭṭatu eṉpatu.
mēṉāṭṭu nākarikam munnūṟṟāṇṭuk kālattatē yātaliṉ, ataṟkumuṉ ulakiṟ ciṟantiruntatu tamiḻa nākarikamē.
(13) nākarikam kuṟuntalai (Brachycephalic) makkaḷiṭattilēyē ciṟantu tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu.
tamiḻa nākarikam kumarināṭṭilēyē peruvaḷarcciyaṭaintu viṭṭataṉāl, māntaṉ nākarikam nīḷtalai (Dolichocephalic) makkaḷiṭattum tōṉṟakkūṭiyatēyām.
(14) koḷuvunilai teṉṉintiya allatu cittiya moḻikaṭkē uriyatu eṉpatu.
koḷuvunilai (Agglutination) eṉpatu piṟaviṉaikkēyaṉṟi ellāviṉaikaṭkum peyarkaṭkum uriyataṉṟu.
teṉmoḻiyilum cittiya moḻiyilum koḷuvunilai piṟa viṉaic colliṉ piṉ nikaḻiṉ, āriyamoḻiyil atu viṉaic colliṉ muṉ nikaḻkiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu: forest, afforest, disafforest, anti-disafforestation, re-anti-disafforestation. 
(15) colliṉ īṟumutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu piriyātu ataṉoṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu niṟpatu, colliṉ tiripu mutircciyai yaṉṟi moḻiyiṉ uyarnilaiyaik kuṟikkātu: pututtaṇṭiṉ pūṇ piriyak kūṭiya nilaiyaiyum turuppiṭitta paḻantaṇṭiṉ pūṇ piriyamuṭiyāta nilaiyaiyum oppunōkkik kāṇka.
(16) irumaiyeṇ moḻiyiṉ uyarvaḷarcciyaik kāṭṭum eṉpatu.
anākarika māntar pēcum muṇṭāmoḻikaḷilum irumai yeṇ iruppatāl atu moḻiyiṉ ciṟappu vaḷarcciyaik kuṟikkātu.
(17) coṟpāl allatu ilakkaṇappāl makkaḷiṉ uruvalipput (Imagination) tiṟaṉaik kāṭṭum eṉpatu.
uyirillāp poruḷkaṭku uyirttaṉmaiyūṭṭuvatu ciṟu piḷḷaikaṭkum iyalpām.
(18) col iyalpākap poruḷuṇarttum eṉpatu.
oru cila olikkuṟippukkaḷaṉṟip piṟacoṟkaḷellām kaṟṟā laṉṟip poruḷuṇarttā.
(19) moḻiyalaku (Unit of speech) coṟṟoṭar allatu colliyam (Sentence) eṉpatu.
ovvoru collākavē moḻi tōṉṟiyiruppataṉālum, oru karuttai orutaṉiccollum uṇarttumātalālum collē moḻiyalakām.
(20) eṇṇattiṟku moḻi iṉṟiyamaiyāta teṉpatu.
ūmaiyar uḷḷattilum eṇṇam nikaḻvatālum vilaṅku paṟavaikaḷum eṇṇi viṉai ceyvatākavē terivataṉālum, moḻiyiṉṟiyum eṇṇam nikaḻak kūṭiyatē.
u. neṟimuṟaiya tavaṟukaḷ
(1) ellā moḻikaḷum iṭukuṟit tokutikaḷē eṉpatu. tamiḻil ellāc coṟkaḷum vērp poruḷuṇarttuvatālum,
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē.   (610)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.  (877)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatālum, inneṟimuṟai tavaṟāṉa tām.
(2) ellā moḻikaḷum āyiravāṇṭiṟ korumuṟai aṭip paṭaiccol uṭpaṭa muṟṟum māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu.
tamiḻ pallāyiram āṇṭāka māṟātiruppatāl, ituvum tavaṟāṉatē.
(3) potumakkaḷ pēcuvatē moḻi eṉpatu.
varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta paṇṭaik kālattilēyē, uyirum uyirum uyirillatum uyiruḷḷamākavuḷḷa mūvakai iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷaiyoppa, uyirum meyyum uyirmeyyum eṉa eḻuttolikaḷai mutaṉmutal mūvakaiyākap pakuttu, ataṟ kēṟpa mūvakai vaṭivamaittu. orolikku orē variyum oru varikku orē oliyumāka neṭuṅkaṇakku vakuttu, pēcukiṟapaṭiyē eḻutiyum eḻutukiṟapaṭiyē pēciyum poruḷkaḷaip pakuttaṟi vuḷḷatum illatum eṉa irutiṇaiyāka vakuttu ataṟkēṟpac colvaṭivukaḷai yamaittum; ceyyuḷilēyē pēcuntiṟaṉai vaḷarttuk karuttukkaḷaiyellām meyppāṭu tōṉṟa veḷippaṭuttaṟkēṟṟa palvakaip pirivu koṇṭa nālvakaip pākkaḷaippaṭaittu, ellā ilakkiyattaiyum ceyyuḷilēyē iyaṟṟiyum: eḻuttu col yāppaṇiyoṭu iṉṟum piṟa moḻikaṭkillāta poruḷilakkaṇattai vakuttum; collum coṟṟoṭarum  eṉṟum ilakkaṇavaṭivu citaiyātiruntaṟ poruṭṭuc centamiḻ eṉṉum varampiṭṭum; moḻittuṟai yil oppuyarvaṟṟa nākarimaṭaintavar tamiḻarē. avar iṭṭa cemmai eṉṉum varampē, pallāyiram āṇṭiṉ piṉpum piṟa moḻikaḷ pōl urutteriyātavāṟu tiriyātu ulaka vaḻakkaḻin toḻintu maṟaiyātum, tamiḻaikkākkum vallaraṇāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu. ivvaraṇaic vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūliṉ peyarāl takarkkappārkkiṉṟaṉar.
vaḻakkeṉap paṭuva tuyarntōr mēṟṟē
nikaḻcci yavarkaṭ ṭāka lāṉa.  (1592)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, pulavarum uyarntōrum pēcum vaḻakkē tamiḻukku ulakavaḻakkām. avai paṇṭaic ceyyuḷil iṭam peṟāta kāṭai, tavacam mutaliya peyarc coṟkaḷum ceytukoṇṭu, ceykiṉṟa mutaliya viṉaic colvāypāṭukaḷum aṉṉā, antā mutaliya iṭaiccoṟ kaḷum, kēḻppai (kēḻva varaku) taṭimpal mutaliya colvaṭivu kaḷum, oruvaraikkuṟikkum uyarvup paṉmaic coṟkaḷum. kūṭappōtal, koṭittaṭṭal mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷumēyaṉṟi, koccaic coṟkaḷum vaḻūuc coṟkaḷum iṭakkarc coṟkaḷum, ākā.
uṭaivakaiyilum ūrtivakaiyilum tamiḻar mēlaiyaraip piṉpaṟṟuvatu pōṉṟu, moḻittuṟaiyil mēlaiyarē tamiḻaraip piṉpaṟṟutal vēṇṭum.
(4) ellā naṭaimoḻikaḷum koḷḷattakkaṉavē eṉpatu.
itu ellāk kīḻmakkaḷiṉ tīya oḻukkaṅkaḷaiyum iḻinta paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatu pōṉṟatē. narikkuṟavaṉ naṭaimoḻiyai yellām ārāyvati lum nakarāṇmaik kuppaiyaik kiḷaippiṉ ētēṉum naṟporuḷ kiṭaippatu tiṇṇam.
muttiṟa moḻinūlpōl vaṇṇaṉaimoḻinūṟkup pōtiya ārāyccikkiṭam iṉmaiyālēyē, ittakaiya iḻitakaiya ārāycciyil iṟaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. itu "vēlaiyillāta mañcikaṉ (ampaṭṭaṉ) veṇkaḻutaiyaip piṭittuc ciraittāṉām." eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyaiyē niṉaivuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, naṭaimoḻikaḷaiyum oru varaituṟaiyiṉṟi, vaṭṭāra naṭaimoḻi (Regional Dialect), uḷḷūr naṭaimoḻi (Local Dialect) vakuppu naṭaimoḻi (Class Dialect), kumuka naṭaimoḻi (Communal Dialect), kuṭumpa naṭaimoḻi (Family Dialect) taṉippaṭṭavar naṭaimoḻi (Personal Dialect), eṉappalavāṟu vakuttuḷḷaṉar mēlaiyar. tamiḻarpōl oru ciṟanta ilakkaṇa naṭaimoḻiyai aḷavaippaṭuttip pēccuvaḻakkaiyum palukkal (uccarippu) muṟaiyaiyuṅ kaṭṭuppaṭuttātu, caṇṭik kutiraiyum paṭṭimāṭumpōl pōṉa pōṉa pōk kellāntiriya viṭṭu kīḻōr vaḻakkaiyum mēlōr vaḻakkoppak koṇṭu, avaṟṟai eḻuttuvaṭivil eḻutik kāṭṭavum vaḻiyiṉṟi iṭarppaṭuvatu ettuṇaip paṇpāṭaṟṟa ceyalām!
(5) malaivāṇar muntiyal māntar eṉpatu.
tamiḻariṉ muntiyal (Primitive) nilaimai kumari nāṭṭilēyē kaḻintuviṭṭamaiyālum, pōrukkuṅ koḷḷaikkun tappik kīḻiruntē cila vakuppār malaiyēṟi vāḻvatālum, malaivāṇar moḻikaḷellām moḻiyiṉ muntunilaiyaik kāṭṭātu tirivu nilaiyaiyē kāṭṭuvatālum, malaivāṇar muntiyal māntarallar eṉpatu teḷḷat teḷivām.
(e-ṭu) tamiḻ pirākuvī (peluccittāṉa malaiyaṭi vāṇar moḻi
cevi    kav
vāykaḷ  pāk
vaḻi-vayi-vāy-pāy-pā

(6) vāyppayiṟciyāl ellā moḻiyolikaḷaiyum oruvar payiṉṟu koḷḷalām eṉpatu.
cila moḻiyolikaḷ ayalnāṭṭu makkaḷāl olikka iyalā maiyāl, avaṟṟai avai vaḻaṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ pallāṇṭō cila talaimuṟaiyō kuṭiyiruntutāṉ payilamuṭiyum.
ōr amerikkar ceṉṉaiyil aṇṇāmalainakarukkup pukai vaṇṭic cīṭṭu vāṅkavēṇṭiyavar tavaṟāka olittataṉāl, āṉaimalaikku vāṅkic ceṉṟu viṭṭār.
(7) ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷuḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivukoṇṭu, piṟa vaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaiyum  ottamaivu (Analogy) muṟaiyil amaittuk koḷḷalām eṉpatu.
ilakkaṇa vakaiyil ottacoṟkaḷ potuvāka oroḻuṅku paṭṭiruppiṉum, ciṟupāṉmai neṟikku vilakkākavumiruppatāl, ottamaivumuṟai ellāviṭattuñ cellātākum.

coṟpuṇarcci

(8) oru kuṭumpa moḻikaḷiṉ potuviyalpaikkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ tāymoḻiyai mīḷa amaikkalām eṉpatu
itu, iṟantupōṉa oru tāyiṉ uruvappaṭattai avaḷ putalvi yariṉ uruvap paṭaṅkaḷai oppunōkki amaikkalām eṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
iṉi, vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūlār varalāṟṟāycciyai aṭiyōṭu viṭṭuviṭṭamaiyāl, oru kuṭumpa moḻikaḷiṉ mūlamoḻiyirukkumpōtē ataiyaṟiyātu vēṟoru putumoḻiyai ākkit tāyākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
(9) oru colliṉ vaṭivum poruḷum, orilakkiyattil munti variṉ muntiyavaiyeṉṟum, pintivariṉ pintiyavai yeṉṟum koḷvatu.
oru putumoḻiyil putitu putitākac collum poruḷum tōṉṟuvatāl, ataṟkuttāṉ inneṟimuṟai perumpālum ēṟkum. muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭainta oru mutumoḻiyil oru colliṉ poruḷkaḷ avai tōṉṟiya varicaiyilaṉṟi iṭattiṉ tēvaikkēṟpa āḷappeṟuvatāl, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ muṉmaiyiṉmaiyai aṟiyamuṭiyātu.
paḷḷi eṉṉum colliṉ irupatu poruḷkaḷuḷ iṭaip paṭṭatāṉa iṭam eṉpatu, iṉṟuḷḷa mutal toṉṉūlākiya tolkāppiyattil,
colliya paḷḷi eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ  (102)
eṉṟu vantiruttal kāṇka.
(10) ōreḻuttu iṉṉōreḻuttākat tiriyum tiripil oruvaḻip pōkkaiyē koḷvatu.
mēlaiyāriyamoḻikaḷiṟ potuvākak kakaram cakaramākat tirin tuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, intiya moḻikaḷil, ciṟappākat teṉmoḻi yil avai iruvaḻippōkkākavun tiriyum.
e-ṭu :
ca-ka	ka-ca
cempu-kempu	kīrai-cīrai(ma.)
cey-kai	keṭu-ceṭu(te.)
(11) coṉmāṟṟak kālakkaṇippāl (Glottochronology) iṉa moḻikaḷ oṉṟiṉiṉṟoṉṟu pirintupōṉa kālattaik kaṇit taṟiyalām eṉpatu.
itaṉ viḷakkam varumāṟu
oru moḻiyiṉ  mutaṟṟakai aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ āyira vāṇṭiṟkorumuṟai nūṟṟiṟku 19 viḻukkāṭu iṟantupaṭukiṉ ṟaṉa.  iru moḻikaḷ pirintupōy āyiravāṇṭiṟkup piṉ paḻam potuc coṟṟokutiyil nūṟṟumēṉi 19 iḻantu, 81 taṉittaṉi pōṟṟikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu vaittuk koḷvōm.  mūlappotut tokuti 200 coṟkoṇṭa teṉṟāl, mutalām moḻi iraṇṭām moḻiyaip pōṉṟē 162 coṟkaḷai vaittukkoṇṭirukkum. āyiṉ, iru moḻikaḷum orē tokutiyāṉa coṟkaḷai iḻakkumeṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamillai. perumpālum nērak kūṭiyapaṭi, iraṇṭām moḻi mutalām moḻi vaittukkoṇṭirukkum 162 coṟkaḷilum iḻantu pōṉa 38 coṟkaḷilum nūṟṟumēṉi 81-ait tāṅki niṟkum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, iru moḻikaḷum ēṟattāḻa mūlat tokutiyil nūṟṟumēṉi 132-ai allatu 66-aip potuvākak koṇ ṭirukkum eṉa etirpārkkalām.
ikkaṇippait talaikīḻāka māṟṟi, mūlac coṟṟokutiyil nūṟṟumēṉi 66 iru moḻikaṭku iṉac coṟkaḷāyiruntāl, avai pirintupōy āyiram āṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟum; nūṟṟu mēṉi 44 iṉac coṟkaḷāyiruntāl, avai īrāyiram āṇ ṭiṟkumuṉ pirintaṉaveṉṟum, aṟintukoḷḷalām.
itu ettuṇai uṉṉippait (Guess) taḻuviyateṉpataiyum eṅṅaṉam uṇmaikku māṟāka irukkakkūṭum eṉpataiyum, pakuttaṟivāḷar kaṇṭukoḷka.
(12) moḻittōṟṟam moḻinūṟku appāṟpaṭṭu uṭaliyal māntaṉūlait taḻuviyateṉpatu.
māntaṉ kīḻiṉattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟātu iṟaivaṉāṟ paṭaikkap peṟṟāṉeṉṟu koḷḷiṉ, ikkoḷkai muṟṟum vaḻuvātal kāṇka.
tamiḻpōlum iyaṉmoḻiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koḷḷātu, āriyam allatu camaṟkirutampōlum tiripil tiripāṉa moḻiyai aṭippaṭaiyākakkoṇṭu āyntatiṉ viḷaivē, mammarukkum mayalukkum karaṇiyam eṉa aṟika.
vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl vilakku
mēlai moḻinūlār, tamiḻiṉ toṉmai muṉmai yaṟiyātu camaṟkirutattai aṭippaṭaiyāka vaittāyntu moḻimūlattai aṟiya muṭiyātu muṭṭuṇṭu, iyaṟkaikkum uṇmaikkum uttikkum māṟāka, varalāṟṟut toṭarpai aṟavē vilakki aṟiviyaṉ muṟaippaṭātatum niṟaivaṟṟatumāṉa vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl eṉṉum putumoḻinūl muṟaiyait tōṟṟuvit tuḷḷaṉar. itu tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṟ pukuttap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
itu paḻantamiḻilakkaṇa nūlār iṭṭa centamiḻ varampu mīṟi avar vilakkiṉa koṭuntamiḻaiyum koccaittamiḻaiyum ēṟṟuk koḷvataṉālum, moḻikaḷiṉ koṭivaḻi muṟaiyai aṟiya muṭiyāvāṟu varalāṟṟai vilakkuvataṉālum, tamiḻiṉ vaḷarccikku nilaiyāṉa valutta muṭṭukkaṭṭaiyiṭuvataṉālum, coṟkaḷiṉ vēraiyum mūlap poruḷaiyum kāṇum muyaṟciyai aṟavē taṭuppataṉālum, tamiḻnāṭṭu muppalkalaik kaḻakaṅka ḷiṉṟum uṭaṉē vilakkappaṭaṟ pālatām.
atikārikaḷ itai uṭaṉaṭiyāyk kavaṉittu nīkki, kālṭuvel aiyar kālattu varalāṟṟū moḻi nūlai mīṇṭum pukuttutal ceyaṟpālatām.
vaṇṇāṉ tāḻi viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar: pāṇṭi nāṭṭil, vaṇṇārak kulattaiyum tāḻttakkappaṭṭa vakuppukkaḷaiyum cērnta ciṟuvar vaṇṇāṉ tuṟaiyil āṭaiyolippatu pōl naṭittāṭum āṭṭu vaṇṇāṉ tāḻi. tāḻi eṉpatu alacuvataṟkut tuṇikaḷ vaikkap paṭṭirukkum perumpāṉai.
āṭuvōr tokai:  potuvāka, nālvārkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi:  oru tuṇi mūṭṭai itaṟku vēṇṭuṅkaruviyām.
āṭiṭam:  ūrp poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa teruvilum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭu muṟai:  āṭuvār aṉaivarum ovvoru tuṇi pōṭa vēṇṭum. avai oru mūṭṭaiyākak kaṭṭappaṭum. atai oruvaṉ eṭuttut taṉ piṭariyil vaittuk koṇṭu, maruḷkoṇṭa tēvarāḷaṉ pōl āṭi varicaiyāy niṟkum ēṉaiyōruḷ oruvaṉmēl kaṇṇai mūṭikkoṇṭu eṟivāṉ. atu yār mēl viḻuntatō avaṉ atait taṉ piṭariyil vaittu irukai yālum, piṭittukkoṇṭu kīḻē uṭkāra vēṇṭum. mūṭṭaiyai eṟintavaṉ, atil iraṇṭōr aṭiyaṭittuviṭṭu, "kūḻ kuṭikkappōkiṟēṉ," eṉṟu collic caṟṟut tolai pōy mīḷvāṉ. ataṟkuḷ ēṉaiyōrellām ammūṭṭaiyil topput toppeṉṟu aṭittu makiḻvar. pōy mīṇṭavaṉ avaruḷ oruvaṉait toṭa muyalvāṉ. avaṉ aṇukiyavuṭaṉ aṉaivarum ōṭippōvar. avaṉ oruvaṉait toṭumuṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ mūṭṭaiyil aṭittuviṭiṉ, at toṭukai kaṇakkillai. iṉṉoruvaṉ mūṭṭaiyil aṭikkumuṉ oruvaṉait toṭṭuviṭiṉ, toṭappaṭṭavaṉ mūṭṭaiyai vāṅkit taṉ piṭarimēl vaittukkoṇṭu kīḻē uṭkāra vēṇ ṭum. muṉpu kīḻē uṭkārntiruntavaṉ. piṉpu piṟarait toṭu pavaṉāvaṉ.  muṉpu toṭṭavaṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu mūṭṭaiyil aṭittu viḷaiyāṭuvāṉ.
iṅṅaṉamē, neṭukalum, toṭappaṭṭavaṉ mūṭṭai vaittirup pavaṉākavum, mūṭṭai vaittiruntavaṉ toṭupavaṉākavum toṭṭavaṉ mūṭṭaiyil aṭittu viḷaiyāṭu pavaṉākavum, māṟikkoṇṭē varuvar.
putitāyt toṭupavaṉākum ovvoruvaṉum, mutalāvatu mūṭṭaiyil iraṇṭaṭiyaṭittu viṭṭuk kūḻ kuṭikkap pōvatum, piṉpu mīṇṭum piṟarait toṭuvatum, marapām.
mūṭṭaiyil aṭittu makiḻum ovvoruvaṉum piṉpu mūṭṭai tāṅki aṭivāṅku vataṟku iṭamiruttalāl, muṉpu piṟaṉ mutukil mūṭṭaiyiruntapōtu kaṇṇōṭṭamiṉṟi vaṉmaiyāy aṭittavaṉ, piṉpu taṉ viṉaiviḷaivai mikutiyāy aṟukka nērum.
viḷaiyāṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, avaṉavaṉ tuṇiyai avaṉavaṉ eṭuttukkoḷvāṉ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟa viḷakkam:  vaṇṇāruḷ ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru tuṟaiyuṇṭu. oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ tuṟaiyil veḷuppatu, vacatik kuṟaivumaṭṭumaṉṟi iḻappumuṇṭu. peṇ ṇum. paḻaṅ kālattil ovvoru vaṇṇāṉum vaṇṇārap pāṭṭam allatu vaṇṇārap pāṟai eṉṉum toḻil vari celutta vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ataṉāl oruvaṉ pāṟaiyil iṉ ṉoruvaṉ veḷuppatu, mikak kaṇṭippāyt taṭukkappaṭṭirut tal vēṇṭum. āyiṉum, oruvaṉ pāṟai mika vacatiyuḷḷatāka viruppiṉ, avaṉ tāṉ vaḻakkamāka uṇṇuṅ kūḻuṇṇac ceṉ ṟirukkumpōtu, piṟar avaṉ pāṟaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttiyiruppar. avaṉ mīṇṭu varumpōtu, avar oṭiyiruppar. avaṉ avarait turatti aṭittiruppaṉ, allatu kaṭumaiyāyt tiṭṭiyiruppāṉ.
icceyalaiyē iv viḷaiyāṭṭu uṇarttukiṉṟatu. toṭupa vaṉ  vaṇṇāṉaiyum, pāṟaiyaiyum, atil aṭippavar avaṉ kūḻuṇṇac ceṉṟirukkum pōtu ataip payaṉpaṭuttum piṟa vaṇṇāraiyum, oruvaṉai ōṭit toṭuvatu tiruṭṭuttaṉamāyp pāṟaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttiya oruvaṉaip piṭittu aṭippataiyum, kuṟippatākak koḷḷappaṭum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ: ōṭum oruvaṉaip piṭippatum oruvaṉāṟ piṭi paṭāmal ōṭippōvatumāṉa viṉaippayiṟci, iv viḷai yāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.  (ta.nā.vi.)
vaṇikamum pōkkuvarattum
uḻaviṉāl viḷaikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷaiyum kait toḻi lāṟ ceyyappaṭum poruḷkaḷaiyum uḷ nāṭṭiṟ pakiravum, uḷnāṭṭup poruḷkaḷai veḷināṭṭilum veḷi nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷai uḷ nāṭṭilum parappavum, perum poḷīṭṭi nāṭṭuc celvattaip perukkavum, vaṇikam iṉṟiyamaiyāta teṉṟu kaṇṭa paṇṭaiyaracar, ataṉai ollum vāyellām ūkkivantaṉar.
nakarantoṟum vaṇikar kuḻumam (Merchant Guild) iruntatu. nāṭṭuppuṟa nakarāṭci perumpālum nakara vaṇikar kaiyil iruntatu. nakaraṅkaḷaiyellām aracaṉ nēraṭiyāka mēṟ pārttu vantaṉaṉ. talaimai vaṇikaṉukku eṭṭip paṭṭamum eṭṭippuravum aḷikkappaṭṭatu. vaṇikar kuḻumaṅkaṭku valañcai maṇikkirāmam añcuvaṇṇam mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpē, nilavāṇikam nīr vāṇikam ākiya iruvakai vaṇikamum tamiḻakattil taḻaittōṅkiyirun taṉa. nilaṅkaṭantu ceyyum nila vaṇikam kāliṟpirivu eṉṟum, nīrkaṭantu ceyyum nīr vāṇikam kalattiṟ pirivu eṉṟum, kūṟap paṭṭaṉa.
munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūuvō ṭillai
eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl (980), tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē nīrvāṇikam ciṟantiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
nilappōkkuvarattu:  taṭivaḻi yeṉṟum peruvaḻiyeṉṟum kūṟap paṭum cālaikaḷ nāṭeṅkum iruntaṉa. avai 64 cāṇ akalamum iru maruṅkum maraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉavāyiruntaṉa. `kōṭṭāṟṟuccālai' `maturaip peruvaḻi' `araṅkam nōkkip pōnta peruvaḻi' `koṅkuvaḻi' `taṭikaivaḻi' `vaṭukavaḻi' mutaliya peyarkaḷāl, tamiḻnāṭṭup perunakarkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟit teṉṉāṭṭuc cīmaikaḷum cālaikaḷāl iṇaikkappaṭṭin tamai pulaṉām. toṇṭai nāṭṭiṉ mēṟkellai oru peru vaḻiyāyirunta teṉpatu, `mēṟkup peruvaḻiyām' eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa ūravaiyār avaravar ūrarukēyuḷḷa taṭivaḻip pakutikaḷaik kavaṉittu vantaṉar.
vēṭṭaikkuc ceṉṟa aracaṉ "neṭumāṉ ṟērōṭu pākaṉai nilavumaṇaṟ kāṉyāṟṟu niṟkap paṇittu........ irumpoḻil pukum" eṉa nakkīrar kūṟi yiruppatāl, iyaṉṟaviṭamellām caṅka kālat taracar tērēṟic ceṉṟaṉareṉṟum, ataṟkuriya vaḻivacatikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, ūkikkalām.
cālaikaḷ piriyumiṭaṅkaḷil allatu cērumiṭaṅkaḷil, avvac cālai cellum ūrp peyar poṟitta vaḻikāṭṭi maraṅkaḷ nāṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
māṭu, kutirai, kōvēṟukaḻutai ākiya mūvakaip poti māk kaḷaiyuṭaiya vaṇikaccāttukkaḷ, aṭikkaṭi cālaikaḷil iyaṅkikkoṇṭiruntaṉa.
kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum kauntiyaṭikaḷum aruvilaic cilam puṭaṉ pukāriliruntu muppatu kātat tolaiviluḷḷa maturaik kum, tēvantiyum kaṇṇakiyiṉ ceviliyum aṭittōḻiyum pukāriliruntu maturai vaḻiyāy vañcikkum, maṇimēkalai pukāri liruntu vañcikkum piṉṉar vañciyiliruntu kāñcikkum, māṭalaṉ pukāriṉiṉṟu kumarikkum piṉṉark kaṅkaikkum, parācaraṉ cōṇāṭṭiṉiṉṟu cēra nāṭṭiṟkum piṉpu perum poruḷuṭaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻiyākat taṉṉūrukkum, yātōr iṭarppāṭumiṉṟic ceṉṟatāl, akkālattil vaḻip pōkkarkku vēṇṭum vaḻivacatiyum pātukāppum nirampa viruntamai yaṟiyappaṭum.
tamiḻaracar aṭikkaṭi vaṭanāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉ ṟatum; avaruḷ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭuc celavil, 100 tērkaḷum 500 yāṉaikaḷum 10,000 kutiraikaḷum, 20,000 paṇṭamēṟṟiya vaṇṭikaḷum, 1,000 caṭṭaiyiṭṭa atikāri kaḷum, māperun tokaiyiṉarāṉa kālāṭ paṭaiñarum. 102 nāṭaka makaḷirum, 208 kuyiluvarum, 100 nakaivēḻamparum, uṭaṉ ceṉṟatum; akkālattirunta pōkkuvarattu vacatiyai oruvāṟuṇarttum.
`aṉuppu' eṉṟoru vari muṟkālattu vāṅkappaṭṭatiṉāl, orukāl añcal ēṟpāṭu akkālattiruntirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
nīrp pōkkuvarattu :  paḵṟuḷiyāṟu muḻuku muṉṉarē, kiḻakkil cāvakam malaiyā kāḻakam (parmā) cīṉam mutaliya tēcaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum, mēṟkil arapiyā pāpilōṉiyā kaltēyā mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷuṭaṉum, tamiḻakam vaṇikañ ceytu vantatu.
cīṉakkaṇṇāṭi cīṉakkāram cīṉakkiṇṇam cīṉac cūṭaṉ cīṉap paṭṭu mutaliya paṇṭaṅkaḷ cīṉattiṉiṉṟum ilavaṅkappaṭṭai, kirāmpū, cātikkāy, cātippattiri mutaliya carakkukaḷ nākanāṭukaḷ eṉṉum kīḻintiyattīvuk kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum,1 tamiḻaracariṉ nālvakaip paṭaikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉ kutiraip paṭaikku vēṇṭuṅ kutiraikaḷ arapiyāviṉiṉṟum, cittirap pēḻai pāvai viḷakku matu mutaliya poruḷkaḷ yavaṉa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum, tamiḻakat tiṟkuk kalaṅkaḷil vantiṟaṅkiṉa.
tēkku,  tōkai (mayil), arici, akil, cantaṉam, iñci, koṭṭai (pañcuc curuḷ), veṟṟilai, aṭaikkāy (pākku) mutaliya pala poruḷkaṭkut tamiḻp peyarkaḷē mēlai moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkuvatu, paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭu ēṟṟumatic ciṟappaik kāṭṭum.
kaltēyā nāṭṭaiccērnta ūr eṉṉum iṭattil akaḻn teṭukkappaṭṭa oru tamiḻ nāṭṭut tēkkavuttaram, ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṟpaṭṭa cālamōṉ eṉṉum yūtavaracaṉ kālattil tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu yūtēyāviṟku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa tōkaiyiṉ peyar, yūta moḻiyāṉa epirēyattil tuki eṉa vaḻaṅki vantatu.
kaṭalvāṇikattaip perukkutaṟporuṭṭu, ki.mu. 55il pāṇṭiyaṉ rōmavaracaṉukkum, ki.pi. 1015il mutalām irāca rācaṉum, ki.pi. 1033il irācēntiraṉum, ki.pi.1077il mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉum, cīṉavaracaṉukkum; tūtu viṭuttaṉar.
muttamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkaraiyilum, oru kālattiṟku oṉṟum palavumāka, vevvēṟu kālattil vevvēṟu tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ amaintiruntaṉa. cōḻa nāṭṭiṟku mayilai, mallai, pukār, kāraikkāl, nākai, toṇṭi mutaliyaṉavum; pāṇṭi nāṭ ṭiṟkuk kavāṭam, koṟkai, kāyal mutaliyaṉavum; cēra nāṭṭiṟku vañci, muciṟi, toṇṭi, māntai, naṟavūr, koṭuṅkōḷūr, kāntaḷūr, viḻiñam, kōḻikkōṭu mutaliyaṉavum; tuṟai nakarkaḷāy iruntu vantaṉa.
tuṟaimukantōṟum kalaṅkarai viḷakkam (Light House) iruntatu. pukār kavāṭam vañcipōṉṟa talaimai allatu kōnakart tuṟaimukaṅkaḷil, palvēṟu nāṭṭuk kalaṅkaḷ palvakaip paṇṭaṅ kaḷai nāḷtōṟum ēṟṟuvatum, iṟakkuvatumāyiruntaṉa. ēṟṟumatiyum iṟakkumatiyumāṉa aḷaviṭappaṭāta paṇṭap potikaḷ, āyattiṟkāka niṟukkappaṭṭu avvat tamiḻnāṭṭaraca muttirai poṟikkappaṭṭapiṉ, tuṟaimukattaiviṭṭu nīṅkum varai ciṟanta kāval ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aṇiyavum cēyavu māṉa palvēṟu nāṭṭiliruntu vanta pulampeyar mākkaḷ taṅkuva taṟku, vacatiyākac cērikaḷum viṭutikaḷum iruntaṉa. tamiḻakak kīḻk kaṭaṟkaraiyil nākanāṭṭār kumari mutal vaṅkam varai pala nakaraṅkaḷil vantu taṅkiyiruntamaiyāl, avai nākarkōyil, nākappaṭṭiṉam, nākūr, nākapuri eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa. mīṉ piṭikkum kaṭṭumaram mutal kutiraip paṭaiyēṟṟattakka nāvāy varai, palatarappaṭṭa marakkalaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṇikarkkuc contamāyiruntaṉa. ēlēlaciṅkaṉ, kōvalaṉ muṉṉōr, paṭṭiṉattār eṉṉum tiruveṇkāṭar mutaliyōr peruṅkala vaṇikarāvar "ēlēlaciṅkaṉ kappal ēḻukaṭal ceṉṟālum mīḷum" eṉpatu paḻamoḻiyākum.
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ "kaṭaṟkaṭam peṟinta kāvalaṉ" eṉṟum, "kaṭalpiṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ" eṉṟum, pukaḻap peṟuva tāl; akkālattaracar pakaivarālum kaṭaṟkoḷḷaik kāra rālum viḷaikkappaṭum tīṅkukaḷaip pōkkik kaṭal vaṇi kattaik kāttamai ūkikkappaṭum, pīlivaḷai nāka nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu pukār vantu mīḷavum, maṇimēkalai īḻattiṟkum cāvakat tiṟkum ceṉṟu mīḷavum, vacatiyum pātukāvalumāṉa marakkalap pōkkuvarattu akkālattiruntatu.
nīriṉ vanta nimirparip puraviyuṅ
kāliṉ vanta karuṅkaṟi mūṭaiyum
vaṭamalaip piṟanta maṇiyum poṉṉuṅ
kuṭamalaip piṟanta vāramu makilun
teṉkaṭal muttuṅ kuṇakaṭaṟ ṟukiruṅ
kaṅkai vāriyuṅ kāvirip payaṉu

varaṇam, varaṇi eṉṉum iru collum, vaṭacol vaṭivaip piṉpaṟṟi varuṇa, varuṇi eṉat tamiḻil vaḻuppaṭa eḻutappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. gh©o eh£o‰ Fy« Édî«nghJ "v‹d tuz«?" eṉṟē iṉṟum rakara vaṭivil nāṭṭup puṟattil vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
vaṇṇam, varaṇam eṉṉum iru collum orē mūlattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavaiyē. āyiṉ, muṉṉatu aṭippaṭai mūlamākiya vaḷ eṉpatiṉiṉṟum, piṉṉatu mēṟpaṭai mūlamākiya vari eṉpatiṉiṉṟum, tirintavaiyākum.
vaḷ-vaṇ-vaṇṇam-vaṇṇi-vaṇṇaṉai.
(vaḷ-var) vari-varaṇam-varaṇi-varaṇaṉai.
o.nō : tiḷ-tiṇ-tiṇṇai (aṭippaṭai mūlam).
(tiḷ-tir) tiraḷ-tiraḷai-tiraṇai(mēṟpaṭai mūlam). (va.va.)
tiṇṇai, tiraṇai eṉṉum iraṇṭum orē mūlattiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiya oruporuṭ coṟkaḷē, ivaiyiraṇṭum iṉṟum nellaināṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkām.
iṅṅaṉamē vaṇṇam, varaṇam eṉpaṉavum.
vaṇṇam eṉṉum aṭippaṭai mūlattiripu vaṭamoḻiyi liṉmaiyum, varaṇam (varṇa) eṉṉum mēṟpaṭai mūlantiripē atiluṇmaiyum, kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
vaṇṇaka vottāḻicaikkali eṉṉum ceyyuḷ vakaip peyar, tolkāppiyar muntu nūliṟkaṇṭa ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭātaliṉ, talaikkaḻakak kālattatu eṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum.
varṇ eṉṟu vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam ceykaiyē.
""var@n (rather Nom. fr. var@n@a") eṉṟu mā.vi.a. kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va: 252-253)
varam
puram = mēl, mēṉmāṭam.  puram-param = mēl, mēliṭam, mēlulakam.
param-varam = mēṉmai. varam - varaṉ (kaṭaippōli) mēlulakam, vīṭṭulakam.  (ti.ma.49)
varalāṟṟiṉ payaṉ
kalaikaḷum (Arts) aṟiviyalkaḷum (Sciences) (1) taṟcārpuḷḷatu (Independent) (2) maṟcārpuḷḷatu (Dependent) eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. taṟcār paṟiviyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa varalāṟu, ēṉaiyaṟiviyaṅkaṭ kellām aṭimaṇaiyāyum mutukantaṇṭāyum iruppatākum. ovvoru tuṟaiyilum, toṭakkantoṭṭuvarum āciriyariṉ allatu aṟiñariṉ varalāṟṟu muṟaippaṭṭa aṟivuttokutiyē orukalai aṟiviyam.
kāṭciyum karuttumākiya ovvoru poruṭkum varalāṟirup piṉum oru nāṭu, ataṉ makkaḷ, avarkaḷ moḻi, avarkaḷ nākarikam, āṭci ākiya cilavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟē ciṟappāka varalā ṟeṉappaṭum.
oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu annāṭṭiṉ paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaiyum (Abori-gines) vantēṟi kaḷaiyum (Immigrants) pirittuk kāṭṭuvatāl oru vīṭṭukkāraṉukku avvīṭṭu āvaṇam ēmakkāppāvatu pōl, oru nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaṭkum annāṭṭu varalāṟu cila vurimai vakaiyil ēmakkāppām.
iṉi, varalāṟiṉṟi oru moḻiyiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa iyalpai aṟivatum aritām.
varalāṟuraippaṉa
oru nāṭṭiṉ paṇṭai varalāṟṟai aṟiyap puttakamum ceyyuḷuntāṉ vēṇṭum eṉpatu illai. annāṭṭu moḻi allatu coṟkaḷ kūṭa varalāṟuraippaṉa ākum.
"paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavu nilai araciyal nilai ivaṟṟiṉ niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷai iṟṟaic coṟkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu, avaṟṟaip paṟṟit tam karuttai aṟivikkumāṟu āciriyar avaṟṟaik kuṟittu vaittal vēṇṭum. ovvoru nāṭṭāriṉ uḷḷuṟu vāḻkkaiyum avaratu moḻiyiṟ peyarā eḻuttiṟ poṟikkap paṭṭu varuṅkāla makkaḷiṉ aṟiviṟkākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu: nāṭṭu varalāṟu paḻakka vaḻakkam oḻukkavitikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaip poruḷ vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭuttāta āciriyar mikat tavaṟiyavarāvar" eṉṟu kirakam eṉṟum āṅkilēya āciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār. English Word - Book by John Graham. col. 16.
varalāṟṟuk kaḷañciyam
coṟkaḷum ovvoru varalāṟṟuṇmaiyai uṇarttak kūṭum eṉṟum virivāṉa akarāti oru varalāṟṟuk kaḷañciyam eṉ ṟum, varalāṟṟuc coṟkaḷaiyum varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷaip pōlap pōṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷḷum vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟ kaḷai mikak karuttāyp pōṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟum aṟika. (col -30.)
varaṉ
varaṉ :
puram=uyarcci, uyaramāṉa kaṭṭiṭam, kōpuramuḷḷa nakar.  kōpuram = aracaṉirukkum uyaramāṉa kaṭṭiṭam, uyaramāṉa kāvaṟ kūṇṭu.
puraiyuyar pākum.  (tol. e. aru.).
puram--param= mēlulakam :  param--varam--  varaṉ = mēlāṉa vīṭṭulakam.  (ti.ma. 754)
varāl
varāl - murala
vari=nīḷam;  vari-varāl - kuṟavaiyilum nīṇṭiruppatu. E murrel.
mur (cūḻ,piṉṉu, piṇai) eṉpatu mūlamāyirukkalāmeṉṟu. mā.vi.a. karutuvatu cariyaṉṟu.  (va.va. 255)
vari
vari - vrīhi (a.vē.).
vari=variccampā, variyuḷḷa nel, nel (piṅ.).
eṭuttuvari muṟattiṉiliṭṭu  (taṉippāṭal).
mā.vi.a "of doubtful derivation"v‹W kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va: 255).
varikaḷ
kucakkāṇam, taṟikkaṭamai, taṭṭāṟappāṭṭam, kiṭaippūṭci, cekkiṟai, vaṇṇārap pāṟai, tarakupāṭṭam, ōṭakkūli, nallā, nallerutu mutaliyaṉa. kaliyāṇakkāṇam eṉpatu maṇamakkaḷ celuttiyavari. atu ikkālattup pativumaṇak kaṭṭaṇam pōlvatu. kaḷḷiṟakkuvār celuttiya vari mēṉip poṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. īḻa nāṭṭil iruntu īḻavar vantapiṉṉarē atu īḻam pūṭci eṉap peyar māṟiṟṟu. teṉṉaiyum paṉaiyum toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻakattil iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. nāṭu kāval (pāṭikāval), nīrāṇi (nīrkkūli) mutaliya cilavarikaḷ ūravaiyārāl vāṅkappaṭṭaṉavām.  (tiruk. 754)
varicai yaṟital
aracaṉ allatu āṭcittalaivaṉ kalaiñar, pulavar, pāvalar aṟiñar, āciriyar, ārāycciyāḷar, paṇittiṟavōr mutali yōrkkup paricaḷikkumpōtum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkumpōtum vēṟu ciṟappuc ceyyumpōtum, avaravar takutiyun taramumākiya varicaiyaṟintu ceytal vēṇṭum. paricaḷittal eḷitu; varicaiyaṟintaḷittal aritu.
oruticai yoruvaṉai yuḷḷi nāṟṟicaip
palarum varuvar pariciṉ mākkaḷ
varicai yaṟitalō aritē peritum
ital eḷitē māvaṇ ṭōṉṟal
atunaṟ kaṟintaṉai yāyiṉ
potunōk koḻimati pulavar māṭṭē  (puṟanā. 121)
eṉṟu, kapilar malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāriyai nōkkip pāṭi ṉār. ivvēṇṭukōḷ ellā īkaiyāḷarkkum ēṟkum.
tamiḻar vaḻikāṭṭiyaruḷ tiruvaḷḷuvarkku aṭuttavar maṟai malaiyaṭikaḷē;
perumpulavaruḷ ovvoruvarum ovvoṉṟil vallavar. pērā. auvai cu. turaicāmip piḷḷai uraivēntar; cintā maṇic celvar mē. vī. vēṇukōpālap piḷḷai ilakkaṇap pulavar; mayilai. cīṉivēṅkaṭacāmi varalāṟṟāycciyāḷar.
pāvalaruḷ icaip pāṭṭicaippārum ceyyuḷ ceyvārum ceyyuḷ ceyvāruḷuḷ ḷum, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum ēṟṟavāṟu palavakaip paṉuval iyaṟṟuvārum pāṭuvārum vevvēṟu.
kalaiñarkkup paricaḷikkumpōtu, takutiyai maṭṭumiṉṟit tēvaiyaiyum kavaṉittal vēṇṭum. aṭikkaṭi icai yaraṅku nikaḻtti āyirak kaṇakkākap poruḷ peṟum miṭaṟṟicai ñarkkup poṟṟāmaraiyum, aṅṅaṉamē, naṭavaraṅku nikaḻttip perumporuḷ peṟum naṭaṉiyarkkut talaikkōlum vaḻaṅki ṉāṟ pōtum.
iyal icai nāṭakam mūṉṟum muttamiḻ eṉappaṭutalālum, iyalil tērcci peṟavum matinuṭpamum neṭuṅkāla vuḻaippum vēṇṭiyiruttalālum, paricaḷavil allatu ciṟappuc ceyyum vakaiyil, vēṟupāṭiṉṟi,  muttamiḻp pulavaraiyum camamākavē karututal vēṇṭum. miṭaṟṟicai vāyppāṭṭu.

vaḷā +aka« =vaḷāka« =1. vaḷaikkai, cūḻkai (cūṭā.). 2. cūḻnta iṭam, iṭam. "pulaṉka ḷaintum veṉṟavar vaḷākan taṉṉuṭ ceṉṟilēṉ" (tēvā. 1193:1). 3. kaṭal cūḻnta ulakam. "munnīr vaḷāka mellām" (kalit. 146). 4. nilapperuṅ kaṇṭam. "ēḻperu vaḷāka vēntar" (kamparā. tirumuṭi. 6). 5. tēcam. "vēṟōr vaḷākam tuṟṟāṉ..... vaṇikaṉ" (kantapu. mārk. 142). 6. tiṉaippuṉap parappu. vaḷā - vaḷāñci = vaḷār, (i.va.).
vaḷ - vaḷi = vaḷaintu vīcuṅ kāṟṟu. "vaḷivaḻaṅku mallaṉmā ñālañ kari" (kuṟaḷ. 245). 2. uṭampiluḷḷa ūtaik kūṟu. "vaḷimutalā veṇṇiya mūṉṟu" (kuṟaḷ. 941).
vaḷa - vaḷai, vaḷaital = 1. kōṇutal. 2. kuṉintu tāḻtal. "muṟṟa mūttuk kōltuṇaiyā muṉṉaṭi nōkki vaḷaintu" (tiv. periyati. 1:3:1). 3. tōṟṟu vaṇaṅkutal. "vaḷaiyā vayavarum" (pu.ve. 7. 18). 4. tiṭamaṟutal (cīvaka. 1068, urai). 5. ceṅkōl vaḷaivatu pōṉṟu āṭci nērmaiyiṉiṉṟu vilakutal. "vaḷai yāta ceṅkōl vaḷainta tituveṉkol" (cilap. 19:16). 6. varuntutal uṭampu vaḷaintu vēlai ceyvatillai. (u.va.). "vaḷaiyār paciyiṉ" (patiṉo. tiruviṭai. mummaṇik. 27). 7. cuṟṟutal.
- (ce. kuṉṟāvi.) 1. cūḻtal. "vaḷaikaṭal valaiyiṟ cūḻntu" (cīvaka. 1115). 2. cuṟṟi varutal. "vaiyaka muḻutuṭaṉ vaḷaii" (puṟam. 69).
vaḷaiya vaḷaiya = tirumpat tirumpa.
vaḷaittal = 1. vaḷaiyacceytal. 2. cūḻtal. "irumuṭ puricai yēṟa vaḷaii" (mullaip. 27). 3. taṭuttal. "vaḷḷa ṉīṅkap peṟāy vaḷaittēṉeṉa" (cīvaka. 889). 4. paṟṟutal. 5. kavartal. koḷḷaikkārar ellāvaṟṟaiyum vaḷaittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭṭārkaḷ (u.va.) 6. coṉṉa collai māṟṟutal. vaḷaittu vaḷaittup pēcukiṟāṉ (u.va.). 7. vaḷaittu eḻututal. "uruvap palpū vorukoṭi vaḷaii" (neṭunal. 113). 8. aṇital. "caṭaimuṭimēl muṭiveṇṭiṅkaḷ vaḷaittāṉai" (tēvā.871:1)
vaḷai = 1. vaḷaiyal. "muṉkai yiṟaiyiṟavā niṉṟa vaḷai" 
(kuṟaḷ. 1157). 2. cakkarappaṭai. "tāṅkaṇaip paṇilamum vaḷai yum" (kamparā. urukkāṭṭup. 43). 3. caṅku "vaḷaiyoṭu puraiyum vāli yōṟku" (paripā. 2:20). 4. vaṭṭattuḷai, eli naṇṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaḷai. 5. cuṟṟiṭam. "parimēccura maṅkalattu ḷakappaṭṭa vaḷaiyil" (S.I.I.i, 151;72).
ka. paḷe (b).
vaḷai - vaḷaiyam = 1. vaṭṭam (aka.ni.). 2. kaivaḷai (W.) 3. muṭiyiṟ cūṭum vaḷaiyamālai. 4. tāmaraic curuḷ (piṅ). 5. cuṟṟu. 6. oru kōḷ vāṉamaṇṭalattai orumuṟai cuṟṟivaruṅ kālam. 7. kuḷam. (W.).
vaḷai - va. valaya.
vaḷai - vaḷaiyal = 1. vaḷaivuḷḷatu. (yāḻ. aka.). 2. kaivaḷai. vaḷaiyal viṟṟa paṭalam.
vaḷai - vaḷaivu = 1. vaḷaivuḷḷatu. (yāḻ. aka.). 2. kaivaḷai. vaḷaiyal viṟṟa paṭalam.
vaḷai - vaḷaivu = kōṇal. 2. kaṭṭaṭattil amaikkum vilvaṭivu. 3. vaṭṭam. 4. cuṟṟu. 5. vīṭṭuppuṟam (W.) 6. paṇivu (yāḻ. aka).
vaḷaivi - 1. vīṭṭiṟappu. 2. vaḷaiyal.
vaḷai-vaṉai. vaṉaital = vaṇṇam = 1. vaḷaittoḻutukai. 2. eḻutum ōviyam. 3. eḻutum eḻuttu. 4. cittira meḻutuṅ kalavai. "palakai vaṇṇa nuṇ ṭukilikai" (cīvaka. 1107). 5. kalavai niṟam. 6. niṟam. "veḷiyārmuṉ vāṉcutai vaṇṇaṅkoḷal" (kuṟaḷ. 714). "vaṇṇam vaṭivē aḷavē cuvaiyē" (tol. vēṟ. 17). 7. ōviya aḻaku. 8. aḻaku. "piṟainutal vaṇṇamākiṉṟu"  (puṟam.1). 9. iyaṟkai yaḻaku. "vaṇṇamun tēcu moḷiyun tikaḻa" (paripā. 12: 20). 10. ceyaṟkai yaḻaku. oppaṉai (aka.ni.). 11. ciṟappu.
"kaivaṇṇa miṅkuk kaṇṭēṉ" (kamparā. akalikai. 82). 12. naṉmai (yāḻ. aka.) 13. kuṇam. "niraluṭaimaiyum vaṇṇamun tuṇaiyum" (kuṟiñcip. 31). 14. vaṭivu (piṅ.). 15. kulam (tol. puṟat. 27, urai). 16. iṉam. "vaṇṇam vaṇṇatta malar" (kuṟiñcip. 114). 17. vakai. "talaiccellā vaṇṇattāl" (kuṟaḷ. 561). 18. ōcaivakai. "vaṇṇan tāṉē nālain teṉpa" (tol. cey. 210). 19. ciṟanta ōcaivakaip pāṭṭu, vaṇṇattiraṭṭu. 20. muṭukiya ōcaiyuḷḷa kaliyuṟuppu. muṭukiyal (piṅ.) 21. iṉṉicaip paṇ (irākam) (catu.) 22. iṉṉicaip pāṭṭu. o. nō : til - tiḷ - tiṇ - tiṇṇam

o. nō : tiṇṇam - tiṇṇaṉ.
vaṇ - vaṇṇi, vaṇṇittal = ōr oviyattaip palvēṟu niṟaṅkaḷālum nuṇviṉaikaḷāluñ ciṟappippatum, ōr aṇikattai (vākaṉattai)p paṉṉiṟa aḻakiya poruḷkaḷāṟ cuvaṭippatum, ōr uṭampaip paṉṉiṟa āṭaiyaṇikaḷāl aḻaku paṭuttuvatum pōṉṟu, iṟaivaṉai allatu oru tavaiṉaip palvēṟu karuttukaḷālum palvakaic ceyyuḷuṟuppukaḷālum ciṟappittup pukaḻtal: allatu, poruḷiṭaṅ kālaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ iyal ceyaluṟuppu kaḷellāvaṟṟaiyum viḷattamāka (vivaramāka) eṭuttuk kūṟutal.
o. nō : tiṇṇam - tiṇṇi - tiṇi.
vaṇṇi - vaṇṇakam = 1. vaṇṇittup pukaḻkai. "vaṇṇittup pukaḻtaliṉ vaṇṇaka meṉappaṭum" (tol. cey. 140, urai). 2. oru kalippā vuṟuppu.
vaṇṇaka vottāḻicaik kalippā - taravu, tāḻicai, arākam, ampōtaraṅkam, taṉiccol, curitakam eṉṉuṅ kaliyuṟup pāṟum vantu, muḻu niṟaivākum kalippā eṉṉum pāvakai.
oruvaṉ taṉ kātalai veḷippaṭuttaṟkum, kaṭavuḷai vaḻuttaṟ kum, vaṇṇaka vottāḻicaik kalippāppōl mikap porutta māṉa ceyyuḷ vakaiyai vēṟem moḻiyiluṅ kāṇa viyalātu.

ma. t©o, bj., ka. paṇṭi.
cakaṭam eṉṉum poruḷil, vaṇṭi eṉṉuñ col paṇṭi eṉpataṉ tiripākat terikiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉiṉ, paṇṭi eṉṉum vaṭivē cīvaka cintāmaṇi mūlattil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷatu. mēlum , pāṇṭi, pāṇṭil eṉpavaṟṟai yotta vakara mutaṟcoṟkaḷ iruvakai vaḻakkilumillai. orukāl, vaṇṭiyeṉṉum vaṭivē toṉṟutoṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkāyiruntirukkalām. vaṇṭi yeṉṉum vaṭuka kaṉṉaṭa vaṭivam, vakara pakarat tiripiṉ viḷaivām.

"kommaik kuyavaṭṭaṇai koṇṭilaṉō" (kamparā. atikāyaṉ. 9). 4. tāḷam pōṭukai (piṅ.). 5. aṭikkai (aru.ni.). 6. uruṇṭai. 7. iṭacāri vala cāriyākac cuṟṟukai. "cuṟṟivarum vaṭṭaṇayiṟ ṟōṉṟāvakai kalantu" (periyapu. ēṉāti. 29). 8. vaṭṭamāṉa celavu. "māppuṇṭara vāciyiṉ vaṭṭaṇaimēl" (kamparā paṭait. 98). 9. toḻutaṟku aṟikuṟiyākat tāmarai moṭṭup pōlak kaikaḷaik kuvikkai. "vakaiteri mākkaṭku vaṭṭaṉai kāṭṭi" (maṇimē 7:43). 10. vakkaṇai. "vaṭṭaṇai pēcuvar" (patiṉo.kōpappira. 85).
vaṭṭam - vaṭṭaravu - vaṭṭavaṭivu (yāḻp).
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṟavu = muṭivāka aṟutiyiṭukai (yāḻ. aka.).
vaṭṭam - vaṭṭaṉ = (pe.) uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa vuṭampiṉaṉ. "kuṟuvaṭṭā niṉṉi ṉiḻintatō kūṉiṉ piṟappu" (kalit 94:27).
(iṭai.) tōṟum. "āṭṭai vaṭṭaṉ mukkuṟuṇi neṟpoli caiyāka" (S.I.I.II, 69:3).
vaṭṭam - vaṭṭā = tiṟamai, camarttu, (tiruvirut. 71. arum. pa.371).
te. vaṭṭāri.
vaṭṭam - vaṭṭāram = 1. cuṟṟuppuṟam. 2. nilap parappu.
te. vaṭṭāramu, tu. vaṭṭāra, ka. vaṭṭāra.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭi. vaṭṭittal = (ce.ku.vi.) 1. vaṭṭamātal. (yāḻ. aka.). 2. cuḻalutal. "vaṭṭittup puyalē ṟuraiiya viyaliru ṇaṭunāḷ" (akam. 218). 3. tāḷavoṟṟaṟuttal (cūṭā.). 4. cūḷuraittal. "vaṭṭittu viṭṭāḷeṟintāḷ" (cilap. 21:45).
(ce. kuṉṟāvi). 1. vaḷaittal (ilak. aka.). 2. vaḷaittu eḻututal (cilap. 21:46i arum. 3. cuḻaṟṟutal "maruntil kaṇicci varunta vaṭṭittu" (puṟam. 42). 4. uruṭṭutal. "kaḻakattut tavirātu vaṭṭippa" (kalit. 136).
cuṟṟik kaṭṭutal. "alakil mālai yārppa vaṭṭittu" (puṟam. 394). 6. pari māṟutal (W.). 7. tōḷ puṭaittal. "ceruvaṟaintu pāḻittōḷ vaṭṭittār" (paḻa. 326).
vaṭṭi = 1. kaṭakap peṭṭi "vēṭṭuvaṉ māṉṟacai corinta vaṭṭiyum" (puṟam 33). 2. kūṭai "palkala vaṭṭiyar" (akam. 391). 3. nāḻi. (tol. eḻuttu. 170). 4. kiṇṇam (aka.ni.). 5. oru virutu.
vaṭṭi - vaṭṭikai = 1. vaṭṭam (yāḻ. aka.). 2. cuṟṟaḷavu 3. kūṭai (piṅ). 4. kaimmaṇi (yāḻ. aka.). 5. ōṭavakai. "tuḷḷiyal vaṭṭikai tuṭuppiṟ kaṭaii" (peruṅ. uñcaik. 40:46). 6. oruvakai virutu. "alakil vaṭṭikai taḻal viḻittalāl" (kaliṅ. 333). 7. cittira meḻutuṅkōl "vaṭṭikaic ceytiyiṉ varainta pāvaiyiṉ" (maṇimē. 4:57). 8. cittiram. "vaṭṭikaip pāvai nōkki" (cīvaka. 2085).
vaṭṭippu = 1. vaṭṭam. (yāḻ. aka.). 2. cūḷ (nāmatīpa. 667).
vaṭṭi - vaṭṭil = 1. vaṭṭamāṉa (veṇkala) uṇkalam. 2. kiṇṇam. "poṉ vaṭṭil piṭittu" (tiv. perumāḷ. 4:3). 3. nāḻi (tol. eḻuttu. 170 : iḷampū). 4. nāḻikai vaṭṭil. 5. ampuk kūṭu. "vāḷi vaṭṭil puṟamvaittu" (kamparā.tērēṟu. 39). 6. kūṭai (aka.ni.). 7. oruvakai virutu. "ēṟumāl yāṉaiyē civikai yantaḷaka mīccōppi vaṭṭil" (tēvā. 692:7). 8. appaḷañceyya uruṭṭi vaikkum māvuruṇṭai (i.va.).
ka. paṭṭalu (b) 
vaṭṭiṟpū = tāmaraippū.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭai = 1. vēṅkaiyiṉ uṭalvari (W.). 2. cakkarattiṉ mēl vaḷaimarap palakai. "uruḷkiṉṟa maṇivaṭṭai" (cilap. 29, uraippāṭṭu maṭai). 3. tēr (yāḻ. aka.). 4. nilapparappu. 5. vaṭṭap pakkā eṉṉum paṭṭaṇam paṭi. (nāñ).
vaṭṭak karuttuc coṟkaḷuḷ vaṭṭu, vaṭṭakai, vaṭṭaṇi, vaṭṭa ṇai, vaṭṭaravu, vaṭṭā, vaṭṭāṇi, vaṭṭāram, vaṭṭittal, vaṭṭil, vaṭṭai eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illai. vaṭṭam eṉpatu varutta eṉṉum vaṭivilum vaṭṭaṇam eṉpatu aṭṭaṉa (na) eṉṉum vaṭivilum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟ kellām varutta eṉpataiyē mūlamākak kāṭṭuvatu et tuṇaiyum  poruntātu.
vaṭṭai - vaṭai = tērccakkara vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭum uḻuntup palakāram.
paṭai - va. vaṭā.
vaṭai - vaṭaiyam = 1. nellakkaṉi mutaliyavaṟṟai araittut taṭṭiya vaṭai. 2. poṭṭalamākak kaṭṭiya veṟṟilai pākku (tañ).
vaṭṭam-vaṭam = 1. vaṭṭavaṭivam (aka. ni.). 2.). vaṭṭamākap paṭarum ālamaram, "vaṭanīḻaṟ kaṇṇūṭṭirunta kuruvē" (tāyu. karuṇā. 1). 3. tiraṇṭa kayiṟu. kamalai vaṭam, tēr vaṭam, 4. tiraṇṭapoṉnāṇ. maṇivaṭam. "vaṭaṅkaḷ acaiyumpaṭi uṭuttu" (tirumuruku. 204, urai).
vaṭam (ālamaram) - va. vaṭa (vata). ip poruḷil ic coṟku vaṭa moḻiyil vērillai. māṉiyar uvilliyamcu tam camaṟkiruta- āṅkila akaramutaliyil. perhaps Prakrit for vrita surrounded, covered' eṉṟu poruṭkāraṇaṅ kūṟiyiruttalai nōkkuka.
vaṭam - vaṭaku-vaṭakam = tāḷipput tuṇaik karaṇamāka, araitta māvuṭaṉ kaṟiccarakkukaḷ cērttup porittu. veyilil ularttiya naṟumaṇavuruṇṭai.
vaṭakam - va. vaṭaka (vataka).
vaḷ-var-vari. varital = 1. eḻututal (piṅ.). 2. cittirameḻututal. "vallōṉ ṟaiiya varivaṉap puṟṟa vallip pāvai" (puṟam. 33.). 3.  pūcutal. 4. mūṭutal. "puṇṇai maṟaiya varintu" (tiv. tiruvā. 5:1:5). 5. kaṭṭutal. "varinta kaccaiyaṉ" (cūḷā. cīya. 11).
varittal = 1. eḻututal. "vaḷḷukir varitta cāntiṉ vaṉamulai" (cīvaka. 2532). 2. cittira meḻututal. 3. pūcutal (cūṭā.). 4. kōlañ ceytal. "puṉṉai yaṇimalar tuṟaitoṟum varikkum" (aiṅ. 117). 5. kaṭṭutal. "varikkuṅ kāṭciyilā vaṟivē"  (ñāṉavā. māvali. 48). 6. varaintu koḷḷutal. 7. tirumaṇañ ceytal.
vari = 1. kōṭu. "nuṇṇiya variyoṭu tiraṇṭu" (cīvaka. 1702).  2. toyyil varaivu. "maṇivari taiiyum" (kalit. 76). 3. kaivarai (piṅ.). 4. varicai, oḻuṅku. "kuruki ṉeṭuvari poṟpa" (patiṟ. 83:2). 5. eḻuttu (nāmatīpa. 673). 6. niṟam. "variyaṇicuṭar vāṉ poykai" (paṭṭiṉap. 38). 7. aḻaku. "varivaḷai" (pu.ve. 11 : 12). 8. vaṭivu (aka.ni.). 9. icaivakai. 10. icaippāṭṭu. "varinavil koḷkai" (cilap. 13 : 38). 11. varikkūttu. "kaṇkūṭu variyum" (cilap. 3 : 14).
12. kaṭaikkaḻaka nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟu (iṟai. 1. pa. 5). 13. kaṭṭu. "variccilaiyāṟ ṟanta vaḷam" (pu.ve. 1 : 16). 14. kuṭiyiṟai (piṅ.).  15. nel. "eṭuttuvari muṟṟat tiṉiliṭṭu" (taṉippā I, 354 : 41).   16. nīḷam (cūṭā.).

"māmi varaviṭṭa varicai yeṉṟu" (viṟaliviṭu. 533).
te. varuca.
vari - variccu = 1. kōṭupōl nīṇṭa kaṭṭu variccal. "variccumēl viriccu mūṭṭiyē" (kamparā. cittira. 46).
variccu - variccal.
vari + aṇam = varaṇam. ō.nō : maṭi - mari + aṇam = maraṇam.
varaṇam = 1. cūḻkai (yāḻ. aka. ). 2. cuṟṟumatil (ilak. aka.).  3. maṟaikkai (ilak. aka.). 4. caṭṭai (nāmatīpa. 454). 5. eḻuttu. 6. niṟam. 7. vakai. 8. cūlam. 9. ōcaivakai (vaṇṇam). 10. muraṟpāṭṭu (tāṉa varṇam). 11. pāṭṭicai (varṇameṭṭu).
varaṇamālai = vaṇṇamālai (neṭuṅkaṇakku).
varaṇam-va, varṇa, varaṇa.
varaṇa allatu varṇa eṉṉum vaṭacollē tamiḻil vaṇṇam eṉṟu tirintu vaḻaṅkuvatākap pērāciriyar uṭpaṭap palaruṅ karutik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. vaṇṇam, varaṇam eṉṉum irucollum oru ciṟitu vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭaṉa vēṉum, orē vēriṉiṉṟu muṟaiyē kiḻppaṭaiyiṉu mēṟpaṭaiyilumākat tōṉṟiṉaveṉ ṟaṟital vēṇṭum.
o.nō : tiṇṇai, tiraṇai.
tillumullu = tiṇṭumuṇṭu.
til - tel - teḷ - teṟu - teṟṟu - teṟṟi = tiṇṇai.
til - tiḷ - tiṇ - tiṇṇai = tiraṇṭamēṭai.
tiḷ - tiṇṭu - tiḷ - tiṭṭu - tiṭṭai.
til - tir - tiraḷ - tiraḷai - tiraṇai = tiṇṇai. tiṇṇai. tiraṇai eṉṉum irucollum, kīḻppaṭaiyilum mēṟpaṭai yilumāka orē vēriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi orē poruḷai yuṇarttu tal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē vaṇṇam, varaṇam eṉṉum iru collu meṉak kaṇṭu teḷika. `tiṇṇai' `tiraṇai' yiṉ tiripaṉṟu: aṅṅaṉamē. `vaṇṇam' `varaṇat' tiṉ tiripaṉṟu. `vaṇṇakavot tāḻicai' varaṇakavot tāḻicaiyeṉṟu vaḻaṅkāmaiyum nōkkuka.
varaṇam - varaṇi. varaṇittal = palaniṟaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu ōr ōviyattaic ciṟappit tāṟ pōṉṟu, pala coṟkaḷaiyum aṇikaḷaiyuṅ koṇṭu oru poruḷaip puṉain turaittal.
varaṇi - va. varṇ.
varaṇi eṉpatai varṇi yeṉṟō varuṇi yeṉṟō tamiḻil eḻutuvatu tavaṟākum.
varaṇi - varaṇaṉai - va. varṇaṉā.
vaṇṇam, vaṇṇi, vaṇṇaṉai, vaṇṇakam, vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukavillai.
maṟaittaṟ karuttum eḻututaṟ karuttum varaṇittaṟ karuttum vaḷaitaṟ karuttoṭu toṭarpuḷḷavaiyē. ātalāl, cūḻtalum maṟaittalum paṟṟiya collai vēṟākavum, niṟamum vaṇṇaṉai yum paṟṟiya collai vēṟākavum tamiḻiṟ pirittal kūṭātu. eḻututal eṉpatu vaṇṇalōviyam varaitalaiyum kuṟikkum. vaṇṇa allatu niṟakkaruttiṉiṉṟē kulam, icai, vaṇṇaṉai mutaliya karuttukaḷ kiḷaittuḷḷaṉa. eḻuttum vaṇṇappaṭa mum perumpālum vaḷaittē eḻutavum, varaiyavum paṭuvatai iṉṟuṅ kāṇka.
vari (varai)-varantu-varantai = ellai, ōram.

"varaikilēṉ pulaṉka ḷaintum" (tēvā. 631:1). 7. vilakkutal. "uruvukoḷal varaiyār" (tol. eḻuttu. 41). 8. kaiviṭutal. "koḷkalam varaitaliṉ" (kalit. 133). 9. maṟuttal varicaiyaṟital. "koṭaimaṭa meṉpa tamma varaiyātu koṭutta lāmē"  (cūṭā.). 10. nērmaivaḻiyil īṭṭutal. "varaiporuḷ vēṭkaiyēṉ" (cilap. 10:51). 11. taṉakkuriyatākkutal. "aṟaṉvarai yāṉ" (kuṟaḷ. 150). 12. maṇañceytal.
